package com.teikinui.game.app;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input;
import com.teikinui.game.model.PlayerInput;

/** Lit les touches utilisÃ©es pour contrÃ´ler le joueur. */
public final class InputHandler implements PlayerInput {
    public boolean isLeft() {
        return Gdx.input.isKeyPressed(Input.Keys.LEFT);
    }

    public boolean isRight() {
        return Gdx.input.isKeyPressed(Input.Keys.RIGHT);
    }

    public boolean isJump() {
        // FlÃ¨che du haut OU barre d'espace : les deux font sauter.
        return Gdx.input.isKeyPressed(Input.Keys.UP)
                || Gdx.input.isKeyPressed(Input.Keys.SPACE);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\level\LevelLoader.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Tile;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;

/** Convertit une carte texte en grille de tuiles. */
public final class LevelLoader {
    private LevelLoader() {
    }

    public static Level fromString(String text) {
        if (text == null || text.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Le texte du niveau ne peut pas Ãªtre vide.");
        }

        // \R reconnaÃ®t LF et CRLF; strip retire les lignes vides aux extrÃ©mitÃ©s.
        String[] lines = text.strip().split("\\R");
        int rows = lines.length;
        int cols = lines[0].length();
        if (cols == 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Les lignes du niveau ne peuvent pas Ãªtre vides.");
        }

        Tile[][] grid = new Tile[rows][cols];
        char[][] traps = new char[rows][cols];
        for (int rowFromTop = 0; rowFromTop < rows; rowFromTop++) {
            if (lines[rowFromTop].length() != cols) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Toutes les lignes du niveau doivent avoir la mÃªme largeur.");
            }
            int rowFromBottom = rows - 1 - rowFromTop;
            for (int col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
                char symbol = lines[rowFromTop].charAt(col);
                grid[rowFromBottom][col] = Tile.fromChar(symbol);
                if (TrapFactory.isTrap(symbol)) traps[rowFromBottom][col] = symbol;
            }
        }

        Level level = new Level(grid);
        for (int row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
                if (traps[row][col] != 0) level.setTrapLetter(col, row, traps[row][col]);
            }
        }
        return level;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\level\LevelManager.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameListener;

/**
 * ConnaÃ®t l'ordre des pays et le pays en cours. Quand l'objectif est atteint,
 * il passe au pays suivant ; aprÃ¨s le dernier, le voyage est terminÃ©.
 */
public final class LevelManager implements GameListener {
    private LevelTheme current;
    private boolean finished;

    /** Le voyage complet, Ã  partir du premier pays (Tahiti). */
    public LevelManager() {
        this(LevelTheme.values()[0]);
    }

    /** Le voyage Ã  partir du pays choisi dans le menu. */
    public LevelManager(LevelTheme start) {
        current = start;
    }

    @Override
    public void onEvent(GameEvent event) {
        if (event != GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED || finished) {
            return;
        }
        LevelTheme next = current.next();
        if (next == null) {
            finished = true;
        } else {
            current = next;
        }
    }

    public LevelTheme getCurrent() {
        return current;
    }

    /** Vrai quand l'objectif du dernier pays a Ã©tÃ© attrapÃ©. */
    public boolean isFinished() {
        return finished;
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import com.teikinui.game.model.ContactBounds;

/**
 * Les pays du voyage, dans l'ordre. Chaque ligne donne le fichier du niveau,
 * le nom affichÃ©, l'objet Ã  attraper et les couleurs (rouge, vert, bleu entre 0 et 1).
 * Ajouter un pays = ajouter une ligne ici + un fichier .txt.
 */
public enum LevelTheme {
    TAHITI("tahiti.txt", "Tahiti", "le palmier",
            new float[] {0.10f, 0.12f, 0.16f},
            new float[] {0.55f, 0.40f, 0.25f},
            new float[] {0.20f, 0.70f, 0.30f}),
    CHINE("chine.txt", "Chine", "la porte du temple",
            new float[] {0.12f, 0.08f, 0.08f},
            new float[] {0.62f, 0.16f, 0.12f},
            new float[] {0.95f, 0.90f, 0.80f}),
    TUNISIE("tunisie.txt", "Tunisie", "le piment rouge",
            new float[] {0.07f, 0.13f, 0.28f},
            new float[] {0.82f, 0.64f, 0.36f},
            new float[] {0.85f, 0.10f, 0.10f}),
    VIETNAM("vietnam.txt", "Vietnam", "le chapeau conique",
            new float[] {0.05f, 0.12f, 0.55f},
            new float[] {0.30f, 0.55f, 0.28f},
            new float[] {0.93f, 0.82f, 0.50f},
            true),
    SRI_LANKA("srilanka.txt", "Sri Lanka", "le bÃ¢ton de cannelle",
            new float[] {0.08f, 0.22f, 0.14f},
            new float[] {0.72f, 0.40f, 0.22f},
            new float[] {0.55f, 0.27f, 0.07f}),
    TURQUIE("turquie.txt", "Turquie", "la lampe turque",
            new float[] {0.04f, 0.28f, 0.30f},
            new float[] {0.80f, 0.55f, 0.45f},
            new float[] {0.95f, 0.75f, 0.20f}),
    FRANCE("france.txt", "France", "la tour Eiffel",
            new float[] {0.08f, 0.10f, 0.25f},
            new float[] {0.80f, 0.76f, 0.66f},
            new float[] {0.80f, 0.62f, 0.30f}),
    LAPONIE("laponie.txt", "Laponie", "le traÃ®neau du PÃ¨re NoÃ«l",
            new float[] {0.04f, 0.07f, 0.16f},
            new float[] {0.78f, 0.90f, 0.97f},
            new float[] {0.85f, 0.15f, 0.15f});

    private final String fileName;
    private final String displayName;
    private final String goalName;
    private final float[] backgroundColor;
    private final float[] wallColor;
    private final float[] goalColor;
    private final boolean invertedControls;

    LevelTheme(String fileName, String displayName, String goalName,
               float[] backgroundColor, float[] wallColor, float[] goalColor) {
        this(fileName, displayName, goalName, backgroundColor, wallColor, goalColor, false);
    }

    /** Le dernier paramÃ¨tre Ã  true Ã©change les flÃ¨ches gauche et droite dans ce pays. */
    LevelTheme(String fileName, String displayName, String goalName,
               float[] backgroundColor, float[] wallColor, float[] goalColor,
               boolean invertedControls) {
        this.invertedControls = invertedControls;
        this.fileName = fileName;
        this.displayName = displayName;
        this.goalName = goalName;
        this.backgroundColor = backgroundColor;
        this.wallColor = wallColor;
        this.goalColor = goalColor;
    }

    /** Le pays suivant, ou null si c'est le dernier. */
    public LevelTheme next() {
        LevelTheme[] all = values();
        int nextIndex = ordinal() + 1;
        return nextIndex < all.length ? all[nextIndex] : null;
    }

    public String getFileName() {
        return fileName;
    }

    public String getDisplayName() {
        return displayName;
    }

    public String getGoalName() {
        return goalName;
    }

    /** Opaque PNG bounds for the country goal, in the goal's 32Ã—32 tile. */
    public ContactBounds getGoalContactBounds() {
        return switch (this) {
            case TAHITI -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 8, 3, 24, 29);
            case CHINE -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 6, 5, 26, 28);
            case TUNISIE -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 11, 4, 21, 28);
            case VIETNAM -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 5, 7, 27, 26);
            case SRI_LANKA -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 2, 2, 29, 31);            
            case TURQUIE -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 8, 6, 25, 26);
            case FRANCE -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 6, 9, 26, 22);
            case LAPONIE -> ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(32, 32, 4, 9, 28, 25);
        };
    }

    public float[] getBackgroundColor() {
        return backgroundColor.clone();
    }

    public float[] getWallColor() {
        return wallColor.clone();
    }

    public float[] getGoalColor() {
        return goalColor.clone();
    }

    public boolean hasInvertedControls() {
        return invertedControls;
    }

    /** DifficultÃ© affichÃ©e dans le menu : de 1 Ã  3 bombes. */
    public int getDifficulty() {
        return switch (this) {
            case TURQUIE, FRANCE -> 1;
            case TAHITI, VIETNAM, SRI_LANKA -> 2;
            case CHINE, TUNISIE, LAPONIE -> 3;
        };
    }

    /** En Laponie, tout le sol est gelÃ© : le joueur glisse. */
    public boolean isIcy() {
        return this == LAPONIE;
    }

    /** En Chine, la vraie sortie est cachÃ©e : elle est dessinÃ©e comme un trou Ã  pics. */
    public boolean hidesGoal() {
        return this == CHINE;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\level\TahitiTrapPlacement.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Tile;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.random.RandomGenerator;

/** Ajoute trois piÃ¨ges de Tahiti sur des cases libres au-dessus d'un sol. */
public final class TahitiTrapPlacement {
    private static final char[] TYPES = {'c', 's', 'q'};
    /** Une Ã©toile tire Ã  4 cases : rien d'alÃ©atoire dans les 5 colonnes aprÃ¨s P, sur sa rangÃ©e. */
    static final int SAFE_COLUMNS_AFTER_START = 5;

    private TahitiTrapPlacement() { }

    /** Plan immuable tirÃ© une seule fois pour la partie, rÃ©utilisÃ© Ã  chaque essai. */
    public record Placement(char type, int col, int row) { }

    public static List<Trap> addThree(Level level, Player player, List<Trap> existing, RandomGenerator random) {
        return instantiate(level, player, existing, plan(level, existing, random));
    }

    public static List<Placement> plan(Level level, List<Trap> existing, RandomGenerator random) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(random);
        List<int[]> candidates = new ArrayList<>();
        int spawnCol = (int) (level.getStartX() / Level.TILE);
        int spawnRow = (int) (level.getStartY() / Level.TILE);
        int goalCol = (int) (level.createGoal().getX() / Level.TILE);
        int goalRow = (int) (level.createGoal().getY() / Level.TILE);
        for (int row = 1; row < level.getRows() - 1; row++) {
            for (int col = 1; col < level.getCols() - 1; col++) {
                if (level.getTile(col, row) != Tile.EMPTY || level.getTrapLetter(col, row) != 0
                        || !level.isWall(col, row - 1) || col == spawnCol && row == spawnRow
                        || col == goalCol && row == goalRow || col == goalCol && row == spawnRow
                        || row == goalRow && col >= goalCol - 3 && col < goalCol
                        || isInStartSafeZone(col, row, spawnCol, spawnRow)
                        || returnPosition(col, row) || occupied(existing, col, row)) continue;
                candidates.add(new int[] {col, row});
            }
        }
        if (candidates.size() < 3) throw new IllegalStateException("Pas assez de cases libres Ã  Tahiti");
        List<Placement> plan = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            int[] cell = candidates.remove(random.nextInt(candidates.size()));
            plan.add(new Placement(TYPES[random.nextInt(TYPES.length)], cell[0], cell[1]));
        }
        return List.copyOf(plan);
    }

    public static List<Trap> instantiate(Level level, Player player, List<Trap> existing, List<Placement> plan) {
        List<Trap> result = new ArrayList<>(existing);
        for (Placement placement : plan) {
            Trap trap = TrapFactory.create(placement.type(), placement.col() * Level.TILE,
                    placement.row() * Level.TILE, player);
            trap.setStart(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY());
            trap.setLevel(level);
            result.add(trap);
        }
        return result;
    }

    /** Emplacements vÃ©rifiÃ©s dans tahiti.txt : deux chutes au-dessus du retour bas et un tir vers P. */
    public static List<Trap> returnWave(Level level, Player player) {
        List<Placement> positions = List.of(new Placement('c', 23, 4),
                new Placement('c', 12, 4), new Placement('s', 7, 1));
        for (Placement p : positions) {
            if (level.isWall(p.col(), p.row()) || level.getTrapLetter(p.col(), p.row()) != 0
                    || p.col() * Level.TILE == level.getStartX() && p.row() * Level.TILE == level.getStartY()
                    || p.col() * Level.TILE == level.createGoal().getX()
                        && p.row() * Level.TILE == level.createGoal().getY()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Emplacement de vague Tahiti occupÃ©");
            }
        }
        return instantiate(level, player, List.of(), positions);
    }

    /** Zone sans piÃ¨ge alÃ©atoire autour du dÃ©part, pour ne jamais mourir dÃ¨s l'apparition. */
    static boolean isInStartSafeZone(int col, int row, int spawnCol, int spawnRow) {
        return row == spawnRow && Math.abs(col - spawnCol) <= SAFE_COLUMNS_AFTER_START;
    }

    private static boolean returnPosition(int col, int row) {
        return col == 23 && row == 4 || col == 12 && row == 4 || col == 7 && row == 1;
    }

    private static boolean occupied(List<Trap> traps, int col, int row) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if ((int)(trap.getX() / Level.TILE) == col && (int)(trap.getY() / Level.TILE) == row) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\factory\TrapFactory.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory;

import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.TrapType;

/** Convertit une lettre de carte en piÃ¨ge de Tahiti. */
public final class TrapFactory {
    private TrapFactory() {
    }

    public static boolean isTrap(char letter) {
        for (TrapType type : TrapType.values()) {
            if (type.getLetter() == letter) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    public static Trap create(char letter, float x, float y, Player target) {
        for (TrapType type : TrapType.values()) {
            if (type.getLetter() == letter) return type.getFamily().create(type, x, y, target);
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("PiÃ¨ge inconnu : " + letter);
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\falling\CocoFall.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/** Noix de coco tombante : reste sur l'Ã©tage qui la porte. */
public final class CocoFall extends FallingTrap {
    private final float initialY;

    public CocoFall(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, target);
        initialY = y;
    }

    /** Premier sol sous la position initiale, sans traverser les plateformes. */
    private float floorY() {
        if (level == null) return Float.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
        int col = (int) (x / GameConfig.TILE);
        for (int row = (int) (initialY / GameConfig.TILE) - 1; row >= 0; row--) {
            if (level.isWall(col, row)) return (row + 1) * GameConfig.TILE;
        }
        return Float.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
    }

    @Override protected boolean isTriggered() {
        if (target.getY() < floorY() - 1f) return false;
        return super.isTriggered();
    }

    @Override protected void activate(float dt) {
        super.activate(dt);
        y = Math.max(y, floorY());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\falling\FallingTrap.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling;

import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/** Objet suspendu qui tombe lorsque le joueur est proche sous lui. */
public class FallingTrap extends Trap {
    public FallingTrap(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
        setTrigger((trap, player) -> Math.abs(player.getX() - trap.getX()) < GameConfig.TILE * 2
                && player.getY() < trap.getY());
        setVisible(false);
    }

    @Override protected boolean isTriggered() {
        if (!triggeredByPlayer()) return false;
        setVisible(true);
        return true;
    }

    @Override protected void activate(float dt) {
        y -= GameConfig.TILE * 4f * dt;
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground;

import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/** Leur-re au sol qui disparaÃ®t au contact sans blesser. */
public final class FakeFloorTrap extends Trap {
    public FakeFloorTrap(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
    }

    @Override protected boolean isTriggered() { return collidesWith(target); }
    @Override protected void activate(float dt) { }

    @Override public boolean isActive() { return false; }

    @Override public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
        if (state != State.DONE && collidesWith(player)) state = State.DONE;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\ground\QuickSand.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground;

import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/** Sable mouvant : ralentit pendant le contact, sans tuer. */
public final class QuickSand extends Trap {
    public QuickSand(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
    }

    private boolean touchesSand(Player player) {
        // Dans la carte Tahiti q est une tuile solide : les pieds reposent sur son bord supÃ©rieur.
        boolean standingOnTop = Math.abs(player.getY() - (y + height)) < 1f
                && player.getHitbox().getX() < x + width
                && player.getHitbox().getX() + player.getHitbox().getWidth() > x;
        return collidesWith(player) || standingOnTop;
    }

    @Override protected boolean isTriggered() { return touchesSand(target); }
    @Override protected void activate(float dt) { }

    @Override public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
        if (touchesSand(player)) player.slowNextFrame();
        else state = State.WAITING;
    }

    @Override public boolean isActive() { return false; }

    @Override public boolean isMarked() { return true; }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground;

import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/** Porte qui ramÃ¨ne au dÃ©part du niveau. */
public final class ReturnDoor extends Trap {
    public ReturnDoor(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
    }

    @Override protected boolean isTriggered() { return collidesWith(target); }
    @Override protected void activate(float dt) { }

    @Override public boolean isActive() { return false; }

    @Override public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
        if (state != State.DONE && collidesWith(player)) {
            player.teleport(startX, startY);
            state = State.DONE;
        }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\projectile\ProjectileTrap.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps.projectile;

import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/** Projectile horizontal lancÃ© vers le joueur proche. */
public class ProjectileTrap extends Trap {
    private float direction;

    public ProjectileTrap(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
        setTrigger((trap, player) -> Math.abs(player.getX() - trap.getX()) <= GameConfig.TILE * 4
                && Math.abs(player.getY() - trap.getY()) < GameConfig.TILE);
        setVisible(false);
    }

    @Override protected boolean isTriggered() {
        if (!triggeredByPlayer()) return false;
        direction = target.getX() >= x ? 1f : -1f;
        setVisible(true);
        return true;
    }

    @Override protected void activate(float dt) {
        x += direction * GameConfig.TILE * 4f * dt;
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Cactus cachÃ© dans le sable (Tunisie) : invisible, il jaillit quand le joueur

    }

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Mur qui fonce (Chine) : un mur de toute la hauteur part de la droite dÃ¨s que le
 * joueur s'avance, traverse l'Ã©cran et s'arrÃªte juste avant le bord gauche.
 * Seule la toute premiÃ¨re colonne est sÃ»re : il faut y Ãªtre, sur la dalle invisible.
 * Ensuite il repart Ã  droite et disparaÃ®t.
 */
public final class ChargingWall extends Trap {
    private static final float WAKE_DISTANCE = 22f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float SPEED = 8f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float BACK_SPEED = 6f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float STOP_X = 2f * GameConfig.TILE + 2f;
    private static final float PAUSE = 1f;

    private final float homeX;
    private boolean goingBack;
    private float paused;

    public ChargingWall(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, (GameConfig.LEVEL_ROWS - 2) * GameConfig.TILE, target);
        homeX = x;
        setVisible(false);
        setMarked(false);
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        return target.getX() > homeX - WAKE_DISTANCE;
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        setVisible(true);
        if (!goingBack) {
            x = Math.max(STOP_X, x - SPEED * dt);
            if (x <= STOP_X) {
                paused += dt;
                goingBack = paused >= PAUSE;
            }
            return;
        }
        x += BACK_SPEED * dt;
        if (x >= homeX) {
            state = State.DONE;
        }
    }
}

/**

                WIDTH, HEIGHT, target);

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Bloc piÃ©gÃ© (Vietnam) : il ressemble Ã  un mur normal, mais peu aprÃ¨s que le
 * joueur a posÃ© les pieds dessus, il tombe dans les pics. Il faut repartir vite !
 */
public final class CrumbleBlock extends FallingBlock {
    private static final float FALL_SPEED = 10f;
    private static final float DELAY = 0.35f;

    private float steppedFor;

    public CrumbleBlock(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, target, FALL_SPEED);
    }

    @Override
    public void setLevel(Level level) {
        super.setLevel(level);
        level.setWall(col, row, true);
    }

    private boolean playerStandsOnIt() {
        boolean onTop = Math.abs(target.getY() - (y + height)) < 1f;
        boolean above = target.getX() + target.getWidth() > x && target.getX() < x + width;
        return target.isOnGround() && onTop && above;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean shouldFall() {
        if (steppedFor > 0f || playerStandsOnIt()) {
            steppedFor += 1f / 60f;
        }
        return steppedFor >= DELAY;
    }

    /** Il disparaÃ®t dans les pics au lieu de devenir une marche. */
    @Override
    protected boolean staysAsWall() {
        return false;
    }

    /** Ce bloc ne tue pas lui-mÃªme : ce sont les pics en dessous qui tuent. */
    @Override
    public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\Dervish.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Derviche tourneur (Turquie) : il tourne sur lui-mÃªme et fait des allers-retours
 * sur le sol (3 cases de chaque cÃ´tÃ©). Le toucher tue : il faut le sauter au bon moment.
 */
public final class Dervish extends Trap {
    private static final float WIDTH = 24f;
    private static final float HEIGHT = 30f;
    private static final float SPEED = 2.5f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float RANGE = 3f * GameConfig.TILE;

    private final float leftLimit;
    private final float rightLimit;
    private float direction = 1f;
    private float spin;

    public Dervish(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x + (GameConfig.TILE - WIDTH) / 2f, y, WIDTH, HEIGHT, target);
        leftLimit = this.x - RANGE;
        rightLimit = this.x + RANGE;
        setMarked(false);
    }

    /** Angle de rotation, seulement pour le dessin. */
    public float getSpin() {
        return spin;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        spin += 720f * dt;
        x += direction * SPEED * dt;
        if (x <= leftLimit) {
            x = leftLimit;
            direction = 1f;
        } else if (x >= rightLimit) {
            x = rightLimit;
            direction = -1f;
        } else if (wallAhead()) {
            direction = -direction;
        }
    }

    /** Un mur juste devant ses pieds : il fait demi-tour. */
    private boolean wallAhead() {
        float front = direction > 0 ? x + width : x;
        int col = (int) Math.floor(front / GameConfig.TILE);
        int row = (int) Math.floor((y + 1f) / GameConfig.TILE);
        return level.isWall(col, row);
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Le bloc du puits de dÃ©part (Tunisie) : invisible tant que le joueur tombe.
 * Juste aprÃ¨s son atterrissage en bas du puits, le bloc tombe derriÃ¨re lui :
 * s'il ne part pas tout de suite, il est Ã©crasÃ©.
 */
public final class DoorSlam extends FallingBlock {
    private static final float FALL_SPEED = 20f;
    private static final float DELAY = 0.1f;

    private float landedFor;

    public DoorSlam(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, target, FALL_SPEED);
    }

    private boolean playerLandedBelow() {
        boolean below = target.getY() < y;
        boolean near = target.getX() + target.getWidth() > x - GameConfig.TILE
                && target.getX() < x + width + GameConfig.TILE;
        return below && near && target.isOnGround();
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean shouldFall() {
        if (landedFor > 0f || playerLandedBelow()) {
            landedFor += 1f / 60f;
        }
        return landedFor >= DELAY;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\Dune.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Dune (Sri Lanka) : du sable qui a l'air d'un sol normal. DÃ¨s qu'on marche
 * dessus, il s'effondre, et toute la dune s'Ã©croule de proche en proche.
 */
public final class Dune extends FallingBlock {
    private static final char LETTER = 'w';
    private static final float FALL_SPEED = 6f;
    private static final float DELAY = 0.12f;

    private float collapsingFor;

    public Dune(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, target, FALL_SPEED);
    }

    @Override
    public void setLevel(Level level) {
        super.setLevel(level);
        level.setWall(col, row, true);
    }

    private boolean playerStandsOnIt() {
        boolean onTop = Math.abs(target.getY() - (y + height)) < 1f;
        boolean above = target.getX() + target.getWidth() > x && target.getX() < x + width;
        return target.isOnGround() && onTop && above;
    }

    /** Une dune voisine a dÃ©jÃ  commencÃ© Ã  tomber : son mur a disparu. */
    private boolean neighbourCollapsed(int side) {
        return level.getTrapLetter(col + side, row) == LETTER && !level.isWall(col + side, row);
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean shouldFall() {
        if (collapsingFor > 0f || playerStandsOnIt() || neighbourCollapsed(-1) || neighbourCollapsed(1)) {
            collapsingFor += 1f / 60f;
        }
        return collapsingFor >= DELAY;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean staysAsWall() {
        return false;
    }

    /** Le sable ne tue pas : ce sont les pics en dessous qui tuent. */
    @Override
    public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\FallingBlock.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Bloc qui tombe puis devient un mur (patron Template Method) :
 * cette classe gÃ¨re la chute et l'atterrissage, chaque sous-classe
 * dÃ©cide seulement QUAND le bloc se dÃ©croche (shouldFall).
 */
public abstract class FallingBlock extends Trap {
    protected final int col;
    protected final int row;
    private final float fallSpeed;
    private boolean detached;

    protected FallingBlock(float x, float y, Player target, float fallSpeedInTiles) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
        col = Math.round(x / GameConfig.TILE);
        row = Math.round(y / GameConfig.TILE);
        fallSpeed = fallSpeedInTiles * GameConfig.TILE;
        setVisible(false);
    }

    /** Vrai quand le bloc doit se dÃ©crocher. */
    protected abstract boolean shouldFall();

    /** Vrai si le bloc s'est dÃ©jÃ  dÃ©crochÃ© (utile pour les blocs voisins). */
    public boolean isDetached() {
        return detached;
    }

    /** Vrai (par dÃ©faut) si le bloc devient un mur en touchant le sol. */
    protected boolean staysAsWall() {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    protected final boolean isTriggered() {
        return shouldFall();
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        if (!detached) {
            detached = true;
            level.setWall(col, row, false);
            setVisible(true);
        }
        y -= fallSpeed * dt;
        int belowRow = (int) Math.floor((y - 1f) / GameConfig.TILE);
        float floorTop = (belowRow + 1) * GameConfig.TILE;
        if (level.isWall(col, belowRow) && y <= floorTop) {
            y = floorTop;
            if (!staysAsWall()) {
                state = State.DONE;
                return;
            }
            if (collidesWith(target)) {
                return;
            }
            level.setWall(col, belowRow + 1, true);
            state = State.DONE;
        }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\GrowingSpike.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Pilier de pics (Vietnam) : cachÃ©, il jaillit quand le joueur passe Ã  cÃ´tÃ©.
 * Du sol : il monte de 2 cases et reste sorti (il faut sauter par-dessus).
 * Du plafond : c'est un pilon, il descend jusqu'au sol trÃ¨s vite, remonte,
 * attend un peu puis recommence, sans fin. Il faut passer quand il est en haut.
 */
public final class GrowingSpike extends Trap {
    private static final float THICKNESS = 16f;
    private static final float FLOOR_LENGTH = 2f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float MAX_CEILING_LENGTH = 5f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float GROW_SPEED = 12f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float DROP_SPEED = 24f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float RISE_SPEED = 8f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float HOLD_DOWN = 0.25f;
    private static final float PAUSE_UP = 0.9f;
    private static final float REACH = 1.5f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float SIGHT = 5f * GameConfig.TILE;

    private enum Phase { DOWN, HOLD, UP, PAUSE }

    private final float tileX;
    private final float tileY;
    private boolean fromCeiling;
    private float maxLength = FLOOR_LENGTH;
    private float length;
    private Phase phase = Phase.DOWN;
    private float phaseTime;

    public GrowingSpike(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x + (GameConfig.TILE - THICKNESS) / 2f, y, THICKNESS, 0f, target);
        tileX = x;
        tileY = y;
        setVisible(false);
        setMarked(false);
    }

    @Override
    public void setLevel(Level level) {
        super.setLevel(level);
        int col = Math.round(tileX / GameConfig.TILE);
        int row = Math.round(tileY / GameConfig.TILE);
        fromCeiling = level.isWall(col, row + 1);
        if (fromCeiling) {
            y = tileY + GameConfig.TILE;
            int free = 0;
            while (row - free >= 0 && !level.isWall(col, row - free)) {
                free++;
            }
            maxLength = Math.min(MAX_CEILING_LENGTH, free * GameConfig.TILE);
        }
    }

    public boolean isFromCeiling() {
        return fromCeiling;
    }

    /** Le joueur est juste Ã  cÃ´tÃ© ET dans le mÃªme couloir (pas Ã  l'Ã©tage du dessous). */
    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        boolean near = target.getX() + target.getWidth() > tileX - REACH
                && target.getX() < tileX + GameConfig.TILE + REACH;
        float bottom = target.getY();
        float top = bottom + target.getHeight();
        boolean sameRoom = fromCeiling
                ? top > tileY + GameConfig.TILE - SIGHT && bottom < tileY + GameConfig.TILE
                : bottom < tileY + SIGHT && top > tileY;
        return near && sameRoom;
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        setVisible(true);
        if (fromCeiling) {
            pound(dt);
        } else {
            length = Math.min(maxLength, length + GROW_SPEED * dt);
        }
        height = length;
        y = fromCeiling ? tileY + GameConfig.TILE - length : tileY;
    }

    /** Le cycle du pilon : descend, reste en bas, remonte, attend en haut, recommence. */
    private void pound(float dt) {
        phaseTime += dt;
        switch (phase) {
            case DOWN -> {
                length = Math.min(maxLength, length + DROP_SPEED * dt);
                if (length >= maxLength) {
                    next(Phase.HOLD);
                }
            }
            case HOLD -> {
                if (phaseTime >= HOLD_DOWN) {
                    next(Phase.UP);
                }
            }
            case UP -> {
                length = Math.max(0f, length - RISE_SPEED * dt);
                if (length <= 0f) {
                    next(Phase.PAUSE);
                }
            }
            case PAUSE -> {
                if (phaseTime >= PAUSE_UP) {
                    next(Phase.DOWN);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void next(Phase newPhase) {
        phase = newPhase;
        phaseTime = 0f;
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Stalactite de glace (Laponie) : accrochÃ©e au plafond, elle se dÃ©croche d'un coup
 * quand le joueur passe juste en dessous, tombe trÃ¨s vite et se brise au sol.
 */
public final class Icicle extends Trap {
    private static final float WIDTH = 16f;
    private static final float HEIGHT = 28f;
    private static final float REACH = 0.6f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float FALL_SPEED = 18f * GameConfig.TILE;

    public Icicle(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x + (GameConfig.TILE - WIDTH) / 2f, y + GameConfig.TILE - HEIGHT, WIDTH, HEIGHT, target);

    }

    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        boolean below = target.getY() < y;
        boolean near = target.getX() + target.getWidth() > x - REACH && target.getX() < x + width + REACH;
        return below && near;
    }

        int row = (int) Math.floor(y / GameConfig.TILE);

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\InvisibleBlock.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Bloc invisible (Sri Lanka) : on ne le voit pas. Quand le joueur saute
 * et que sa tÃªte arrive dessous, il apparaÃ®t, devient un mur et le joueur
 * se cogne. Ensuite, on peut monter dessus comme sur une marche.
 */
public final class InvisibleBlock extends Trap {
    private final int col;
    private final int row;

    public InvisibleBlock(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
        col = Math.round(x / GameConfig.TILE);
        row = Math.round(y / GameConfig.TILE);
        setVisible(false);
        setMarked(false);
    }

    /** La tÃªte du joueur, qui monte, touche le dessous du bloc. */
    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        boolean below = target.getX() + target.getWidth() > x && target.getX() < x + width;
        float head = target.getY() + target.getHeight();
        return below && target.getVy() > 0f && target.getY() < y && head > y - 2f;
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        if (level.isWall(col, row)) {
            return;
        }
        level.setWall(col, row, true);
        setVisible(true);
        if (target.getY() + target.getHeight() > y) {
            // Bonk : on remet la tÃªte juste sous le bloc et le joueur retombe.
            target.teleport(target.getX(), y - target.getHeight());
        }
    }

    /** Le bloc ne tue pas : il fait seulement tomber le joueur. */
    @Override
    public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\Jar.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Jarre du plafond (Tunisie). Elle tombe d'un coup quand le joueur arrive juste
 * Ã  cÃ´tÃ©, puis devient une marche. Deux jarres cÃ´te Ã  cÃ´te tombent ensemble.
 * AprÃ¨s une mort, elle attend que le joueur soit encore plus prÃ¨s : le piÃ¨ge apprend.
 */
public final class Jar extends FallingBlock {
    private static final float FALL_SPEED = 20f;
    private static final float REACH = 0.5f;
    private static final float REACH_AFTER_DEATH = 0.25f;

    public Jar(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, target, FALL_SPEED);
    }

    /** Au chargement, la jarre bouche sa case du plafond. */
    @Override
    public void setLevel(Level level) {
        super.setLevel(level);
        level.setWall(col, row, true);
    }

    /** Distance de dÃ©clenchement : Â½ case, ou Â¼ de case si le joueur est dÃ©jÃ  mort ici. */
    float triggerDistance() {
        boolean learned = level != null && level.getAttempt() > 0;
        return (learned ? REACH_AFTER_DEATH : REACH) * GameConfig.TILE;
    }

    /** La jarre de gauche est tombÃ©e : on tombe avec elle. */
    private boolean leftJarFell() {
        return level.getTrapLetter(col - 1, row) == TrapType.JAR.getLetter()
                && !level.isWall(col - 1, row);
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean shouldFall() {
        boolean below = target.getY() < y;
        boolean close = target.getX() + target.getWidth() >= x - triggerDistance()
                && target.getX() <= x + width;
        return below && close || leftJarFell();
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\Monkey.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Singe macaque (Sri Lanka) : accrochÃ© sous le plafond, il lance un fruit
 * droit sur le joueur quand celui-ci passe en dessous, puis recommence.
 * Le piÃ¨ge (sa hitbox) est le fruit : c'est lui qui tue.
 */
public final class Monkey extends Trap {
    private static final float FRUIT = 14f;
    private static final float RANGE = 5f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float SPEED = 7f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float COOLDOWN = 1.2f;

    private final float homeX;
    private final float homeY;
    private float vx;
    private float vy;
    private float cooldown;
    private boolean flying;

    public Monkey(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x + (GameConfig.TILE - FRUIT) / 2f, y, FRUIT, FRUIT, target);
        homeX = x;
        homeY = y;
        setMarked(false);
    }

    public float getHomeX() {
        return homeX;
    }

    public float getHomeY() {
        return homeY;
    }

    private boolean playerInRange() {
        float playerCenter = target.getX() + target.getWidth() / 2f;
        float monkeyCenter = homeX + GameConfig.TILE / 2f;
        return Math.abs(playerCenter - monkeyCenter) < RANGE && target.getY() < homeY;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        return playerInRange();
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        if (flying) {
            x += vx * dt;
            y += vy * dt;
            if (hitSomething()) {
                backToHand();
            }
            return;
        }
        cooldown -= dt;
        if (cooldown <= 0f && playerInRange()) {
            throwFruit();
        }
    }

    /** Vise le centre du joueur au moment du lancer. */
    private void throwFruit() {
        float dx = target.getX() + target.getWidth() / 2f - (x + FRUIT / 2f);
        float dy = target.getY() + target.getHeight() / 2f - (y + FRUIT / 2f);
        float length = (float) Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        vx = dx / length * SPEED;
        vy = dy / length * SPEED;
        flying = true;
        cooldown = COOLDOWN;
    }

    private boolean hitSomething() {
        int col = (int) Math.floor((x + FRUIT / 2f) / GameConfig.TILE);
        int row = (int) Math.floor((y + FRUIT / 2f) / GameConfig.TILE);
        return y < -GameConfig.TILE || level.isWall(col, row);
    }

    private void backToHand() {
        flying = false;
        x = homeX + (GameConfig.TILE - FRUIT) / 2f;
        y = homeY;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\PopSpike.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

/**
 * Pic cachÃ© sur un bloc (Vietnam) : invisible, il surgit quand le joueur
 * arrive Ã  cÃ´tÃ©. Il faut alors sauter par-dessus au lieu d'atterrir dessus.
 */
public final class PopSpike extends Trap {
    private static final float SIZE = 24f;
    private static final float REACH = 1.5f * GameConfig.TILE;

    public PopSpike(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x + (GameConfig.TILE - SIZE) / 2f, y, SIZE, SIZE, target);

                && target.getX() < x + width + REACH;

                && target.getY() < y + 4 * GameConfig.TILE;
        return near && sameFloor;
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        setVisible(true);
    }

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Vendeur de simits (Turquie) : il lance ses simits (pains ronds au sÃ©same)
 * au ras du sol, droit vers le joueur, toutes les 1,6 s. Il faut sauter par-dessus.
 * Le piÃ¨ge (sa hitbox) est le simit : c'est lui qui tue.
 */
public final class SimitVendor extends Trap {
    private static final float SIMIT_WIDTH = 16f;
    private static final float SIMIT_HEIGHT = 10f;
    private static final float SPEED = 6f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float RANGE = 14f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float COOLDOWN = 1.6f;

    private final float homeX;
    private final float homeY;
    private float vx;
    private float cooldown = 0.5f;
    private boolean flying;

    public SimitVendor(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x + 8f, y + 6f, SIMIT_WIDTH, SIMIT_HEIGHT, target);
        homeX = x;
        homeY = y;
        setMarked(false);
    }

    public float getHomeX() {
        return homeX;
    }

    public float getHomeY() {
        return homeY;
    }

    /** Le joueur est dans le mÃªme couloir, pas trop loin. */
    private boolean playerInLane() {
        return Math.abs(target.getY() - homeY) < 2f * GameConfig.TILE
                && Math.abs(target.getX() - homeX) < RANGE;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        return playerInLane();
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        if (flying) {
            x += vx * dt;
            if (hitsWall()) {
                backToTray();
            }
            return;
        }
        cooldown -= dt;
        if (cooldown <= 0f && playerInLane()) {
            vx = target.getX() < homeX ? -SPEED : SPEED;
            flying = true;
            cooldown = COOLDOWN;
        }
    }

    private boolean hitsWall() {
        int col = (int) Math.floor((x + SIMIT_WIDTH / 2f) / GameConfig.TILE);
        int row = (int) Math.floor((y + SIMIT_HEIGHT / 2f) / GameConfig.TILE);
        return level.isWall(col, row);
    }

    private void backToTray() {
        flying = false;
        x = homeX + 8f;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\SkyFall.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Ciel qui tombe (Chine) : un dÃ©compte 3, 2, 1 prÃ©vient le joueur, puis le ciel descend sur toute
 * la largeur de l'Ã©cran, jusqu'Ã  toucher la tÃªte du joueur. Tant qu'il est en bas, c'est Â« 1, 2, 3, soleil Â» :
 * si le joueur bouge (court, saute ou tombe), il meurt. Ensuite le ciel remonte.
 */
public final class SkyFall extends Trap {
    private static final float WAIT = 0.5f;
    /** DÃ©compte avant la chute : le joueur a le temps de s'arrÃªter. */
    private static final float WARN = 3f;
    private static final float DOWN = 1f;
    private static final float HOLD = 1.5f;
    private static final float UP = 0.8f;
    private static final float FALL = WAIT + WARN;
    private static final float TOP = GameConfig.LEVEL_ROWS * GameConfig.TILE;
    /** Petit espace entre le ciel et la tÃªte du joueur. */
    private static final float GAP = 2f;

    private float time;
    private float lowest = TOP;
    private boolean aimed;

    public SkyFall(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, target);
        setMarked(false);
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        if (!aimed && time >= FALL) {
            // Au moment oÃ¹ il commence Ã  tomber, le ciel vise la tÃªte du joueur.
            lowest = Math.min(TOP, target.getY() + target.getHeight() + GAP);
            aimed = true;
        }
        time += dt;
        if (time > FALL + DOWN + HOLD + UP) {
            state = State.DONE;
        }
    }

    /** Vrai pendant le dÃ©compte qui annonce la chute : bouger ne tue pas encore. */
    public boolean isWarning() {
        return time >= WAIT && time < FALL;
    }

    /** Vrai pendant que le ciel descend ou reste en bas : il ne faut pas bouger. */
    public boolean isDangerous() {
        return time > FALL && time < FALL + DOWN + HOLD;
    }

    /** Le chiffre Ã  afficher avant la chute : 3, 2, 1 ; 0 le reste du temps. */
    public int getCountdown() {
        return isWarning() ? (int) Math.ceil(FALL - time) : 0;
    }

    /** Hauteur (en pixels) du bord bas du ciel, pour le dessin. */
    public float getSkyBottom() {
        if (time < FALL) {
            return TOP;
        }
        if (time < FALL + DOWN) {
            return TOP - (TOP - lowest) * (time - FALL) / DOWN;
        }
        if (time < FALL + DOWN + HOLD) {
            return lowest;
        }
        float up = Math.min(1f, (time - FALL - DOWN - HOLD) / UP);
        return lowest + (TOP - lowest) * up;
    }

    private boolean playerMoves() {
        return Math.abs(target.getVx()) > 0f || Math.abs(target.getVy()) > 1f;
    }

    @Override
    public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
        if (isDangerous() && playerMoves()) {
            bus.publish(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
            state = State.DONE;
        }
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Boule de neige (Laponie) : posÃ©e au sol, elle se met Ã  rouler vers le joueur
 * dÃ¨s qu'il entre dans le mÃªme couloir (moins de 12 cases), et se brise contre un mur.
 * La toucher tue : il faut la sauter.
 */
public final class Snowball extends Trap {
    private static final float SIZE = 26f;
    private static final float RANGE = 12f * GameConfig.TILE;
    private static final float SPEED = 5f * GameConfig.TILE;

    private float direction;
    private float spin;

    public Snowball(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x + (GameConfig.TILE - SIZE) / 2f, y, SIZE, SIZE, target);
        setMarked(false);
    }

    /** Angle de rotation, seulement pour le dessin. */
    public float getSpin() {
        return spin;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        boolean sameFloor = Math.abs(target.getY() - y) < 1.5f * GameConfig.TILE;
        return sameFloor && Math.abs(target.getX() - x) < RANGE;
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        if (direction == 0f) {
            direction = target.getX() < x ? -1f : 1f;
        }
        x += direction * SPEED * dt;
        spin += direction * -540f * dt;
        float front = direction > 0 ? x + width : x;
        int col = (int) Math.floor(front / GameConfig.TILE);
        int row = (int) Math.floor((y + 2f) / GameConfig.TILE);
        if (level.isWall(col, row)) {
            state = State.DONE;
        }
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\TiltSlab.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/**
 * Dalle qui bascule (Chine) : une dalle normale. Peu aprÃ¨s que le joueur s'y pose,
 * elle se penche (toutes ses cases ensemble) et le joueur glisse dans le trou dessous.
 */
public final class TiltSlab extends Trap {
    private static final char LETTER = 'b';
    private static final float DELAY = 0.3f;
    private static final float TILT_SPEED = 4f;

    private final int col;
    private final int row;
    private int index;
    private float standingFor;
    private float tilt;

    public TiltSlab(float x, float y, Player target) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE, target);
        col = Math.round(x / GameConfig.TILE);
        row = Math.round(y / GameConfig.TILE);
        setMarked(false);
    }

    @Override
    public void setLevel(Level level) {
        super.setLevel(level);
        level.setWall(col, row, true);
        while (level.getTrapLetter(col - index - 1, row) == LETTER) {
            index++;
        }
    }

    /** Place de la case dans la dalle (0 = la plus Ã  gauche), pour dessiner la pente. */
    public int getIndex() {
        return index;
    }

    /** 0 = Ã  plat, 1 = complÃ¨tement penchÃ©e. */
    public float getTilt() {
        return tilt;
    }

    private boolean playerStandsOnIt() {
        boolean onTop = Math.abs(target.getY() - (y + height)) < 1f;
        boolean above = target.getX() + target.getWidth() > x && target.getX() < x + width;
        return target.isOnGround() && onTop && above;
    }

    private boolean neighbourTilted(int side) {
        return level.getTrapLetter(col + side, row) == LETTER && !level.isWall(col + side, row);
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean isTriggered() {
        if (standingFor > 0f || playerStandsOnIt()) {
            standingFor += 1f / 60f;
        }
        return standingFor >= DELAY || neighbourTilted(-1) || neighbourTilted(1);
    }

    @Override
    protected void activate(float dt) {
        level.setWall(col, row, false);
        tilt = Math.min(1f, tilt + TILT_SPEED * dt);
    }

    @Override
    public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\Trap.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.Entity;
import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Interactable;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.triggers.Trigger;

/** Base pour les piÃ¨ges dÃ©clenchÃ©s par une entitÃ© du jeu. */
public abstract class Trap extends Entity implements Interactable {
    public enum State {
        WAITING,
        TRIGGERED,
        DONE
    }

    protected State state = State.WAITING;
    protected final Player target;
    protected float startX;
    protected float startY;
    protected Level level;
    private Trigger trigger;
    private boolean visible = true;
    private boolean marked = true;
    private String spriteName = "";

    protected Trap(float x, float y, float width, float height, Player target) {
        super(x, y, width, height);
        this.target = target;
    }

    public void setStart(float x, float y) {
        startX = x;
        startY = y;
    }

    /** Le niveau oÃ¹ se trouve le piÃ¨ge : il peut y ajouter ou enlever des murs. */
    public void setLevel(Level level) {
        this.level = level;
    }

    public void setTrigger(Trigger trigger) {
        this.trigger = trigger;
    }

    /** Nom logique du PNG, optionnel pour les piÃ¨ges construits sans fabrique. */
    public void setSpriteName(String spriteName) {
        this.spriteName = java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(spriteName);
    }

    public String getSpriteName() {
        return spriteName;
    }

    public boolean isVisible() {
        return visible && state != State.DONE;
    }

    /** Seuls les piÃ¨ges dÃ©jÃ  dÃ©clenchÃ©s peuvent blesser (ou tÃ©lÃ©porter). */
    public boolean isActive() {
        return state == State.TRIGGERED;
    }

    /** Le rendu peut dessiner un indice de la case mÃªme avant dÃ©clenchement. */
    public boolean isMarked() {
        return marked && state != State.DONE;
    }

    protected void setMarked(boolean marked) {
        this.marked = marked;
    }

    protected void setVisible(boolean visible) {
        this.visible = visible;
    }

    protected boolean triggeredByPlayer() {
        return trigger != null && trigger.isTriggered(this, target);
    }

    protected abstract boolean isTriggered();

    protected abstract void activate(float deltaTime);

    @Override
    public final void update(float deltaTime) {
        if (state == State.WAITING && isTriggered()) {
            state = State.TRIGGERED;
        }
        if (state == State.TRIGGERED) {
            activate(deltaTime);
        }
    }

    /** Par dÃ©faut, un piÃ¨ge consommÃ© ne peut pas tuer deux fois. */
    @Override
    public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
        if (isActive() && collidesWith(player)) {
            bus.publish(com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
            state = State.DONE;
        }
    }

    public final State getState() {
        return state;
    }
}

/** Knows how to build one family of traps. */
public interface TrapCreator {
    Trap create(TrapType type, float x, float y, Player target);

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\traps\TrapFamily.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model.traps;

import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling.CloudShark;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling.CocoFall;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground.FakeFloorTrap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground.QuickSand;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground.ReturnDoor;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.projectile.Starfish;

/** The trap families (one line per family, added by lots 2 and 3). */
public enum TrapFamily implements TrapCreator {
    FALLING((type, x, y, target) -> switch (type) {
        case COCONUT -> new CocoFall(x, y, target);
        case CLOUD_SHARK -> new CloudShark(x, y, target);
        case JAR -> new Jar(x, y, target);
        case DOOR_SLAM -> new DoorSlam(x, y, target);
        case CRUMBLE_BLOCK -> new CrumbleBlock(x, y, target);
        case DUNE -> new Dune(x, y, target);
        case ICICLE -> new Icicle(x, y, target);
        default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("PiÃ¨ge incompatible : " + type);
    }),
    PROJECTILE((type, x, y, target) -> switch (type) {
        case STARFISH -> new Starfish(x, y, target);
        case MONKEY -> new Monkey(x, y, target);
        case SIMIT_VENDOR -> new SimitVendor(x, y, target);
        default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("PiÃ¨ge incompatible : " + type);
    }),
    GROUND((type, x, y, target) -> switch (type) {
        case QUICKSAND -> new QuickSand(x, y, target);
        case FAKE_FLOOR -> new FakeFloorTrap(x, y, target);
        case CACTUS -> new Cactus(x, y, target);
        case SPIKE_FLOOR -> new SpikeFloor(x, y, target);
        case POP_SPIKE -> new PopSpike(x, y, target);
        case GROWING_SPIKE -> new GrowingSpike(x, y, target);
        case FAKE_CHILI -> new FakeChili(x, y, target);
        case INVISIBLE_BLOCK -> new InvisibleBlock(x, y, target);
        case DERVISH -> new Dervish(x, y, target);
        case LOUKOUM -> new Loukoum(x, y, target);
        case SKY_FALL -> new SkyFall(x, y, target);
        case CHARGING_WALL -> new ChargingWall(x, y, target);
        case HIDDEN_SLAB -> new HiddenSlab(x, y, target);
        case FAKE_SLAB -> new FakeSlab(x, y, target);
        case TILT_SLAB -> new TiltSlab(x, y, target);
        case SNOWBALL -> new Snowball(x, y, target);
        default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("PiÃ¨ge incompatible : " + type);
    }),
    TELEPORT((type, x, y, target) -> {
        if (type != TrapType.RETURN_DOOR) throw new IllegalArgumentException("PiÃ¨ge incompatible : " + type);
        return new ReturnDoor(x, y, target);
    }),
    /** Objets qui aident le joueur au lieu de le piÃ©ger (France). */
    BONUS((type, x, y, target) -> {
        if (type != TrapType.CROISSANT) throw new IllegalArgumentException("PiÃ¨ge incompatible : " + type);
        return new Croissant(x, y, target);
    });

    private final TrapCreator creator;

    TrapFamily(TrapCreator creator) {
        this.creator = creator;
    }

    @Override
    public Trap create(TrapType type, float x, float y, Player target) {
        Trap trap = creator.create(type, x, y, target);
        trap.setSpriteName(type.getSpriteName());
        trap.setContactBounds(type.getContactBounds());
        return trap;
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** Something that can touch other things. */
public interface Collidable {
    Hitbox getHitbox();

    default boolean collidesWith(Collidable other) {
        return getHitbox().overlaps(other.getHitbox());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\CollisionSystem.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.List;

/** RÃ©sout les dÃ©placements du joueur contre les murs, sans dÃ©pendance LibGDX. */
public final class CollisionSystem {
    // Petite marge pour Ã©viter de compter une case touchÃ©e seulement par son bord.
    private static final float EPSILON = 0.0001f;

    /**
     * DÃ©place le joueur horizontalement, puis verticalement, en corrigeant sa position
     * lorsqu'un mur est rencontrÃ©.
     *
     * @param player joueur Ã  dÃ©placer
     * @param level grille contenant les murs
     * @param dt temps Ã©coulÃ© depuis la derniÃ¨re image, en secondes
     */
    public void move(Player player, Level level, float dt) {
        moveX(player, level, dt);
        moveY(player, level, dt);
    }

        /**
     * PrÃ©vient chaque objet interactif (piÃ¨ges, objectif) que le joueur est lÃ  :
     * chacun vÃ©rifie lui-mÃªme s'il est touchÃ© et rÃ©agit.
     *
     * @param player le joueur
     * @param things les objets Ã  prÃ©venir
     * @param bus le bus oÃ¹ publier les Ã©vÃ©nements (mort, objectif atteint)
     */
    public void checkContacts(Player player, List<? extends Interactable> things, EventBus bus) {
        for (Interactable thing : things) {
            thing.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        }
    }

    /** Si le joueur est tombÃ© sous l'Ã©cran (dans un trou), il meurt. */
    public void checkFall(Player player, EventBus bus) {
        if (player.getY() + player.getHeight() < 0) {
            bus.publish(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
        }
    }

    /** VÃ©rifie toutes les colonnes traversÃ©es par le joueur sur l'axe X. */
    private void moveX(Player player, Level level, float dt) {
        float vx = player.getVx();
        if (vx == 0f) {
            return;
        }

        // Position X prÃ©vue avant correction Ã©ventuelle par un mur.
        float targetX = player.x + vx * dt;
        // RangÃ©es occupÃ©es par la hitbox de dÃ©placement du joueur.
        int firstRow = floorToTile(player.y);
        int lastRow = floorToTile(player.y + player.height - EPSILON);

        if (vx > 0f) {
            // En allant Ã  droite, on vÃ©rifie chaque colonne touchÃ©e par le bord droit.
            int firstCol = floorToTile(player.x + player.width);
            int lastCol = floorToTile(targetX + player.width - EPSILON);
            for (int col = firstCol; col <= lastCol; col++) {
                for (int row = firstRow; row <= lastRow; row++) {
                    if (level.isWall(col, row)) {
                        player.x = col * Level.TILE - player.width;
                        player.stopHorizontalMovement();
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            // En allant Ã  gauche, on vÃ©rifie chaque colonne touchÃ©e par le bord gauche.
            int firstCol = floorToTile(player.x) - 1;
            int lastCol = floorToTile(targetX);
            for (int col = firstCol; col >= lastCol; col--) {
                for (int row = firstRow; row <= lastRow; row++) {

                    if (level.isWall(col, row)) {
                        player.x = (col + 1) * Level.TILE;
                        player.stopHorizontalMovement();
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        player.x = targetX;
    }

    /** RÃ©sout les collisions verticales : sol en descente, plafond en montÃ©e. */
    private void moveY(Player player, Level level, float dt) {
        float vy = player.getVy();
        // Position Y prÃ©vue avant la dÃ©tection du sol ou du plafond.
        float targetY = player.y + vy * dt;

        // Le joueur peut chevaucher plusieurs colonnes pendant sa chute ou son saut.
        int firstCol = floorToTile(player.x);
        int lastCol = floorToTile(player.x + player.width - EPSILON);

        if (vy < 0f) {
            // En descendant, parcourir les rangÃ©es du joueur vers sa position prÃ©vue.
            int firstRow = floorToTile(player.y - EPSILON);
            int lastRow = floorToTile(targetY - EPSILON);

            for (int row = firstRow; row >= lastRow; row--) {
                for (int col = firstCol; col <= lastCol; col++) {
                    if (level.isWall(col, row)) {
                        // Le joueur repose juste au-dessus du mur; il a atterri.
                        player.y = (row + 1) * Level.TILE;
                        player.land();
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
            // Aucune case murale : la chute continue et le joueur quitte le sol.
            player.y = targetY;
            player.leaveGround();
        } else if (vy > 0f) {
            // En montant, parcourir les rangÃ©es atteintes par le haut de la hitbox.
            int firstRow = floorToTile(player.y + player.height);
            int lastRow = floorToTile(targetY + player.height - EPSILON);

            for (int row = firstRow; row <= lastRow; row++) {
                for (int col = firstCol; col <= lastCol; col++) {
                    if (level.isWall(col, row)) {
                        // Le joueur est replacÃ© sous le mur; son saut s'arrÃªte.
                        player.y = row * Level.TILE - player.height;
                        player.stopVerticalMovement();
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
            player.y = targetY;
        }
    }

    /** Convertit une position en pixels vers l'index de case correspondant. */
    private int floorToTile(float position) {
        // floor (et pas une simple conversion int) gÃ¨re aussi les coordonnÃ©es nÃ©gatives.
        return (int) Math.floor(position / Level.TILE);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\ContactBounds.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** Contact rectangle normalized to an entity's movement/draw rectangle. */
public record ContactBounds(float left, float bottom, float right, float top) {
    public static final ContactBounds FULL = new ContactBounds(0f, 0f, 1f, 1f);

    public ContactBounds {
        if (!Float.isFinite(left) || !Float.isFinite(bottom)
                || !Float.isFinite(right) || !Float.isFinite(top)
                || right <= left || top <= bottom) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Contact bounds must be finite and non-empty");
        }
    }

    /**
     * Converts an opaque PNG pixel rectangle (right and bottom exclusive) to game-space fractions.
     * PNG coordinates start at the top; game coordinates start at the bottom.
     */
    public static ContactBounds fromAlphaBounds(
            int imageWidth, int imageHeight, int left, int top, int right, int bottom) {
        if (imageWidth <= 0 || imageHeight <= 0 || left < 0 || top < 0
                || right > imageWidth || bottom > imageHeight
                || right <= left || bottom <= top) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid image alpha bounds");
        }
        return new ContactBounds(
                left / (float) imageWidth,
                (imageHeight - bottom) / (float) imageHeight,
                right / (float) imageWidth,
                (imageHeight - top) / (float) imageHeight);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\Entity.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** Base commune des objets du monde, indÃ©pendante de libGDX et de JavaFX. */
public abstract class Entity implements Updatable, Collidable {
    protected float x;
    protected float y;
    protected float width;
    protected float height;
    private ContactBounds contactBounds = ContactBounds.FULL;

    protected Entity(float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
    }

    /** Hitbox calculÃ©e Ã  partir de la position actuelle, y compris aprÃ¨s les collisions. */
    @Override
    public Hitbox getHitbox() {
        return new Hitbox(
                x + contactBounds.left() * width,
                y + contactBounds.bottom() * height,
                (contactBounds.right() - contactBounds.left()) * width,
                (contactBounds.top() - contactBounds.bottom()) * height);
    }

    /** Sets the visible/contact rectangle as fractions of the entity's movement rectangle. */
    public void setContactBounds(ContactBounds contactBounds) {
        this.contactBounds = java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(contactBounds);
    }

    /** Mise Ã  jour de l'entitÃ© pour le temps Ã©coulÃ© depuis la frame prÃ©cÃ©dente. */
    public abstract void update(float deltaTime);

    /** Retourne true lorsque les rectangles des deux entitÃ©s se chevauchent. */
    public boolean intersects(Entity other) {
        return getHitbox().overlaps(other.getHitbox());
    }

    public float getX() {
        return x;
    }

    public float getY() {
        return y;
    }

    public float getWidth() {
        return width;
    }

    public float getHeight() {
        return height;
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/** Envoie les Ã©vÃ©nements aux abonnÃ©s dans leur ordre d'inscription. */
public class EventBus {
    private final List<GameListener> listeners = new ArrayList<>();

    public void subscribe(GameListener listener) {
        listeners.add(listener);
    }

    public void publish(GameEvent event) {
        for (GameListener listener : List.copyOf(listeners)) {
            listener.onEvent(event);
        }
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** ChronomÃ¨tre du jeu, en secondes, indÃ©pendant du rendu. */
public final class GameTimer implements Updatable {
    private float remaining = GameConfig.GAME_DURATION;

    @Override
    public void update(float dt) {
        if (!Float.isFinite(dt) || dt < 0f) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Le temps Ã©coulÃ© doit Ãªtre fini et positif.");
        }
        remaining = Math.max(0f, remaining - dt);
    }

    public float getRemaining() {
        return remaining;
    }

    /** Remet le chrono Ã  3 minutes (nouveau pays). */
    public void reset() {
        remaining = GameConfig.GAME_DURATION;
    }

    public boolean isTimeUp() {
        return remaining <= 0f;
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** L'objet Ã  attraper Ã  la fin d'un pays (palmier, bol de ramen, piment). */
public final class Goal extends Entity implements Interactable {
    private boolean reached;
    private boolean movedToStart;

    public Goal(float x, float y) {
        super(x, y, GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE);
    }

    /** DÃ©place une seule fois l'objectif au dÃ©part sans dÃ©placer le joueur. */
    public boolean moveToStart(float startX, float startY) {
        if (reached || movedToStart) return false;
        x = startX;
        y = startY;
        movedToStart = true;
        return true;
    }

    public boolean hasMovedToStart() {
        return movedToStart;
    }

    /** L'objectif ne bouge pas lors de la mise Ã  jour par frame. */
    @Override
    public void update(float deltaTime) {
    }

    /** Au premier contact avec le joueur, annonce que le pays est terminÃ©. */
    @Override
    public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
        if (!reached && collidesWith(player)) {
            reached = true;
            bus.publish(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        }
    }

    public boolean isReached() {
        return reached;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\Hitbox.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** A contact rectangle. */
public class Hitbox {
    private float x;
    private float y;
    private final float width;
    private final float height;

    public Hitbox(float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
    }

    public boolean overlaps(Hitbox other) {
        return x < other.x + other.width && x + width > other.x
                && y < other.y + other.height && y + height > other.y;
    }

    public void setPosition(float x, float y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
    }

    public float getX() {
        return x;
    }

    public float getY() {
        return y;
    }

    public float getWidth() {
        return width;
    }

    public float getHeight() {
        return height;
    }
}
