========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\Level.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

/** Grille d'un niveau. Les coordonnÃ©es commencent en bas Ã  gauche. */
public class Level {
    public static final int TILE = 32;

    private final Tile[][] grid;
    private final char[][] trapLetters;
    private int attempt;

    public Level(Tile[][] grid) {
        if (grid == null || grid.length == 0 || grid[0] == null || grid[0].length == 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("La grille du niveau ne peut pas Ãªtre vide.");
        }
        int cols = grid[0].length;
        for (Tile[] row : grid) {
            if (row == null || row.length != cols) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Toutes les lignes du niveau doivent avoir la mÃªme largeur.");
            }
        }
        this.grid = grid;
        this.trapLetters = new char[grid.length][cols];
    }

    /** Enregistre une lettre de piÃ¨ge lors du chargement de la carte texte. */
    public void setTrapLetter(int col, int row, char letter) {
        if (!TrapFactory.isTrap(letter)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("PiÃ¨ge inconnu : " + letter);
        }
        trapLetters[row][col] = letter;
    }

    public char getTrapLetter(int col, int row) {
        return trapLetters[row][col];
    }

    public float getStartX() {
        for (int row = 0; row < getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < getCols(); col++) {
                if (grid[row][col] == Tile.START) return col * TILE;
            }
        }
        return TILE;
    }

    public float getStartY() {
        for (int row = 0; row < getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < getCols(); col++) {
                if (grid[row][col] == Tile.START) return row * TILE;
            }
        }
        return 2 * TILE;
    }

        /** CrÃ©e l'objectif du niveau sur la case G de la carte texte. */
    public Goal createGoal() {
        for (int row = 0; row < getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < getCols(); col++) {
                if (grid[row][col] == Tile.GOAL) {
                    return new Goal(col * TILE, row * TILE);
                }
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("Le niveau n'a pas d'objectif : il manque la lettre G.");
    }

    /** Instancie les piÃ¨ges sur les cases de la carte, aprÃ¨s la crÃ©ation du joueur. */
    public List<Trap> createTraps(Player player) {
        List<Trap> traps = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int row = 0; row < getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < getCols(); col++) {
                char letter = trapLetters[row][col];
                if (letter != 0) {
                    Trap trap = TrapFactory.create(letter, col * TILE, row * TILE, player);
                    trap.setStart(getStartX(), getStartY());
                    trap.setLevel(this);
                    traps.add(trap);
                }
            }
        }
        return traps;
    }

    public int getCols() {
        return grid[0].length;
    }

    public int getRows() {
        return grid.length;
    }

    /** Retourne le type de case; les coordonnÃ©es hors grille sont des murs. */
    public Tile getTile(int col, int row) {
        if (col < 0 || col >= getCols() || row < 0 || row >= getRows()) {
            return Tile.WALL;
        }
        return grid[row][col];
    }

    /** Les coordonnÃ©es hors de la grille sont considÃ©rÃ©es comme des murs. */
        public boolean isWall(int col, int row) {
        if (row < 0) {
            return false;
        }
        if (col < 0 || col >= getCols() || row >= getRows()) {
            return true;
        }
        return grid[row][col] == Tile.WALL;
    }

    /** NumÃ©ro de l'essai dans ce pays : 0 la premiÃ¨re fois, puis 1, 2â€¦ aprÃ¨s chaque mort. */
    public int getAttempt() {
        return attempt;
    }

    public void setAttempt(int attempt) {
        this.attempt = attempt;
    }

    /** Ajoute (true) ou enlÃ¨ve (false) un mur pendant la partie. */
    public void setWall(int col, int row, boolean wall) {
        if (col < 0 || col >= getCols() || row < 0 || row >= getRows()) {
            return;
        }
        grid[row][col] = wall ? Tile.WALL : Tile.EMPTY;
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\model\Player.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** Personnage contrÃ´lÃ© par le joueur, avec une gravitÃ© verticale simple. */
public class Player extends Entity {
    public static final float SPRITE_DRAW_SIZE = 64f;
    public static final float SPRITE_DRAW_Y_OFFSET = -12f;
    private static final float SPRITE_SOURCE_SIZE = 48f;
    // Union of opaque pixels across the current idle, walk and jump PNG frames.
    private static final float SPRITE_ALPHA_LEFT = 16f;
    private static final float SPRITE_ALPHA_TOP = 9f;
    private static final float SPRITE_ALPHA_RIGHT = 35f;
    private static final float SPRITE_ALPHA_BOTTOM = 39f;
    private static final float GROUND_Y = 0f;
    /** Croissant (France) : on court 60 % plus vite et on saute 15 % plus haut. */
    private static final float BOOST_SPEED = 1.6f;
    private static final float BOOST_JUMP = 1.15f;
    /** Glace (Laponie) : la vitesse ne change que petit Ã  petit, on glisse. */
    private static final float ICE_GRIP = 3f;

    private final float moveSpeed;
    private float vx;
    private float vy;
    private boolean onGround;
    private boolean slowedNextFrame;
    private float boostTime;
    private boolean icy;

    public Player(float x, float y, float width, float height, float moveSpeed) {
        super(x, y, width, height);
        if (width <= 0f || height <= 0f) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Player width and height must be positive");
        }
        this.moveSpeed = moveSpeed;
        this.onGround = y <= GROUND_Y;
        setContactBounds(spriteContactBounds(width, height));
    }

    private static ContactBounds spriteContactBounds(float width, float height) {
        float scale = SPRITE_DRAW_SIZE / SPRITE_SOURCE_SIZE;
        float drawX = (width - SPRITE_DRAW_SIZE) / 2f;
        float drawY = SPRITE_DRAW_Y_OFFSET;
        return new ContactBounds(
                (drawX + SPRITE_ALPHA_LEFT * scale) / width,
                (drawY + (SPRITE_SOURCE_SIZE - SPRITE_ALPHA_BOTTOM) * scale) / height,
                (drawX + SPRITE_ALPHA_RIGHT * scale) / width,
                (drawY + (SPRITE_SOURCE_SIZE - SPRITE_ALPHA_TOP) * scale) / height);
    }

    /** Update gÃ©nÃ©rique de Entity : applique la gravitÃ© sans entrÃ©e clavier. */
    @Override
    public void update(float deltaTime) {
        update(deltaTime, false, false, false);
    }

    /** Commandes injectables, sans dÃ©pendance au clavier libGDX. */
    public void update(float deltaTime, PlayerInput input) {
        update(deltaTime, input.isLeft(), input.isRight(), input.isJump());
    }

    /**
     * Calcule les vitesses selon les commandes et la gravitÃ©. La position est ensuite
     * appliquÃ©e par {@link CollisionSystem#move(Player, Level, float)}.
     */
    public void update(float deltaTime, boolean left, boolean right, boolean jump) {
        float speed = isBoosted() ? moveSpeed * BOOST_SPEED : moveSpeed;
        if (slowedNextFrame) {
            speed *= 0.5f;
        }
        slowedNextFrame = false;
        float wanted = 0f;
        if (left != right) {
            wanted = left ? -speed : speed;
        }
        if (icy) {
            // Sur la glace, on se rapproche doucement de la vitesse voulue : on glisse.
            vx += (wanted - vx) * Math.min(1f, ICE_GRIP * deltaTime);
        } else {
            vx = wanted;
        }

        if (jump && onGround) {
            vy = isBoosted() ? GameConfig.JUMP_SPEED * BOOST_JUMP : GameConfig.JUMP_SPEED;
            onGround = false;
        }

        // La gravitÃ© nÃ©gative ralentit la montÃ©e puis accÃ©lÃ¨re la chute.
        vy += GameConfig.GRAVITY * deltaTime;
        boostTime = Math.max(0f, boostTime - deltaTime);
    }

    /** Active la glace : le joueur glisse (Laponie). */
    public void setIcy(boolean icy) {
        this.icy = icy;
    }

    public boolean isIcy() {
        return icy;
    }

    /** Donne un boost (plus rapide, saute plus haut) pendant quelques secondes. */
    public void boost(float seconds) {
        boostTime = seconds;
    }

    public boolean isBoosted() {
        return boostTime > 0f;
    }

    /** Ralentissement temporaire provoquÃ© par le sable mouvant. */
    public void slowNextFrame() {
        slowedNextFrame = true;
    }

    /** Replace le joueur et remet Ã  zÃ©ro sa vitesse (porte de retour). */
    public void teleport(float x, float y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        vx = 0f;
        vy = 0f;
        onGround = false;
        slowedNextFrame = false;
    }

    public float getVx() {
        return vx;
    }

    /** Annule la vitesse verticale aprÃ¨s un contact avec le sol. */
    void land() {
        this.vy = 0f;
        this.onGround = true;
    }

    /** Annule la vitesse verticale aprÃ¨s un contact avec un plafond. */
    void stopVerticalMovement() {
        vy = 0f;
    }

    /** Marque le joueur en l'air lorsqu'il ne touche plus de sol. */
    void leaveGround() {
        onGround = false;
    }

    /** Annule la vitesse horizontale aprÃ¨s un contact avec un mur. */
    void stopHorizontalMovement() {
        vx = 0f;
    }

    public float getVy() {
        return vy;
    }

    public boolean isOnGround() {
        return onGround;
    }
}

/**
 * Patron DÃ©corateur : enveloppe le vrai clavier et Ã©change gauche et droite.

package com.teikinui.game.model;

/** Type d'une case de la grille du niveau. */
public enum Tile {
    EMPTY,
    WALL,
    START,
    GOAL;

    public static Tile fromChar(char symbol) {
        return switch (symbol) {
            case '#', 'q' -> WALL; // le sable mouvant est une case de sol solide
            case 'P' -> START;
            case 'G' -> GOAL;
            default -> EMPTY;
        };
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\view\Assets.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Pixmap;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Texture;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.BitmapFont;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Disposable;

/**
 * Charge chaque ressource une seule fois et la retrouve par son nom.
 * Toutes les ressources sont libÃ©rÃ©es ensemble dans dispose().
 */
public final class Assets implements Disposable {
    private static final float TEXT_SCALE = 1.4f;
    private static final float TITLE_SCALE = 3f;
    private static final float BIG_SCALE = 5f;
    private static final float SMALL_SCALE = 0.9f;

    private final BitmapFont font;
    private final BitmapFont titleFont;
    private final BitmapFont bigFont;
    private final BitmapFont smallFont;
    private final Texture pixel;
    private final Map<String, Texture> textures = new HashMap<>();

    public Assets() {
        font = new BitmapFont();
        font.getData().setScale(TEXT_SCALE);
        titleFont = new BitmapFont();
        titleFont.getData().setScale(TITLE_SCALE);

        bigFont = new BitmapFont();
        bigFont.getData().setScale(BIG_SCALE);
        smallFont = new BitmapFont();
        smallFont.getData().setScale(SMALL_SCALE);

        Pixmap pixmap = new Pixmap(1, 1, Pixmap.Format.RGBA8888);
        try {
            pixmap.setColor(Color.WHITE);
            pixmap.fill();
            pixel = new Texture(pixmap);
        } finally {
            pixmap.dispose();
        }
    }

    /** Police des textes normaux (HUD, messages). */
    public BitmapFont getFont() {
        return font;
    }

    /** Grande police des titres. */
    public BitmapFont getTitleFont() {
        return titleFont;
    }

    public BitmapFont getBigFont() {
        return bigFont;
    }

    public BitmapFont getSmallFont() {
        return smallFont;
    }

    /** Vrai si assets/sprites/nom.png existe; les appelants peuvent alors choisir un fallback. */
    public boolean hasTexture(String name) {
        return name != null && !name.isBlank() && Gdx.files != null
                && Gdx.files.internal("assets/sprites/" + name + ".png").exists();
    }

    /** Image assets/sprites/nom.png, chargÃ©e au premier appel puis rÃ©utilisÃ©e. */
    public Texture getTexture(String name) {
        if (!hasTexture(name)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Texture absente : assets/sprites/" + name + ".png");
        }
        return textures.computeIfAbsent(name,
                key -> new Texture(Gdx.files.internal("assets/sprites/" + key + ".png")));
    }

    /** Dessine un rectangle plein avec les coordonnÃ©es du monde (origine en bas Ã  gauche). */
    public void fill(SpriteBatch batch, Color color, float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        batch.setColor(color);
        try {
            batch.draw(pixel, x, y, width, height);
        } finally {
            batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        }
    }

    /** Dessine une image blanche teintÃ©e; une image absente est simplement ignorÃ©e. */
    public void drawTinted(SpriteBatch batch, String name, Color color, float x, float y) {
        if (!hasTexture(name)) {
            return;
        }
        batch.setColor(color);
        try {
            batch.draw(getTexture(name), x, y);
        } finally {
            batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        font.dispose();
        titleFont.dispose();
        bigFont.dispose();
        smallFont.dispose();
        pixel.dispose();
        for (Texture texture : textures.values()) {
            texture.dispose();
        }
        textures.clear();
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\view\BaseScreen.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Screen;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.OrthographicCamera;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.ScreenUtils;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.viewport.FitViewport;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.viewport.Viewport;
import com.teikinui.game.TripTrapGame;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Updatable;

/**
 * Base de tous les Ã©crans. La camÃ©ra est fixe et montre exactement
 * GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH x GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT pixels du monde.
 * render() est Ã©crit une seule fois ici (patron Template Method) :
 * il met Ã  jour l'Ã©cran, efface, puis le dessine.
 */
public abstract class BaseScreen implements Screen, Updatable, Drawable {
    protected final TripTrapGame game;
    protected final OrthographicCamera camera = new OrthographicCamera();
    private final Viewport viewport =
            new FitViewport(GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT, camera);
    private float[] background = {0f, 0f, 0f};

    protected BaseScreen(TripTrapGame game) {
        this.game = game;
        viewport.update(Gdx.graphics.getWidth(), Gdx.graphics.getHeight(), true);
    }

    @Override
    public final void render(float delta) {
        update(delta);
        ScreenUtils.clear(background[0], background[1], background[2], 1f);
        viewport.apply();
        camera.update();
        SpriteBatch batch = game.getBatch();
        batch.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
        draw(batch);
    }

    /** Couleur du fond : rouge, vert, bleu entre 0 et 1. */
    protected void setBackground(float[] rgb) {
        background = rgb.clone();
    }

    /** Convertit une coordonnÃ©e verticale mesurÃ©e depuis le haut en coordonnÃ©e LibGDX. */
    protected static float top(float yFromTop) {
        return GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT - yFromTop;
    }

    /** Si la fenÃªtre change de taille, l'image s'adapte sans se dÃ©former. */
    @Override
    public void resize(int width, int height) {
        viewport.update(width, height, true);
    }

    @Override
    public void show() {
    }

    @Override
    public void pause() {
    }

    @Override
    public void resume() {
    }

    @Override
    public void hide() {
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose() {
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import java.util.Locale;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.BitmapFont;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.GlyphLayout;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelManager;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameSession;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameTimer;

/** Bandeau supÃ©rieur : pays, chrono et morts, dessinÃ© sur la rangÃ©e haute du niveau. */
public final class Hud implements Drawable {
    private static final float NAME_X = 130f;
    private static final float FLAG_GAP = 10f;
    private final GlyphLayout nameLayout = new GlyphLayout();
    private final Assets assets;
    private final GameTimer timer;
    private final GameSession session;
    private final LevelManager levels;
    private LevelTheme styledTheme;
    private ThemeStyle style;

    public Hud(Assets assets, GameTimer timer, GameSession session, LevelManager levels) {
        if (assets == null || timer == null || session == null || levels == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Les dÃ©pendances du HUD sont obligatoires.");
        }
        this.assets = assets;
        this.timer = timer;
        this.session = session;
        this.levels = levels;
        updateStyle();
    }

    @Override
    public void draw(SpriteBatch batch) {
        updateStyle();
        BitmapFont font = assets.getFont();
        float bottom = GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT - GameConfig.TILE;
        assets.fill(batch, style.hudBar(), 0f, bottom,
                GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, GameConfig.TILE);

        font.setColor(style.accent());
        font.draw(batch, "NIVEAU " + (levels.getCurrent().ordinal() + 1), 16f, bottom + 24f);
        String name = levels.getCurrent().getDisplayName();
        font.setColor(style.soft());
        font.draw(batch, name, NAME_X, bottom + 24f);
        drawFlag(batch, font, name, bottom);

        assets.drawTinted(batch, "icon_clock", style.halo(), 420f, bottom + 8f);
        font.setColor(timer.getRemaining() <= 30f ? Palette.TIME_LOW : Palette.WHITE);
        font.draw(batch, formatTime(timer.getRemaining()), 442f, bottom + 24f);

        assets.drawTinted(batch, "icon_skull", Palette.WHITE, 880f, bottom + 8f);
        font.setColor(Palette.WHITE);
        font.draw(batch, Integer.toString(session.getDeathCount()), 902f, bottom + 24f);
        font.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    }

        /** Le petit drapeau du pays, juste Ã  droite de son nom. */
    private void drawFlag(SpriteBatch batch, BitmapFont font, String name, float bottom) {
        String flag = flagSprite(levels.getCurrent());
        if (!assets.hasTexture(flag)) {
            return;
        }
        nameLayout.setText(font, name);
        batch.draw(assets.getTexture(flag), NAME_X + nameLayout.width + FLAG_GAP, bottom + 8f);
    }

    /** SRI_LANKA donne Â« flag_srilanka Â» : l'image assets/sprites/flag_srilanka.png. */
    static String flagSprite(LevelTheme theme) {
        return "flag_" + theme.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).replace("_", "");
    }

    private void updateStyle() {
        LevelTheme current = levels.getCurrent();
        if (current != styledTheme) {
            styledTheme = current;
            style = ThemeStyle.of(current);
        }
    }

    /** 479,2 secondes donnent Â« 08:00 Â»; 61 secondes donnent Â« 01:01 Â». */
    public static String formatTime(float seconds) {
        int total = (int) Math.ceil(Math.max(0f, seconds));
        return String.format("%02d:%02d", total / 60, total % 60);
    }
}

    private void drawDepartureBoard(SpriteBatch batch, Assets assets) {
        BitmapFont font = assets.getFont();

        font.draw(batch, "D Ã‰ P A R T S", 436f, top(282f));

        small.draw(batch, "DIFFICULTÃ‰", 792f, top(306f));

        for (int index = 0; index < countries.length; index++) {
            float rowTop = 322f + index * 20f;
            if (index == selected) {
                assets.fill(batch, Palette.BOARD_ROW, TABLE_X, top(rowTop + 18f), TABLE_WIDTH, 20f);
                font.setColor(Palette.ORANGE);
                font.draw(batch, ">", 426f, top(rowTop));
            }

            int bombs = countries[index].getDifficulty();
            font.setColor(Palette.GOLD);
            small.setColor(Palette.GOLD);
            small.draw(batch, String.format(Locale.ROOT, "TT %02d", index + 1), 436f, top(rowTop));
            small.draw(batch, countries[index].getDisplayName().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT),
                    496f, top(rowTop));
            small.draw(batch, GATES.charAt(index) + String.valueOf(index + 1), 620f, top(rowTop));
            small.setColor(statusColor(bombs));
            small.draw(batch, statusText(bombs), 672f, top(rowTop));

            if (assets.hasTexture("icon_bomb")) {
                Texture bomb = assets.getTexture("icon_bomb");
                for (int count = 0; count < bombs; count++) {
                    batch.draw(bomb, BOMB_X + count * BOMB_STEP, top(rowTop + 14f),
                            BOMB_SIZE, BOMB_SIZE);
                }
            }
        }

        font.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        small.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    }

    private void drawCredits(SpriteBatch batch, Assets assets) {
        BitmapFont small = assets.getSmallFont();
        small.setColor(Palette.BEIGE);
        small.draw(batch, "Najet Â· Teikinui Â· Julien â€” Epitech 2026",
                600f, top(520f), 330f, Align.right, false);
        small.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    }

    /** Bombes dessinÃ©es en formes simples si icon_bomb.png n'est pas livrÃ©. */
    private void drawFallbackBombs(Assets assets) {
        if (assets.hasTexture("icon_bomb")) {
            return;
        }
        shapes.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
        shapes.begin(ShapeRenderer.ShapeType.Filled);
        for (int index = 0; index < countries.length; index++) {
            float rowTop = 322f + index * 20f;
            float centerY = top(rowTop + 8f);
            for (int count = 0; count < countries[index].getDifficulty(); count++) {
                float x = BOMB_X + count * BOMB_STEP;
                shapes.setColor(Palette.ORANGE_DARK);
                shapes.circle(x + BOMB_SIZE / 2f, centerY, BOMB_SIZE / 2f + 1f);
                shapes.setColor(Palette.ORANGE);
                shapes.circle(x + BOMB_SIZE / 2f, centerY, BOMB_SIZE / 2f - 1f);
                shapes.rect(x + 7f, centerY + 4f, 3f, 4f);
            }
        }
        shapes.end();
    }

    static String statusText(int bombs) {
        return switch (bombs) {
            case 1 -> "Ã€ L'HEURE";
            case 2 -> "RETARDÃ‰";
            case 3 -> "ANNULÃ‰ ?";
            default -> "PERDU";
        };
    }

    static Color statusColor(int bombs) {
        return switch (bombs) {
            case 1 -> Palette.ON_TIME;
            case 2 -> Palette.LATE;
            case 3 -> Palette.CANCELLED;
            default -> Palette.LOST;
        };
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        shapes.dispose();
        super.dispose();
    }
}

/** Carte d'embarquement de fin de partie, mÃªme sans end_bg.png. */
public final class MessageScreen extends BaseScreen {
    /** DonnÃ©es figÃ©es Ã  la fin de la partie (pas de rÃ©fÃ©rence au niveau qui sera dÃ©truit). */
    public record Board(String flight, String destination, String completed, String clock,
                        String gate, String deathsLabel, boolean victory) {
        public static Board forResult(LevelTheme country, int deaths, float remaining, boolean victory) {
            if (country == null || deaths < 0 || !Float.isFinite(remaining)) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("RÃ©sultat de partie invalide.");
            }
            int index = country.ordinal();
            return new Board(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "TT %02d", index + 1),
                    country.getDisplayName().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT),
                    (victory ? LevelTheme.values().length : index) + " SUR " + LevelTheme.values().length,
                    Hud.formatTime(remaining), "ABCDEFGH".charAt(index) + String.valueOf(index + 1),
                    "T'es mort " + deaths + " fois.", victory);
        }
    }

    /** Tampon vert foncÃ© de la victoire, lisible sur le billet crÃ¨me. */
    private static final Color STAMP_OK = Color.valueOf("#2E7D32");

    private final String title;
    private final String details;
    private final Board board;
    private final Runnable restart;
    private final Runnable menu;
    private boolean ended;

    public MessageScreen(TripTrapGame game, String title, String details, Board board,
                         Runnable restart, Runnable menu) {
        super(game);
        this.title = title;
        this.details = details;
        this.board = board;
        this.restart = restart;
        this.menu = menu;
        setBackground(new float[] {Palette.HALL.r, Palette.HALL.g, Palette.HALL.b});
    }

    enum Action { NONE, RESTART, MENU }

    static Action actionFor(boolean ended, boolean r, boolean escape, boolean enter) {
        if (ended) return Action.NONE;
        if (r) return Action.RESTART;
        return escape || enter ? Action.MENU : Action.NONE;
    }

    @Override
    public void update(float dt) {
        if (ended) return;
        Action action = actionFor(false, Gdx.input.isKeyJustPressed(Input.Keys.R),
                Gdx.input.isKeyJustPressed(Input.Keys.ESCAPE),
                Gdx.input.isKeyJustPressed(Input.Keys.ENTER));
        if (action != Action.NONE) {
            ended = true;
            if (action == Action.RESTART) restart.run();
            else menu.run();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void draw(SpriteBatch batch) {
        Assets assets = game.getAssets();
        batch.begin();
        if (assets.hasTexture("end_bg")) {
            batch.draw(assets.getTexture("end_bg"), 0, 0, GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT);
        } else {
            drawFallback(batch, assets);
        }
        BitmapFont small = assets.getSmallFont();
        BitmapFont font = assets.getFont();
        BitmapFont titleFont = assets.getTitleFont();

        String banner = (board.victory() ? "ARRIVÃ‰E Ã€ L'HEURE Â· " : "TEMPS Ã‰COULÃ‰ Â· ") + board.clock();
        small.setColor(board.victory() ? Palette.ON_TIME : Palette.LOST);
        small.draw(batch, banner, 0, top(44), GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, Align.center, false);
        titleFont.setColor(Palette.GOLD);
        titleFont.draw(batch, title, 5, top(64) - 5, GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, Align.center, false);
        titleFont.setColor(Palette.CREAM);
        titleFont.draw(batch, title, 0, top(64), GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, Align.center, false);
        font.setColor(Palette.BEIGE);
        font.draw(batch, details, 100, top(106), 760, Align.center, true);
        font.setColor(Palette.GOLD);
        font.draw(batch, board.deathsLabel(), 0, top(170), GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, Align.center, false);

        font.setColor(Palette.INK);
        font.draw(batch, "TRIP TRAP AIRLINES", 183, top(211));
        small.setColor(Palette.INK);
        small.draw(batch, "CARTE D'EMBARQUEMENT", 404, top(216), 208, Align.right, false);
        field(batch, small, font, "PASSAGER", "TOI", 183, 247);
        field(batch, small, font, "VOL", board.flight(), 333, 247);
        field(batch, small, font, "DESTINATION", board.destination(), 483, 247);
        field(batch, small, font, "PAYS FINIS", board.completed(), 183, 297);
        field(batch, small, font, "MORTS", board.deathsLabel().replaceAll("[^0-9]", ""), 333, 297);
        field(batch, small, font, "CHRONO", board.clock(), 483, 297);
        font.setColor(board.victory() ? STAMP_OK : Palette.STAMP);
        font.draw(batch, board.victory() ? "EMBARQUEMENT VALIDÃ‰" : "EMBARQUEMENT REFUSÃ‰", 362, top(336));
        small.setColor(Palette.PASS_LABEL);
        small.draw(batch, "PORTE", 648, top(217));
        font.setColor(Palette.INK);
        font.draw(batch, board.gate(), 648, top(230));

        small.setColor(Color.WHITE);

    }

    private void field(SpriteBatch batch, BitmapFont small, BitmapFont font,
                       String label, String value, float x, float y) {

    private void drawFallback(SpriteBatch batch, Assets assets) {

        assets.fill(batch, Palette.STAMP, 357, top(381), 263, 3);
        assets.fill(batch, Palette.STAMP, 357, top(381), 3, 31);
        assets.fill(batch, Palette.STAMP, 617, top(381), 3, 31);
        assets.fill(batch, Palette.ORANGE, 268, top(437), 222, 39);
        assets.fill(batch, Palette.BOARD_ROW, 506, top(437), 228, 39);
    }

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Texture;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.Animation;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.TextureRegion;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Array;

/** Animations du voyageur, avec prioritÃ© au sprite propre au pays. */
public final class PlayerSprites {
    public enum State {
        IDLE("idle", 0.25f, Animation.PlayMode.LOOP),
        WALK("walk", 0.10f, Animation.PlayMode.LOOP),
        JUMP("jump", 0.12f, Animation.PlayMode.NORMAL),
        DIE("die", 0.10f, Animation.PlayMode.NORMAL);

        private final String fileName;
        private final float frameDuration;
        private final Animation.PlayMode playMode;

        State(String fileName, float frameDuration, Animation.PlayMode playMode) {
            this.fileName = fileName;
            this.frameDuration = frameDuration;
            this.playMode = playMode;
        }
    }

    private final Animation<TextureRegion> idle;
    private final Animation<TextureRegion> walk;
    private final Animation<TextureRegion> jump;
    private final Animation<TextureRegion> die;

    public PlayerSprites(Assets assets, String country) {
        if (assets == null || country == null || country.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Les ressources et le nom du pays sont obligatoires.");
        }
        idle = loadFor(assets, country, State.IDLE);
        walk = loadFor(assets, country, State.WALK);
        jump = loadFor(assets, country, State.JUMP);
        die = loadFor(assets, country, State.DIE);
    }

    private static Animation<TextureRegion> loadFor(Assets assets, String country, State state) {
        Animation<TextureRegion> countryAnimation = load(assets,
                "player_" + country + "_" + state.fileName, state);
        Animation<TextureRegion> baseAnimation = load(assets, "player_" + state.fileName, state);
        return preferCountryAnimation(countryAnimation, baseAnimation);
    }

    private static Animation<TextureRegion> load(Assets assets, String prefix, State state) {
        Array<TextureRegion> frames = new Array<>();
        for (int index = 0; assets.hasTexture(prefix + "_" + index); index++) {
            frames.add(new TextureRegion(assets.getTexture(prefix + "_" + index)));
        }
        if (frames.isEmpty() && assets.hasTexture(prefix)) {
            Texture strip = assets.getTexture(prefix);
            int frameSize = strip.getHeight();
            if (frameSize > 0 && strip.getWidth() >= frameSize) {
                TextureRegion[][] regions = TextureRegion.split(strip, frameSize, frameSize);
                for (TextureRegion frame : regions[0]) {
                    frames.add(frame);
                }
            }
        }
        return frames.isEmpty() ? null
                : new Animation<>(state.frameDuration, frames, state.playMode);
    }

    static <T> Animation<T> preferCountryAnimation(Animation<T> country, Animation<T> base) {
        return country != null ? country : base;
    }

    static State stateFor(float horizontalVelocity, boolean onGround, boolean dead) {
        if (dead) {
            return State.DIE;
        }
        if (!onGround) {
            return State.JUMP;
        }
        return Math.abs(horizontalVelocity) > 0.01f ? State.WALK : State.IDLE;
    }

    public Animation<TextureRegion> getAnimation(State state) {
        return switch (state) {
            case IDLE -> idle;
            case WALK -> walk;
            case JUMP -> jump;
            case DIE -> die;
        };
    }

    public float getDuration(State state) {
        Animation<TextureRegion> animation = getAnimation(state);
        return animation == null ? 0f : animation.getAnimationDuration();
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

/**
 * La partie : relie le niveau, le chrono, les collisions et l'affichage.
 * La camÃ©ra ne bouge pas : chaque pays tient entiÃ¨rement dans l'Ã©cran.
 */
public final class PlayScreen extends BaseScreen implements GameListener {
    private static final float MAX_STEP = 1f / 30f;

    private final GameSession session;
    private final GameTimer timer;
    private final LevelManager levels;
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final SoundManager sounds = new SoundManager();
    private final InputHandler keyboard = new InputHandler();
    private final CollisionSystem collisionSystem = new CollisionSystem();
    private final ShapeRenderer shapeRenderer = new ShapeRenderer();
    private final Hud hud;
    private final TauntBubble taunt;
    private final SkyCountdown skyCountdown;
    private final Color haloColor = new Color();
    private ThemeStyle style;
    private String themeKey;
    private PlayerSprites playerSprites;
    private PlayerSprites.State spriteState;
    private float stateTime;
    private float deathTime;
    private boolean facingLeft;

    private LevelTheme theme;
    private PlayerInput input;
    private int attempt;
    private Level level;
    private Player player;
    private List<Trap> traps;
    private List<Trap> tahitiReturnWave = List.of();
    private Goal goal;
    private boolean died;
    private boolean goalReached;
    private boolean leaving;

    public PlayScreen(TripTrapGame game, GameSession session, GameTimer timer, LevelManager levels) {
        super(game);
        this.session = session;
        this.timer = timer;
        this.levels = levels;
        bus.subscribe(session);
        bus.subscribe(levels);
        bus.subscribe(this);
        bus.subscribe(sounds);
        hud = new Hud(game.getAssets(), timer, session, levels);
        taunt = new TauntBubble(game.getAssets());
        skyCountdown = new SkyCountdown(game.getAssets());
        loadLevel();
    }

    /** (Re)charge le pays en cours : le joueur revient au dÃ©part, les piÃ¨ges sont remis Ã  zÃ©ro. */
    private void loadLevel() {
        LevelTheme previous = theme;
        theme = levels.getCurrent();
        attempt = theme == previous ? attempt + 1 : 0;
        if (theme != previous) {
            timer.reset();
        }
        String text = Gdx.files.internal("assets/" + theme.getFileName()).readString();
        level = LevelLoader.fromString(text);
        level.setAttempt(attempt);
        player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(),
                GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE, GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        player.setIcy(theme.isIcy());
        if (theme != previous) {
            style = ThemeStyle.of(theme);
            themeKey = theme.name().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).replace("_", "");
            sounds.loadCountry(themeKey);
            playerSprites = new PlayerSprites(game.getAssets(), themeKey);
            taunt.show(style, 3f);
        }
        spriteState = null;
        stateTime = 0f;
        deathTime = 0f;
        facingLeft = false;
        traps = level.createTraps(player);
        skyCountdown.watch(findSky(traps), style);
        goal = level.createGoal();
        tahitiReturnWave = List.of();
        if (theme == LevelTheme.TAHITI) {
            // PrÃ©pare des objets neufs au respawn, hors rendu et collisions avant Gâ†’P.
            tahitiReturnWave = TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player);
        }
        configureContactBounds();
        session.setCurrentLevel(theme.ordinal());
        setBackground(theme.getBackgroundColor());
        // Vietnam : on enveloppe le clavier pour Ã©changer gauche et droite (DÃ©corateur).
        input = theme.hasInvertedControls() ? new ReversedInput(keyboard) : keyboard;
    }

    /** Keeps contact rectangles aligned with the currently rendered sprites. */
    private void configureContactBounds() {
        Assets assets = game.getAssets();
        if (playerSprites.getAnimation(PlayerSprites.State.IDLE) == null) {
            player.setContactBounds(ContactBounds.FULL);
        }

        String goalSprite = "goal_" + themeKey;
        boolean hasGoalSprite = assets.hasTexture(goalSprite);
        goal.setContactBounds(goalContactBounds(theme, hasGoalSprite));

        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (trap instanceof FakeChili) {
                trap.setContactBounds(fakeGoalContactBounds(theme, hasGoalSprite));
            } else if (!usesTrapSprite(trap, assets.hasTexture(trap.getSpriteName()))) {
                // Shape-rendered and intentionally hidden traps keep their gameplay rectangle.
                trap.setContactBounds(ContactBounds.FULL);
            }
        }
    }

    /** On note seulement ce qui s'est passÃ© ; on le traite Ã  la fin de update(). */
    @Override
    public void onEvent(GameEvent event) {
        if (event == GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED) {
            if (!died) {
                deathTime = 0f;
                changeSpriteState(PlayerSprites.State.DIE);
                taunt.show(style, 2f);
            }
            died = true;
        } else if (event == GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED) {
            goalReached = true;
        }
    }

    /** La victoire prend prioritÃ© si un objectif et un piÃ¨ge sont touchÃ©s ensemble. */
    static boolean shouldRestartAfterDeath(boolean died, boolean goalReached) {
        return died && !goalReached;
    }

    /** Attend la fin du sprite de mort s'il existe, sans bloquer le fallback carrÃ©. */
    static boolean shouldRespawnAfterDeath(boolean died, boolean goalReached,
                                           float duration, float elapsed) {
        return shouldRestartAfterDeath(died, goalReached) && elapsed >= duration;
    }

    private void changeSpriteState(PlayerSprites.State next) {
        if (spriteState != next) {
            spriteState = next;
            stateTime = 0f;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void update(float dt) {
        if (Gdx.input.isKeyJustPressed(Input.Keys.M)) {
            sounds.toggleMute();
        }
        if (Gdx.input.isKeyJustPressed(Input.Keys.ESCAPE)) {
            // Ã‰chap : on quitte le niveau et on revient au menu.
            leaving = true;
            game.showMenu();
            return;
        }
        float step = Math.min(dt, MAX_STEP);
        taunt.update(step);
        timer.update(step);
        if (timer.isTimeUp()) {
            bus.publish(GameEvent.TIME_UP);
            game.showTimeUp();
            return;
        }

        if (died) {
            deathTime += step;
            stateTime += step;
        } else {
            player.update(step, input);
            collisionSystem.move(player, level, step);
            activateTahitiReturnWave(theme, player, goal, level, traps, tahitiReturnWave);
            updateTraps(theme, level, goal, traps, step);
            collisionSystem.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
            collisionSystem.checkFall(player, bus);
            collisionSystem.checkContacts(player, List.of(goal), bus);
            if (!died) {
                if (player.getVx() < -0.01f) facingLeft = true;
                else if (player.getVx() > 0.01f) facingLeft = false;
                changeSpriteState(PlayerSprites.stateFor(player.getVx(), player.isOnGround(), false));
                stateTime += step;
            }
        }

        if (goalReached) {
            goalReached = false;
            died = false;
            if (levels.isFinished()) {
                game.showVictory();
            } else {
                loadLevel();
            }
        } else if (shouldRespawnAfterDeath(died, goalReached,
                playerSprites.getDuration(PlayerSprites.State.DIE), deathTime)) {
            died = false;
            loadLevel();
        }
    }

    /** La vague est recrÃ©Ã©e au respawn, mais n'entre en jeu qu'aprÃ¨s Gâ†’P. */
    static boolean activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme theme, Player player, Goal goal,
                                            Level level, List<Trap> traps, List<Trap> wave) {
        if (theme != LevelTheme.TAHITI) return false;
        boolean goalMoved = moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(theme, player, goal,
                level.getStartX(), level.getStartY());
        boolean waveAdded = goal.hasMovedToStart() && addMissingReturnWave(traps, wave);
        return goalMoved || waveAdded;
    }

    /** RÃ©insÃ¨re uniquement les nouveaux objets absents, sans dupliquer la vague. */
    private static boolean addMissingReturnWave(List<Trap> traps, List<Trap> wave) {
        boolean added = false;
        for (Trap trap : wave) {
            if (traps.stream().noneMatch(existing -> existing == trap)) {
                traps.add(trap);
                added = true;
            }
        }
        return added;
    }

    /** L'Ã©toile qui garde G Ã  Tahiti se dÃ©clenche aprÃ¨s le dÃ©part de G, pas avant. */
    static void updateTraps(LevelTheme theme, Level level, Goal goal, List<Trap> traps, float step) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (theme == LevelTheme.TAHITI && trap instanceof Starfish
                    && !goal.hasMovedToStart()
                    && trap.getX() == goal.getX() - 4f * Level.TILE
                    && trap.getY() == goal.getY()
                    && level.getTrapLetter((int)(trap.getX() / Level.TILE),
                            (int)(trap.getY() / Level.TILE)) == 's') continue;
            trap.update(step);
        }
    }

    /** Ã€ une case (Ã©cart entre rectangles) de G, l'objectif de Tahiti rejoint P une fois. */
    static boolean moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme theme, Player player, Goal goal,
                                            float startX, float startY) {
        if (theme != LevelTheme.TAHITI || goal.isReached()) return false;
        float gapX = Math.max(0f, Math.max(goal.getX() - (player.getX() + player.getWidth()),
                player.getX() - (goal.getX() + goal.getWidth())));
        float gapY = Math.max(0f, Math.max(goal.getY() - (player.getY() + player.getHeight()),
                player.getY() - (goal.getY() + goal.getHeight())));
        return gapX <= GameConfig.TILE && gapY <= GameConfig.TILE
                && goal.moveToStart(startX, startY);
    }

    @Override
    public void draw(SpriteBatch batch) {
        if (leaving) {
            // L'Ã©cran vient d'Ãªtre fermÃ© par Ã‰chap : son ShapeRenderer est dÃ©truit.
            return;
        }
        Assets assets = game.getAssets();
        batch.begin();
        String backgroundName = "bg_" + themeKey;
        if (assets.hasTexture(backgroundName)) {
            batch.draw(assets.getTexture(backgroundName), 0, 0,
                    GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT);
        } else {
            assets.fill(batch, style.sky(), 0, 0, GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT);
        }
        drawLevelTextures(batch, assets);
        haloColor.set(style.halo());
        haloColor.a = 0.6f + 0.4f * MathUtils.sin(stateTime * 4f);
        if (!theme.hidesGoal()) {
            assets.drawTinted(batch, "goal_halo", haloColor, goal.getX() - 8f, goal.getY() - 12f);
        } else {
            // Chine : le halo brille autour du faux bol pour attirer le joueur.
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                if (trap instanceof FakeChili && trap.isVisible()) {
                    assets.drawTinted(batch, "goal_halo", haloColor, trap.getX() - 8f, trap.getY() - 12f);
                }
            }
        }
        batch.end();

        shapeRenderer.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
        shapeRenderer.begin(ShapeRenderer.ShapeType.Filled);
        drawLevel();
        if (theme.hidesGoal()) {
            drawSpikes(goal.getX(), goal.getY(), goal.getWidth(), 20f);
        } else if (!assets.hasTexture("goal_" + themeKey)) {
            setColor(theme.getGoalColor());
            shapeRenderer.rect(goal.getX(), goal.getY(), goal.getWidth(), goal.getHeight());
        }
        shapeRenderer.end();

        batch.begin();
        if (!theme.hidesGoal() && assets.hasTexture("goal_" + themeKey)) {
            batch.draw(assets.getTexture("goal_" + themeKey), goal.getX(), goal.getY(),
                    GameConfig.TILE, GameConfig.TILE);
        }
        batch.end();

        shapeRenderer.begin(ShapeRenderer.ShapeType.Filled);
        drawTraps();
        shapeRenderer.end();

        batch.begin();
        drawTrapSprites(batch, assets);
        drawCamouflagedTraps(batch, assets);
        batch.end();

        shapeRenderer.begin(ShapeRenderer.ShapeType.Filled);
        if (playerSprites.getAnimation(spriteState == null ? PlayerSprites.State.IDLE : spriteState) == null) {
            if (player.isBoosted()) shapeRenderer.setColor(1f, 0.80f, 0.25f, 1f);
            else shapeRenderer.setColor(1f, 0.44f, 0.38f, 1f);
            shapeRenderer.rect(player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getWidth(), player.getHeight());
        }
        shapeRenderer.end();

        batch.begin();
        Animation<TextureRegion> animation = playerSprites.getAnimation(
                spriteState == null ? PlayerSprites.State.IDLE : spriteState);
        if (animation != null) {
            TextureRegion frame = animation.getKeyFrame(stateTime);
            PlayerDrawBounds bounds = playerDrawBounds(player);
            if (facingLeft) batch.draw(frame, bounds.x() + bounds.size(), bounds.y(),
                    -bounds.size(), bounds.size());
            else batch.draw(frame, bounds.x(), bounds.y(), bounds.size(), bounds.size());
        }
        hud.draw(batch);
        taunt.draw(batch);
        skyCountdown.draw(batch);
        batch.end();
    }

    /** Le rideau de la Chine, s'il y en a un dans ce pays. */
    static SkyFall findSky(List<Trap> traps) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (trap instanceof SkyFall sky) {
                return sky;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    /** Canvas 48Ã—48 : les pieds opaques (10 px de marge) reposent sur le sol Ã  l'Ã©chelle 64. */
    record PlayerDrawBounds(float x, float y, float size) { }

    static PlayerDrawBounds playerDrawBounds(Player player) {
        float size = Player.SPRITE_DRAW_SIZE;
        return new PlayerDrawBounds(player.getX() + (player.getWidth() - size) / 2f,
                player.getY() + Player.SPRITE_DRAW_Y_OFFSET, size);
    }

    static ContactBounds goalContactBounds(LevelTheme theme, boolean goalTexturePresent) {
        // La vraie sortie chinoise a toujours l'apparence de pics, mÃªme si son PNG existe.
        if (theme.hidesGoal()) return new ContactBounds(0f, 0f, 1f, 0.5f);
        return goalTexturePresent ? theme.getGoalContactBounds() : ContactBounds.FULL;
    }

    static ContactBounds fakeGoalContactBounds(LevelTheme theme, boolean goalTexturePresent) {
        // En Chine, le faux objectif est le bol de nouilles : sa zone suit le dessin du bol.
        return goalTexturePresent ? theme.getGoalContactBounds() : ContactBounds.FULL;
    }

    static String fakeGoalSprite(LevelTheme theme, boolean goalTexturePresent) {
        if (!goalTexturePresent) {
            return null;
        }
        return "goal_" + theme.name().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).replace("_", "");
    }

    static boolean maskHiddenSlabWithBackground(LevelTheme theme, boolean backgroundPresent) {
        return theme == LevelTheme.LAPONIE && backgroundPresent;
    }

    /** Copie le fond derriÃ¨re la dalle cachÃ©e et rend le faux piment identique au vrai. */
    private void drawCamouflagedTraps(SpriteBatch batch, Assets assets) {
        String fakeSprite = fakeGoalSprite(theme, assets.hasTexture("goal_" + themeKey));
        boolean texturedMask = maskHiddenSlabWithBackground(theme, assets.hasTexture("bg_" + themeKey));
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (!trap.isVisible()) continue;
            if (trap instanceof FakeChili && fakeSprite != null) {
                batch.draw(assets.getTexture(fakeSprite), trap.getX(), trap.getY(),
                        trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
            } else if (trap instanceof HiddenSlab slab && !slab.isRevealed() && texturedMask) {
                Texture bg = assets.getTexture("bg_" + themeKey);
                float x = trap.getX(), y = trap.getY();
                batch.draw(bg, x, y, trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight(),
                        x / GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, 1f - y / GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT,
                        (x + trap.getWidth()) / GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH,
                        1f - (y + trap.getHeight()) / GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT);
            }
        }
    }

    static boolean isFloorSurface(Level level, int col, int row) {
        return level.isWall(col, row) && !level.isWall(col, row + 1);
    }

    private void drawLevelTextures(SpriteBatch batch, Assets assets) {
        for (int row = 0; row < level.getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < level.getCols(); col++) {
                if (!level.isWall(col, row)) continue;
                String wall = "wall_" + themeKey;
                String floor = "floor_" + themeKey;
                String name = isFloorSurface(level, col, row) && assets.hasTexture(floor) ? floor : wall;
                if (assets.hasTexture(name)) {
                    batch.draw(assets.getTexture(name), col * Level.TILE, row * Level.TILE,
                            Level.TILE, Level.TILE);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void drawLevel() {
        Assets assets = game.getAssets();
        for (int row = 0; row < level.getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < level.getCols(); col++) {
                if (level.isWall(col, row)) {
                    String floor = "floor_" + themeKey;
                    String name = isFloorSurface(level, col, row) && assets.hasTexture(floor)
                            ? floor : "wall_" + themeKey;
                    if (!assets.hasTexture(name)) {
                        setColor(theme.getWallColor());
                        shapeRenderer.rect(col * Level.TILE, row * Level.TILE, Level.TILE, Level.TILE);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }


    /** Les camouflages et piÃ¨ges Ã  comportement graphique spÃ©cial conservent leur rendu. */
    private static boolean keepsShapeDrawing(Trap trap) {
        return trap instanceof FakeChili || trap instanceof HiddenSlab || trap instanceof SkyFall
                || trap instanceof TiltSlab || trap instanceof InvisibleBlock;
    }

    private static boolean keepsBackdrop(Trap trap) {
        return trap instanceof Monkey || trap instanceof SimitVendor || trap instanceof Croissant;
    }

    static boolean usesTrapSprite(Trap trap, boolean textureAvailable) {
        return textureAvailable && !keepsShapeDrawing(trap);
    }

    static boolean drawsTrapShape(Trap trap, boolean textureAvailable) {
        return !usesTrapSprite(trap, textureAvailable) || keepsBackdrop(trap);
    }

    private boolean hasTrapSprite(Trap trap) {
        return usesTrapSprite(trap, game.getAssets().hasTexture(trap.getSpriteName()));
    }

    private void drawTrapSprites(SpriteBatch batch, Assets assets) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (trap instanceof Monkey monkey && assets.hasTexture("monkey_body")) {
                batch.draw(assets.getTexture("monkey_body"), monkey.getHomeX(), monkey.getHomeY(), 32f, 32f);
            }
            if (trap instanceof SimitVendor vendor && assets.hasTexture("simit_vendor_body")) {
                batch.draw(assets.getTexture("simit_vendor_body"), vendor.getHomeX(), vendor.getHomeY(), 32f, 32f);
            }
            if (!trap.isVisible() || !hasTrapSprite(trap)
                    || trap instanceof Croissant croissant && croissant.isEaten()) continue;
            String name = copiesFloor(trap) && assets.hasTexture("floor_" + themeKey)
                    ? "floor_" + themeKey : trap.getSpriteName();
            batch.draw(assets.getTexture(name), trap.getX(), trapDrawY(level, trap),
                    trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
        }
    }

    /** Une case de sable placÃ©e au-dessus du sol garde son contact aux pieds et son image dans le sol. */
    static float trapDrawY(Level level, Trap trap) {
        if (trap instanceof QuickSand && !level.isWall((int) (trap.getX() / Level.TILE),
                (int) (trap.getY() / Level.TILE))
                && level.isWall((int) (trap.getX() / Level.TILE),
                        (int) (trap.getY() / Level.TILE) - 1)) {
            return trap.getY() - Level.TILE;
        }
        return trap.getY();
    }

    /** PiÃ¨ges troll qui doivent Ãªtre identiques au sol du pays oÃ¹ ils sont posÃ©s. */
    static boolean copiesFloor(Trap trap) {
        return trap instanceof FakeSlab || trap instanceof CrumbleBlock || trap instanceof FakeFloorTrap;
    }

    private void drawTraps() {
    for (Trap trap : traps) {
        if (!trap.isVisible()) {
            continue;
        }
        if (!drawsTrapShape(trap, game.getAssets().hasTexture(trap.getSpriteName()))) {
            continue;
        }
         if (trap instanceof FallingBlock || trap instanceof InvisibleBlock) {
                setColor(theme.getWallColor());
                shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
        } else if (trap instanceof Cactus || trap instanceof PopSpike || trap instanceof SpikeFloor) {
             drawSpikes(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
        } else if (trap instanceof GrowingSpike) {
             drawPillar(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
        } else if (trap instanceof FakeChili) {
             // MÃªme apparence que l'objectif du pays (en Chine : le bol, qui sert d'appÃ¢t).
             if (fakeGoalSprite(theme, game.getAssets().hasTexture("goal_" + themeKey)) == null) {
                 setColor(theme.getGoalColor());
                 shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
             }
        } else if (trap instanceof Monkey monkey) {
             drawMonkey(monkey);
        } else if (trap instanceof Croissant croissant) {
             drawPainting(croissant);
        } else if (trap instanceof Dervish dervish) {
             drawDervish(dervish);
        } else if (trap instanceof SimitVendor vendor) {
             drawSimitVendor(vendor);
        } else if (trap instanceof SkyFall sky) {
             // Le ciel : une bande sombre qui descend depuis le haut de l'Ã©cran.
             shapeRenderer.setColor(0.30f, 0.05f, 0.08f, 1f);
             float top = Level.TILE * level.getRows();
             shapeRenderer.rect(0f, sky.getSkyBottom(), Level.TILE * level.getCols(), top - sky.getSkyBottom());
        } else if (trap instanceof ChargingWall) {
             shapeRenderer.setColor(0.35f, 0.08f, 0.06f, 1f);
             shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
             shapeRenderer.setColor(0.95f, 0.80f, 0.25f, 1f);
             shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), 4f, trap.getHeight());
        } else if (trap instanceof HiddenSlab slab) {
             if (!slab.isRevealed() && !maskHiddenSlabWithBackground(theme,
                     game.getAssets().hasTexture("bg_" + themeKey))) {
                 // Sans PNG, la couleur unie masque la dalle comme auparavant.
                 setColor(theme.getBackgroundColor());
                 shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
             }
        } else if (trap instanceof FakeSlab) {
             setColor(theme.getWallColor());
             shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
        } else if (trap instanceof TiltSlab tiltSlab) {
             if (tiltSlab.getTilt() > 0f) {
                 // PenchÃ©e : chaque case est dessinÃ©e un peu plus bas que sa voisine de gauche.
                 setColor(theme.getWallColor());
                 float drop = tiltSlab.getIndex() * 14f * tiltSlab.getTilt();
                 shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY() - drop, trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
             }
        } else if (trap instanceof Icicle) {
             // Stalactite de glace : un triangle bleu clair pointÃ© vers le bas.
             shapeRenderer.setColor(0.75f, 0.92f, 1f, 1f);
             shapeRenderer.triangle(trap.getX(), trap.getY() + trap.getHeight(),
                     trap.getX() + trap.getWidth(), trap.getY() + trap.getHeight(),
                     trap.getX() + trap.getWidth() / 2f, trap.getY());
        } else if (trap instanceof Snowball) {
             shapeRenderer.setColor(0.97f, 0.98f, 1f, 1f);
             float r = trap.getWidth() / 2f;
             shapeRenderer.circle(trap.getX() + r, trap.getY() + r, r);
        } else if (trap instanceof Loukoum) {
             shapeRenderer.setColor(0.95f, 0.55f, 0.70f, 1f);
             shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trap.getY(), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
        } else {
            shapeRenderer.setColor(0.85f, 0.75f, 0.2f, 1f);
            shapeRenderer.rect(trap.getX(), trapDrawY(level, trap), trap.getWidth(), trap.getHeight());
        }
    }
    }

    /** Deux pics blancs qui sortent du sol. */
    private void drawSpikes(float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.95f, 0.95f, 0.9f, 1f);
        float half = width / 2f;
        shapeRenderer.triangle(x, y, x + half, y, x + half / 2f, y + height * 0.8f);
        shapeRenderer.triangle(x + half, y, x + width, y, x + half * 1.5f, y + height * 0.8f);
    }

    /** Singe : un corps marron sous le plafond + le fruit vert qu'il lance. */
    private void drawMonkey(Monkey monkey) {
        if (!game.getAssets().hasTexture("monkey_body")) {
            shapeRenderer.setColor(0.45f, 0.30f, 0.18f, 1f);
            shapeRenderer.rect(monkey.getHomeX() + 6f, monkey.getHomeY() + 10f, 20f, 20f);
            shapeRenderer.setColor(0.85f, 0.70f, 0.55f, 1f);
            shapeRenderer.rect(monkey.getHomeX() + 11f, monkey.getHomeY() + 14f, 10f, 8f);
        }
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.55f, 0.75f, 0.20f, 1f);
        shapeRenderer.rect(monkey.getX(), monkey.getY(), monkey.getWidth(), monkey.getHeight());
    }

    /** Tableau dorÃ© accrochÃ© au mur, avec le croissant dedans tant qu'il n'est pas mangÃ©. */
    private void drawPainting(Croissant croissant) {
        float x = croissant.getX() - 13f;
        float y = croissant.getY() - 12f;
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.85f, 0.68f, 0.25f, 1f);
        shapeRenderer.rect(x, y, 48f, 40f);
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.20f, 0.25f, 0.35f, 1f);
        shapeRenderer.rect(x + 5f, y + 5f, 38f, 30f);
        if (!croissant.isEaten()) {
            shapeRenderer.setColor(0.90f, 0.60f, 0.20f, 1f);
            shapeRenderer.rect(croissant.getX(), croissant.getY(), croissant.getWidth(), croissant.getHeight());
        }
    }

    /** Derviche : longue robe blanche qui tourne (elle s'Ã©largit et se resserre) + chapeau. */
    private void drawDervish(Dervish dervish) {
        float x = dervish.getX();
        float y = dervish.getY();
        float w = dervish.getWidth();
        float open = 4f * (float) Math.abs(Math.sin(Math.toRadians(dervish.getSpin())));
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.95f, 0.95f, 0.92f, 1f);
        shapeRenderer.triangle(x - open, y, x + w + open, y, x + w / 2f, y + 22f);
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.55f, 0.35f, 0.25f, 1f);
        shapeRenderer.rect(x + w / 2f - 4f, y + 20f, 8f, 10f);
    }

    /** Vendeur de simits : un corps rouge + le simit (anneau brun) qu'il lance. */
    private void drawSimitVendor(SimitVendor vendor) {
        if (!game.getAssets().hasTexture("simit_vendor_body")) {
            shapeRenderer.setColor(0.75f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1f);
            shapeRenderer.rect(vendor.getHomeX() + 6f, vendor.getHomeY(), 20f, 28f);
        }
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.70f, 0.45f, 0.20f, 1f);
        shapeRenderer.rect(vendor.getX(), vendor.getY(), vendor.getWidth(), vendor.getHeight());
    }

    /** Pilier de pics : deux barres blanches cÃ´te Ã  cÃ´te (comme dans la vidÃ©o). */
    private void drawPillar(float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        shapeRenderer.setColor(0.95f, 0.95f, 0.9f, 1f);
        float bar = width / 3f;
        shapeRenderer.rect(x, y, bar, height);
        shapeRenderer.rect(x + 2 * bar, y, bar, height);
    }

    private void setColor(float[] rgb) {
        shapeRenderer.setColor(rgb[0], rgb[1], rgb[2], 1f);
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        shapeRenderer.dispose();
        sounds.dispose();
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.BitmapFont;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.GlyphLayout;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.SkyFall;

/** Panneau au centre de l'Ã©cran : le dÃ©compte du rideau qui tombe (Chine). */
public final class SkyCountdown implements Drawable {
    private static final float WIDTH = 520f;
    private static final float HEIGHT = 150f;
    private static final float BORDER = 3f;
    static final String MESSAGE = "Le rideau tombe, ne bouge plus !";

    private final Assets assets;
    private final GlyphLayout layout = new GlyphLayout();
    private ThemeStyle style;
    private SkyFall sky;

    public SkyCountdown(Assets assets) {
        if (assets == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Les ressources du jeu sont obligatoires.");
        }
        this.assets = assets;
    }

    /** Le rideau Ã  surveiller dans ce pays (null si le pays n'en a pas). */
    public void watch(SkyFall newSky, ThemeStyle newStyle) {
        sky = newSky;
        style = newStyle;
    }

    /** Le panneau ne s'affiche que pendant le dÃ©compte, avant la chute. */
    static boolean shows(SkyFall sky) {
        return sky != null && sky.getCountdown() > 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void draw(SpriteBatch batch) {
        if (style == null || !shows(sky)) {
            return;
        }
        float x = (GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH - WIDTH) / 2f;
        float y = (GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT - HEIGHT) / 2f;
        assets.fill(batch, style.accent(), x - BORDER, y - BORDER, WIDTH + 2f * BORDER, HEIGHT + 2f * BORDER);
        assets.fill(batch, style.hudBar(), x, y, WIDTH, HEIGHT);
        drawCentered(batch, assets.getFont(), MESSAGE, y + HEIGHT - 18f);
        drawCentered(batch, assets.getBigFont(), String.valueOf(sky.getCountdown()), y + 95f);
    }

    private void drawCentered(SpriteBatch batch, BitmapFont font, String text, float top) {
        layout.setText(font, text);
        font.setColor(Palette.WHITE);
        font.draw(batch, layout, (GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH - layout.width) / 2f, top);
        font.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\view\SoundManager.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.audio.Sound;
import com.badlogic.gdx.files.FileHandle;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Disposable;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameListener;

/**
 * Joue une phrase de moquerie du pays Ã  chaque mort.
 * AbonnÃ© Ã  l'EventBus (pattern Observer) : il rÃ©agit Ã  PLAYER_DIED.
 * Fichiers : assets/sounds/death_<pays>_<n>.ogg, n = 0, 1, 2â€¦ sans trou.
 */
public final class SoundManager implements GameListener, Disposable {
    private final List<Sound> deathSounds = new ArrayList<>();
    private final Random random = new Random();
    private boolean muted;

    /** Charge toutes les phrases du pays (s'arrÃªte au premier numÃ©ro manquant). */
    public void loadCountry(String themeKey) {
        dispose();
        for (int n = 0; ; n++) {
            FileHandle file = Gdx.files.internal("assets/sounds/death_" + themeKey + "_" + n + ".ogg");
            if (!file.exists()) {
                break;
            }
            deathSounds.add(Gdx.audio.newSound(file));
        }
    }

    /** Touche M : coupe ou remet le son. */
    public void toggleMute() {
        muted = !muted;
        if (muted) {
            stopAll();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onEvent(GameEvent event) {
        if (event != GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED || muted || deathSounds.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        stopAll();
        deathSounds.get(random.nextInt(deathSounds.size())).play();
    }

    private void stopAll() {
        for (Sound sound : deathSounds) {
            sound.stop();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        for (Sound sound : deathSounds) {
            sound.dispose();
        }
        deathSounds.clear();
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TauntBubble.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.BitmapFont;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.GlyphLayout;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;

/** Bulle temporaire qui affiche la phrase moqueuse du pays courant. */
public final class TauntBubble implements Drawable {
    private static final float TOP = 64f;
    private static final float HEIGHT = 28f;
    private static final float PADDING_X = 14f;
    private static final float BORDER = 2f;

    private final Assets assets;
    private final GlyphLayout layout = new GlyphLayout();
    private ThemeStyle style;
    private float timeLeft;

    public TauntBubble(Assets assets) {
        if (assets == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Les ressources du jeu sont obligatoires.");
        }
        this.assets = assets;
    }

    public void show(ThemeStyle newStyle, float seconds) {
        if (newStyle == null || !Float.isFinite(seconds) || seconds < 0f) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Le style et une durÃ©e positive sont obligatoires.");
        }
        style = newStyle;
        timeLeft = seconds;
        layout.setText(assets.getFont(), style.taunt());
    }

    public void hide() {
        timeLeft = 0f;
    }

    public void update(float deltaTime) {
        timeLeft = remainingAfter(timeLeft, deltaTime);
    }

    boolean isVisible() {
        return style != null && timeLeft > 0f;
    }

    static float remainingAfter(float timeLeft, float deltaTime) {
        if (!Float.isFinite(timeLeft) || timeLeft < 0f
                || !Float.isFinite(deltaTime) || deltaTime < 0f) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("La durÃ©e et le pas de temps doivent Ãªtre finis et positifs.");
        }
        return Math.max(0f, timeLeft - deltaTime);
    }

    @Override
    public void draw(SpriteBatch batch) {
        if (!isVisible()) {
            return;
        }

        float width = layout.width + 2f * PADDING_X;
        float x = (GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH - width) / 2f;
        float y = GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT - TOP - HEIGHT;
        assets.fill(batch, style.accent(), x - BORDER, y - BORDER,
                width + 2f * BORDER, HEIGHT + 2f * BORDER);
        assets.fill(batch, style.hudBar(), x, y, width, HEIGHT);

        BitmapFont font = assets.getFont();
        font.setColor(Palette.WHITE);
        font.draw(batch, layout, x + PADDING_X, y + 20f);
        font.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\view\ThemeStyle.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;

/** Couleurs de niveau et texte de moquerie propres Ã  un pays. */
public record ThemeStyle(Color hudBar, Color accent, Color soft, Color halo, Color sky, String taunt) {
    public ThemeStyle {
        if (hudBar == null || accent == null || soft == null || halo == null || sky == null
                || taunt == null || taunt.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Un style de pays doit dÃ©finir ses couleurs et sa phrase.");
        }
    }

    public static ThemeStyle of(LevelTheme theme) {
        if (theme == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Le thÃ¨me ne peut pas Ãªtre nul.");
        }
        return switch (theme) {
            case TAHITI -> make("#0E3B3C", "#FF8A7E", "#CFEDEB", "#FFD166", "#8FDCDD",
                    "Ce palmier-lÃ  ? T'es sÃ»r ?");
            case CHINE -> make("#2A0E0E", "#F2C14E", "#F3D9C4", "#F2C14E", "#7A1616",
                    "Il sent bon, ce ramen, hein ?");
            case TUNISIE -> make("#3B1D12", "#8EC1EE", "#F3E0C8", "#D7261E", "#F7E3B8",
                    "Il pique pas, celui-lÃ . Promis.");
            case VIETNAM -> make("#1A2A0E", "#F2C14E", "#CFE3D6", "#C8322A", "#CFE3D6",
                    "Gauche, c'est Ã  droite. Ou l'inverse ?");
            case SRI_LANKA -> make("#2A1408", "#F2992E", "#F6C27A", "#F2992E", "#F6C27A",
                    "Le singe vise bien, hein ?");
            case TURQUIE -> make("#2A1A1A", "#3CB7B0", "#E8CDBB", "#E0B04A", "#F7D9B5",
                    "Regarde la mosaÃ¯que, pas tes pieds.");
            case FRANCE -> make("#2B2B33", "#E0A04A", "#EFE4C6", "#E0A04A", "#A9CBEA",
                    "Un croissant, Ã§a donne des ailes ?");
            case LAPONIE -> make("#1C2A4A", "#59E3A0", "#CBFAFC", "#F2C14E", "#2E4470",
                    "Ã‡a glisse un peu, non ?");
        };
    }

    private static ThemeStyle make(String bar, String accent, String soft, String halo,
                                   String sky, String taunt) {
        return new ThemeStyle(Color.valueOf(bar), Color.valueOf(accent), Color.valueOf(soft),
                Color.valueOf(halo), Color.valueOf(sky), taunt);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\main\java\com\teikinui\game\TripTrapGame.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game;

import com.badlogic.gdx.Game;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelManager;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameSession;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameTimer;
import com.teikinui.game.view.Assets;
import com.teikinui.game.view.Hud;
import com.teikinui.game.view.MessageScreen.Board;
import com.teikinui.game.view.MenuScreen;
import com.teikinui.game.view.MessageScreen;
import com.teikinui.game.view.PlayScreen;

/**
 * Point de dÃ©part du jeu. Regroupe toute la navigation entre les Ã©crans :
 * showMenu(), startGame(), showTimeUp(), showVictory().
 */
public class TripTrapGame extends Game {
    private SpriteBatch batch;
    private Assets assets;
    private GameSession session;
    private GameTimer timer;
    private PlayScreen playScreen;

    @Override
    public void create() {
        batch = new SpriteBatch();
        assets = new Assets();
        showMenu();
    }

    /** Le hall d'aÃ©roport. */
    public void showMenu() {
        disposePlayScreen();
        setScreen(new MenuScreen(this));
    }

    /** Une nouvelle partie : chrono Ã  3 minutes, 0 mort, premier pays. */
    public void startGame() {
        startGame(LevelTheme.values()[0]);
    }

    /** Une nouvelle partie qui commence dans le pays choisi au menu. */
    public void startGame(LevelTheme start) {
        disposePlayScreen();
        session = new GameSession();
        timer = new GameTimer();
        playScreen = new PlayScreen(this, session, timer, new LevelManager(start));
        setScreen(playScreen);
    }

    /** Le chrono est Ã  zÃ©ro : Ã©cran de fin avec le nombre de morts. */
    public void showTimeUp() {
        String details = "Les 3 minutes sont Ã©coulÃ©es. Tu es mort " + session.getDeathCount() + " fois.\n"
                + "Le voyage, ce n'est clairement pas pour toi.";
        setScreen(new MessageScreen(this, "TEMPS Ã‰COULÃ‰", details,
                Board.forResult(LevelTheme.values()[session.getCurrentLevel()],
                        session.getDeathCount(), 0f, false), this::startGame, this::showMenu));
    }

    /** Le dernier objectif est attrapÃ© : Ã©cran de victoire. */
    public void showVictory() {
        String details = "Tu as fini ton tour du monde !\n"
                + "Morts : " + session.getDeathCount()
                + "     Temps restant : " + Hud.formatTime(timer.getRemaining());
        setScreen(new MessageScreen(this, "BON VOYAGE !", details,
                Board.forResult(LevelTheme.values()[session.getCurrentLevel()],
                        session.getDeathCount(), timer.getRemaining(), true),
                this::startGame, this::showMenu));
    }

    private void disposePlayScreen() {
        if (playScreen != null) {
            playScreen.dispose();
            playScreen = null;
        }
    }

    public SpriteBatch getBatch() {
        return batch;
    }

    public Assets getAssets() {
        return assets;
    }

    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        super.dispose();
        if (getScreen() != null && getScreen() != playScreen) {
            getScreen().dispose();
        }
        disposePlayScreen();
        assets.dispose();
        batch.dispose();
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

/** Chaque pays doit remplir exactement l'Ã©cran : 30 x 17 cases, un dÃ©part, un objectif. */
class LevelFilesTest {
    private static Path assetFile(String name) {
        Path fromRoot = Path.of("assets", name);
        return Files.exists(fromRoot) ? fromRoot : Path.of("..", "assets", name);
    }

    @Test
    void everyCountryFillsTheWholeScreen() throws IOException {
        for (LevelTheme theme : LevelTheme.values()) {
            Path file = assetFile(theme.getFileName());
            assertTrue(Files.exists(file), "Fichier manquant : " + theme.getFileName());
            List<String> lines = Files.readString(file).strip().lines().toList();
            assertEquals(GameConfig.LEVEL_ROWS, lines.size(), theme + " : nombre de lignes");
            for (String line : lines) {
                assertEquals(GameConfig.LEVEL_COLS, line.length(), theme + " : largeur de ligne");
            }
            String all = String.join("", lines);
            assertEquals(1, all.chars().filter(c -> c == 'P').count(), theme + " : un seul dÃ©part P");
            assertEquals(1, all.chars().filter(c -> c == 'G').count(), theme + " : un seul objectif G");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void everyCountryLoadsWithItsTrapsAndGoal() throws IOException {
        for (LevelTheme theme : LevelTheme.values()) {
            Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(assetFile(theme.getFileName())));
            Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
            assertNotNull(level.createTraps(player));
            assertNotNull(level.createGoal());
        }
    }

     @Test
    void franceHidesASpikeAfterTheHoleAndAFakeGoalBeforeTheRealOne() throws IOException {
        List<String> lines = Files.readString(assetFile("france.txt")).strip().lines().toList();
        String floor = lines.get(13);
        assertTrue(floor.indexOf('i') > 17, "pic cachÃ© aprÃ¨s le grand trou");
        assertTrue(floor.indexOf('v') < floor.indexOf('G'), "faux objectif avant le vrai");
    }
}
