package com.teikinui.game.level;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;

class LevelManagerTest {
    @Test
    void theTripGoesFromTahitiToLapland() {
        LevelManager levels = new LevelManager();
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.TAHITI, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.CHINE, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.TUNISIE, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.VIETNAM, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.SRI_LANKA, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.TURQUIE, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.FRANCE, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.LAPONIE, levels.getCurrent());
        assertFalse(levels.isFinished());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertTrue(levels.isFinished());
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.LAPONIE, levels.getCurrent());
    }

    @Test
    void dyingDoesNotChangeTheCountry() {
        LevelManager levels = new LevelManager();
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.TIME_UP);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.TAHITI, levels.getCurrent());
        assertFalse(levels.isFinished());
    }

    @Test
    void theTripCanStartFromTheCountryChosenInTheMenu() {
        LevelManager levels = new LevelManager(LevelTheme.VIETNAM);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.VIETNAM, levels.getCurrent());
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED);
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.SRI_LANKA, levels.getCurrent());
        assertFalse(levels.isFinished());
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\level\LevelThemeGoalBoundsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Locale;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.model.ContactBounds;

class LevelThemeGoalBoundsTest {
    @Test
    void goalContactBoundsMatchTheirPngAlphaBounds() throws Exception {
        for (LevelTheme theme : LevelTheme.values()) {
            String themeKey = theme.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).replace("_", "");
            Path path = Path.of("assets/sprites/goal_" + themeKey + ".png");
            if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "sprites", "goal_" + themeKey + ".png");
            BufferedImage image = ImageIO.read(path.toFile());
            assertNotNull(image, path.toString());

            int left = image.getWidth();
            int top = image.getHeight();
            int right = -1;
            int bottom = -1;
            for (int y = 0; y < image.getHeight(); y++) {
                for (int x = 0; x < image.getWidth(); x++) {
                    if (((image.getRGB(x, y) >>> 24) & 255) == 0) continue;
                    left = Math.min(left, x);
                    top = Math.min(top, y);
                    right = Math.max(right, x + 1);
                    bottom = Math.max(bottom, y + 1);
                }
            }

            ContactBounds expected = ContactBounds.fromAlphaBounds(
                    image.getWidth(), image.getHeight(), left, top, right, bottom);
            assertEquals(expected, theme.getGoalContactBounds(), theme.name());
        }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\level\TahitiGroundSandTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import com.teikinui.game.model.CollisionSystem;
import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground.QuickSand;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground.ReturnDoor;

class TahitiGroundSandTest {
    @Test
    void sandIsAStandableGroundTileThatSlowsOnItsSurfaceAndMiddleDoorIsGone() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..").resolve(path);
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(path));
        int col = 14;
        assertEquals('q', level.getTrapLetter(col, 0));
        assertEquals((char) 0, level.getTrapLetter(col, 1));
        assertTrue(level.isWall(col, 0));
        assertEquals((char) 0, level.getTrapLetter(10, 7));
        Player player = new Player(col * Level.TILE, Level.TILE, 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        var traps = level.createTraps(player);
        assertEquals(6, traps.stream().filter(QuickSand.class::isInstance).count());
        assertEquals(0, traps.stream().filter(ReturnDoor.class::isInstance).count());
        var sand = traps.stream().filter(t -> t instanceof QuickSand
                && t.getX() == col * Level.TILE && t.getY() == 0).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        player.update(1f / 30f, false, false, false);
        new CollisionSystem().move(player, level, 1f / 30f);
        assertEquals(Level.TILE, player.getY(), 0.001f);
        sand.update(1f / 30f);
        sand.onPlayerContact(player, new EventBus());
        player.update(0f, false, true, false);
        assertEquals(GameConfig.RUN_SPEED / 2f, player.getVx(), 0.001f);
        player.teleport((col + 2) * Level.TILE, Level.TILE);
        sand.onPlayerContact(player, new EventBus());
        player.update(0f, false, true, false);
        assertEquals(GameConfig.RUN_SPEED, player.getVx(), 0.001f);
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\level\TahitiReturnWaveTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling.CocoFall;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.projectile.Starfish;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TahitiReturnWaveTest {
    private String map() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        return Files.readString(path);
    }

    @Test void fixedPlanRecreatesSameTypesPositionsAndFreshStatesAfterDeath() throws Exception {
        String text = map();
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(text);
        Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        var plan = TahitiTrapPlacement.plan(level, level.createTraps(player), new Random(42));
        List<Trap> first = TahitiTrapPlacement.instantiate(level, player, level.createTraps(player), plan);
        String positions = signature(first);
        first.get(first.size() - 1).update(0.2f);
        Level retry = LevelLoader.fromString(text);
        Player respawned = new Player(retry.getStartX(), retry.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> second = TahitiTrapPlacement.instantiate(retry, respawned, retry.createTraps(respawned), plan);
        assertEquals(positions, signature(second));
        assertNotSame(first.get(first.size()-1), second.get(second.size()-1));
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, second.get(second.size()-1).getState());
    }

    @Test void waveSpawnsOnlyOnceAfterGoalMovesAndRearmsOnNextAttempt() throws Exception {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(map());
        Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        int fixed = traps.size();
        assertEquals(fixed, traps.size());
        assertEquals(3, TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player).size());
        assertEquals(2, TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player).stream().filter(CocoFall.class::isInstance).count());
        assertEquals(1, TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player).stream().filter(Starfish.class::isInstance).count());
        for (Trap trap : TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player)) {
            int col = (int)(trap.getX()/Level.TILE), row = (int)(trap.getY()/Level.TILE);
            assertEquals(0, level.getTrapLetter(col,row));
            assertFalse(level.isWall(col,row));
            assertFalse(trap.collidesWith(player));
        }
        Level retry = LevelLoader.fromString(map());
        List<Trap> nextWave = TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(retry, player);
        assertEquals(signature(TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player)), signature(nextWave));
        assertNotSame(nextWave.get(0), TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player).get(0));
    }

    private String signature(List<Trap> traps) {
        return traps.stream().map(t -> t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ":" + t.getX() + ":" + t.getY()).toList().toString();
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** Aucun tirage alÃ©atoire de Tahiti ne doit poser un piÃ¨ge qui tue dÃ¨s l'apparition en P. */
class TahitiSpawnSafetyTest {

    private static String tahiti() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("../assets/tahiti.txt");
        return Files.readString(path);
    }

    @Test
    void safeZoneCoversTheFiveColumnsAfterTheStart() {
        assertTrue(TahitiTrapPlacement.isInStartSafeZone(2, 1, 1, 1));
        assertTrue(TahitiTrapPlacement.isInStartSafeZone(6, 1, 1, 1));
        assertFalse(TahitiTrapPlacement.isInStartSafeZone(7, 1, 1, 1));
        assertFalse(TahitiTrapPlacement.isInStartSafeZone(3, 2, 1, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void noRandomTrapIsPlacedNextToTheStartWhateverTheSeed() throws Exception {
        String map = tahiti();
        for (long seed = 0; seed < 2000; seed++) {
            Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(map);
            Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, 150);
            List<Trap> fixed = level.createTraps(player);
            int spawnCol = (int) (level.getStartX() / Level.TILE);
            int spawnRow = (int) (level.getStartY() / Level.TILE);
            for (TahitiTrapPlacement.Placement p : TahitiTrapPlacement.plan(level, fixed, new Random(seed))) {
                assertFalse(TahitiTrapPlacement.isInStartSafeZone(p.col(), p.row(), spawnCol, spawnRow),
                        "graine " + seed + " : piÃ¨ge " + p + " trop prÃ¨s du dÃ©part");
            }
        }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\level\TahitiTrapPlacementTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.level;

import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Tile;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling.CocoFall;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ground.QuickSand;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.projectile.Starfish;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TahitiTrapPlacementTest {
    private Level tahiti() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        return LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(path));
    }

    @Test void threeUniqueRandomTrapsAvoidSpawnGoalExistingTrapsAndReservedSpikes() throws Exception {
        Level level = tahiti();
        Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = TahitiTrapPlacement.addThree(level, player, level.createTraps(player), new Random(123));
        assertEquals(level.createTraps(player).size() + 3, traps.size());
        for (Trap trap : traps.subList(traps.size() - 3, traps.size())) {
            int col = (int) trap.getX() / GameConfig.TILE;
            int row = (int) trap.getY() / GameConfig.TILE;
            assertTrue(trap instanceof CocoFall || trap instanceof Starfish || trap instanceof QuickSand);
            assertEquals(Tile.EMPTY, level.getTile(col, row));
            assertEquals(0, level.getTrapLetter(col, row));
            assertFalse(col >= 25 && col <= 27 && row == 12);
            assertFalse(trap.getX() == level.getStartX() && trap.getY() == level.getStartY());
            assertFalse(trap.getX() == level.createGoal().getX()
                    && trap.getY() == level.createGoal().getY());
            assertFalse(col == 7 && row == 1 || col == 12 && row == 4 || col == 23 && row == 4);
            assertTrue(level.isWall(col, row - 1), "PiÃ¨ge sur case accessible au-dessus du sol");
        }
        assertEquals(3, traps.subList(traps.size() - 3, traps.size()).stream()
                .map(t -> (int)t.getX() + ":" + (int)t.getY()).distinct().count());
    }

    @Test void seedReproducesPlanAndDifferentNewGamesCanReroll() throws Exception {
        Level level = tahiti();
        Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        String first = signature(TahitiTrapPlacement.addThree(level, player, level.createTraps(player), new Random(1)));
        assertEquals(first, signature(TahitiTrapPlacement.addThree(level, player, level.createTraps(player), new Random(1))));
        assertNotEquals(first, signature(TahitiTrapPlacement.addThree(level, player, level.createTraps(player), new Random(2))));
        Random sessionRandom = new Random(42);
        String attemptZero = signature(TahitiTrapPlacement.addThree(tahiti(), player,
                tahiti().createTraps(player), sessionRandom));
        String attemptOne = signature(TahitiTrapPlacement.addThree(tahiti(), player,
                tahiti().createTraps(player), sessionRandom));
        assertNotEquals(attemptZero, attemptOne);
    }

    private String signature(List<Trap> traps) {
        return traps.subList(traps.size() - 3, traps.size()).stream()
                .map(t -> t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ":" + t.getX() + ":" + t.getY()).toList().toString();
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\CheckContactsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class CheckContactsTest {
    private static final class CountingThing implements Interactable {
        private int calls;

        @Override
        public void onPlayerContact(Player player, EventBus bus) {
            calls++;
        }
    }

    @Test
    void everyInteractableIsAskedOnce() {
        CountingThing first = new CountingThing();
        CountingThing second = new CountingThing();
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        new CollisionSystem().checkContacts(player, List.of(first, second), new EventBus());
        assertEquals(1, first.calls);
        assertEquals(1, second.calls);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\ChinaTrapsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.ChargingWall;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.HiddenSlab;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.SkyFall;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.TiltSlab;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class ChinaTrapsTest {
    private static final float DT = 1f / 60f;
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
    private final CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();

    ChinaTrapsTest() {
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
    }

    private static Trap first(List<Trap> traps, Class<?> type) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (type.isInstance(trap)) {
                return trap;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("piÃ¨ge absent : " + type.getSimpleName());
    }

    private void play(Level level, List<Trap> traps, Player player, boolean left, boolean right, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += DT) {
            player.update(DT, left, right, false);
            collisions.move(player, level, DT);
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                trap.update(DT);
            }
            collisions.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
        }
    }

    private static final String ROOM = String.join("\n",
            "##########",
            "#.......a#",
            "#........#",
            "#P.......#",
            "##########");

    @Test
    void standingStillWhileTheSkyFallsIsSafe() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(ROOM);
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        SkyFall sky = (SkyFall) first(traps, SkyFall.class);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 4.7f);
        assertTrue(sky.isDangerous());
        // Le ciel est descendu jusqu'Ã  la tÃªte du joueur.
        float head = player.getY() + player.getHeight();
        assertTrue(sky.getSkyBottom() >= head);
        assertTrue(sky.getSkyBottom() <= head + 3f);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 3f);
        assertFalse(sky.isDangerous());
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void aCountdownAnnouncesTheSkyAndMovingDuringItIsSafe() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(ROOM);
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        SkyFall sky = (SkyFall) first(traps, SkyFall.class);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 0.6f);
        assertTrue(sky.isWarning());
        assertEquals(3, sky.getCountdown());
        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 1.5f);
        assertTrue(sky.isWarning());
        assertEquals(2, sky.getCountdown());
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty(), "bouger pendant le dÃ©compte ne tue pas");
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1.6f);
        assertTrue(sky.isDangerous());
        assertEquals(0, sky.getCountdown(), "plus de dÃ©compte une fois le rideau tombÃ©");
    }

    @Test
    void movingWhileTheSkyFallsKills() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(ROOM);
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 3.7f);
        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 0.2f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void theWallChargesFromTheRightAndStopsBeforeTheFirstColumn() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "##############",
                "#............#",
                "#............#",
                "#P..........m#",
                "##############"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Trap wall = first(traps, ChargingWall.class);
        for (float t = 0; t < 1.8f; t += DT) {
            wall.update(DT);
        }
        assertTrue(wall.getX() < 3 * 32f);
        assertTrue(wall.getX() >= 2 * 32f);
        assertFalse(wall.collidesWith(player));
    }

    @Test
    void theHiddenSlabIsSolidAndAppearsWhenSteppedOn() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#####",
                "#...#",
                "#.n.#",
                "#e.e#",
                "#####"));
        Player player = new Player(2 * 32f, 3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        HiddenSlab slab = (HiddenSlab) first(traps, HiddenSlab.class);
        assertTrue(level.isWall(2, 2));
        assertFalse(slab.isRevealed());
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 0.3f);
        assertTrue(slab.isRevealed());
        assertTrue(player.isOnGround());
    }

    @Test
    void theFakeSlabLooksLikeFloorButYouFallThrough() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#####",
                "#...#",
                "#.P.#",
                "##x##",
                "##e##",
                "#####"));
        Player player = new Player(2 * 32f, 3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        assertFalse(level.isWall(2, 2));
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void theTiltSlabTipsOverAndDropsThePlayer() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "######",
                "#....#",
                "#.bb.#",
                "#.ee.#",
                "######"));
        Player player = new Player(2 * 32f, 3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        assertTrue(level.isWall(2, 2) && level.isWall(3, 2));
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1.5f);
        assertFalse(level.isWall(2, 2));
        assertFalse(level.isWall(3, 2));
        assertTrue(((TiltSlab) first(traps, TiltSlab.class)).getTilt() > 0f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\CroissantTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Croissant;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class CroissantTest {
    private static final float DT = 1f / 60f;

    @Test
    void aBoostedPlayerRunsFasterAndJumpsHigher() {
        Player normal = new Player(0f, 0f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        Player boosted = new Player(0f, 0f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        boosted.boost(Croissant.BOOST_TIME);

        normal.update(DT, false, true, true);
        boosted.update(DT, false, true, true);

        assertTrue(boosted.isBoosted());
        assertTrue(boosted.getVx() > normal.getVx());
        assertTrue(boosted.getVy() > normal.getVy());
    }

    @Test
    void theBoostStopsAfterAFewSeconds() {
        Player player = new Player(0f, 0f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        player.boost(Croissant.BOOST_TIME);
        for (float t = 0; t < Croissant.BOOST_TIME + 0.1f; t += DT) {
            player.update(DT, false, false, false);
        }
        assertFalse(player.isBoosted());
        player.update(DT, false, true, false);
        assertEquals(GameConfig.RUN_SPEED, player.getVx(), 0.01f);
    }

    @Test
    void jumpingIntoTheCroissantEatsItAndGivesTheBoost() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#####",
                "#...#",
                "#.g.#",
                "#...#",
                "#.P.#",
                "#####"));
        Player player = new Player(2 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Croissant croissant = (Croissant) traps.get(0);
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
        CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();
        assertFalse(croissant.isEaten());

        for (float t = 0; t < 0.6f; t += DT) {
            player.update(DT, false, false, t < 0.2f);
            collisions.move(player, level, DT);
            croissant.update(DT);
            collisions.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
        }

        assertTrue(croissant.isEaten());
        assertTrue(player.isBoosted());
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;

class FallAndWallTest {
    @Test
    void aWallCanBeAddedAndRemoved() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("#####\n#P..#\n#####");
        assertTrue(level.isWall(2, 0));
        level.setWall(2, 0, false);
        assertFalse(level.isWall(2, 0));
        level.setWall(2, 1, true);
        assertTrue(level.isWall(2, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void belowTheScreenIsEmptySoThePlayerCanFall() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("#####\n#P..#\n#####");
        assertFalse(level.isWall(2, -1));
        assertTrue(level.isWall(-1, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void fallingOutOfTheScreenKillsThePlayer() {
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
        CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();
        collisions.checkFall(new Player(32f, 10f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED), bus);
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
        collisions.checkFall(new Player(32f, -40f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED), bus);
        assertEquals(List.of(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED), received);
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class GameSessionTest {
    @Test
    void tracksDeathsAndCurrentLevel() {
        GameSession session = new GameSession();
        assertEquals(0, session.getDeathCount());
        assertEquals(0, session.getCurrentLevel());
        session.onEvent(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
        session.onEvent(GameEvent.TIME_UP);
        assertEquals(1, session.getDeathCount());
        session.setCurrentLevel(2);
        assertEquals(2, session.getCurrentLevel());
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> session.setCurrentLevel(-1));
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\GameTimerTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class GameTimerTest {
    @Test
    void countsDownToZeroWithoutGoingNegative() {
        GameTimer timer = new GameTimer();
        assertEquals(180f, timer.getRemaining());
        assertFalse(timer.isTimeUp());
        timer.update(1.5f);
        assertEquals(178.5f, timer.getRemaining());
        timer.update(500f);
        assertEquals(0f, timer.getRemaining());
        assertTrue(timer.isTimeUp());
    }

    @Test
    void resetPutsTheClockBackToThreeMinutes() {
        GameTimer timer = new GameTimer();
        timer.update(100f);
        timer.reset();
        assertEquals(180f, timer.getRemaining());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNegativeDelta() {
        GameTimer timer = new GameTimer();
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> timer.update(-1f));
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class GoalTest {
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();

    GoalTest() {
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
    }

    @Test
    void touchingTheGoalAnnouncesItOnlyOnce() {
        Goal goal = new Goal(64f, 32f);
        Player player = new Player(64f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        goal.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        goal.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertEquals(List.of(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED), received);
        assertTrue(goal.isReached());
    }

    @Test
    void aFarAwayPlayerDoesNotReachTheGoal() {
        Goal goal = new Goal(640f, 32f);
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        goal.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
        assertFalse(goal.isReached());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\JarTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class JarTest {
    private static final String MAP = String.join("\n",
            "#########",
            "#####j###",
            "#.......#",
            "#.......#",
            "#P......#",
            "#########");

    private Trap jarFor(Level level, Player player) {
        return level.createTraps(player).get(0);
    }

    private void play(Trap jar, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += 1f / 60f) {
            jar.update(1f / 60f);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void theJarIsPartOfTheCeilingAtTheStart() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(MAP);
        jarFor(level, new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED));
        assertTrue(level.isWall(5, 4));
    }

    @Test
    void theJarFallsFastWhenThePlayerIsRightNextToItAndBecomesAStep() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(MAP);
        Player player = new Player(3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        Trap jar = jarFor(level, player);
        play(jar, 0.5f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, jar.getState());

        player.teleport(4 * 32f - 10f, 32f);
        play(jar, 0.25f);
        assertFalse(level.isWall(5, 4));
        assertTrue(level.isWall(5, 1));
        assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, jar.getState());
    }

    @Test
    void theJarKillsThePlayerUnderIt() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(MAP);
        Player player = new Player(5 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        Trap jar = jarFor(level, player);
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 && received.isEmpty(); i++) {
            jar.update(1f / 60f);
            jar.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        }
        assertEquals(List.of(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED), received);
        assertFalse(level.isWall(5, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void afterADeathTheJarWaitsForThePlayerToComeCloser() {
        Player player = new Player(4 * 32f - 12f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        Level firstTry = LevelLoader.fromString(MAP);
        Trap jar = jarFor(firstTry, player);
        jar.update(1f / 60f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.TRIGGERED, jar.getState());

        Level secondTry = LevelLoader.fromString(MAP);
        secondTry.setAttempt(1);
        Trap learningJar = jarFor(secondTry, player);
        learningJar.update(1f / 60f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, learningJar.getState());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\LaplandTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Icicle;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Snowball;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class LaplandTest {
    private static final float DT = 1f / 60f;
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
    private final CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();

    LaplandTest() {
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
    }

    private static Trap first(List<Trap> traps, Class<?> type) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (type.isInstance(trap)) {
                return trap;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("piÃ¨ge absent : " + type.getSimpleName());
    }

    private void play(Level level, List<Trap> traps, Player player, boolean right, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += DT) {
            player.update(DT, false, right, false);
            collisions.move(player, level, DT);
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                trap.update(DT);
            }
            collisions.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void onIceThePlayerSpeedsUpSlowlyAndKeepsSliding() {
        Player player = new Player(0f, 0f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        player.setIcy(true);
        player.update(DT, false, true, false);
        assertTrue(player.getVx() > 0f);
        assertTrue(player.getVx() < GameConfig.RUN_SPEED / 2f);
        for (float t = 0; t < 2f; t += DT) {
            player.update(DT, false, true, false);
        }
        player.update(DT, false, false, false);
        assertTrue(player.getVx() > GameConfig.RUN_SPEED / 2f);
    }

    @Test
    void withoutIceThePlayerStopsAtOnce() {
        Player player = new Player(0f, 0f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        player.update(DT, false, true, false);
        player.update(DT, false, false, false);
        assertEquals(0f, player.getVx(), 0.001f);
    }

    @Test
    void onlyLaplandIsIcyAndItIsTheHardest() {
        for (LevelTheme theme : LevelTheme.values()) {
            assertEquals(theme == LevelTheme.LAPONIE, theme.isIcy(), theme.name());
            assertTrue(theme.getDifficulty() >= 1 && theme.getDifficulty() <= 3, theme.name());
        }
        assertEquals(3, LevelTheme.CHINE.getDifficulty());
        assertEquals(3, LevelTheme.LAPONIE.getDifficulty());
        assertEquals(1, LevelTheme.FRANCE.getDifficulty());
    }

    @Test
    void theIcicleFallsOnSomeoneBelowAndBreaksOnTheFloor() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#######",
                "#..I..#",
                "#.....#",
                "#.....#",
                "#..P..#",
                "#######"));
        Player player = new Player(3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Trap icicle = first(traps, Icicle.class);
        float hanging = icicle.getY();
        play(level, traps, player, false, 0.1f);
        assertTrue(icicle.getY() < hanging);
        play(level, traps, player, false, 1f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void theSnowballRollsTowardThePlayer() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "############",
                "#P........B#",
                "############"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Trap ball = first(traps, Snowball.class);
        float start = ball.getX();
        play(level, traps, player, false, 0.5f);
        assertTrue(ball.getX() < start);
        play(level, traps, player, false, 2f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
        assertFalse(ball.isActive());
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

class LevelGoalTest {
    @Test
    void theLetterGBecomesTheGoal() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("#####\n#P.G#\n#####");
        Goal goal = level.createGoal();
        assertEquals(3 * Level.TILE, goal.getX());
        assertEquals(Level.TILE, goal.getY());
        assertFalse(level.isWall(3, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void aLevelWithoutGoalIsRefused() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("#####\n#P..#\n#####");
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, level::createGoal);
    }
}

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\PlayerContractsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class PlayerContractsTest {
    private static final class FakeInput implements PlayerInput {
        boolean left;
        boolean right;
        boolean jump;

        @Override public boolean isLeft() { return left; }
        @Override public boolean isRight() { return right; }
        @Override public boolean isJump() { return jump; }
    }

    @Test
    void fakeKeyboardMovesAndJumpsOnlyFromGround() {
        Player player = new Player(32f, 0f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        FakeInput input = new FakeInput();
        input.right = true;
        input.jump = true;
        player.update(0.1f, input);
        assertEquals(GameConfig.RUN_SPEED, player.getVx());
        assertEquals(GameConfig.JUMP_SPEED + GameConfig.GRAVITY * 0.1f, player.getVy(), 0.001f);
        assertFalse(player.isOnGround());

        input.left = true;
        player.update(0.1f, input);
        assertEquals(0f, player.getVx());
        assertTrue(player.getVy() < GameConfig.JUMP_SPEED);
    }

    @Test
    void hitboxFollowsCollisionMovementAndEdgesDoNotOverlap() {
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        float contactOffsetX = 32f / 6f;
        assertEquals(32f + contactOffsetX, player.getHitbox().getX(), 0.001f);
        assertFalse(player.getHitbox().overlaps(new Hitbox(64f, 32f, 32f, 32f)));
        player.update(0.1f, false, true, false);
        Tile[][] grid = {{Tile.WALL, Tile.WALL, Tile.WALL, Tile.WALL},
                         {Tile.WALL, Tile.EMPTY, Tile.EMPTY, Tile.WALL},
                         {Tile.WALL, Tile.EMPTY, Tile.EMPTY, Tile.WALL}};
        new CollisionSystem().move(player, new Level(grid), 0.1f);
        assertEquals(player.getX() + contactOffsetX, player.getHitbox().getX(), 0.001f);
        assertEquals(player.getY(), player.getHitbox().getY());
        assertTrue(player.collidesWith(new Collidable() {
            @Override public Hitbox getHitbox() { return new Hitbox(player.getX(), player.getY(), 32f, 32f); }
        }));
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;

class ReversedInputTest {
    /** Faux clavier : on choisit Ã  la main les touches enfoncÃ©es. */
    private static PlayerInput keys(boolean left, boolean right, boolean jump) {
        return new PlayerInput() {
            public boolean isLeft() { return left; }
            public boolean isRight() { return right; }
            public boolean isJump() { return jump; }
        };
    }

    @Test
    void leftArrowMakesThePlayerGoRight() {
        PlayerInput reversed = new ReversedInput(keys(true, false, false));
        assertTrue(reversed.isRight());
        assertFalse(reversed.isLeft());
    }

    @Test
    void rightArrowMakesThePlayerGoLeftButJumpStaysJump() {
        PlayerInput reversed = new ReversedInput(keys(false, true, true));
        assertTrue(reversed.isLeft());
        assertFalse(reversed.isRight());
        assertTrue(reversed.isJump());
    }

    @Test
    void theReversedPlayerReallyMovesTheOtherWay() {
        Player player = new Player(100f, 0f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        player.update(1f / 60f, new ReversedInput(keys(false, true, false)));
        assertTrue(player.getVx() < 0f);
    }

    @Test
    void onlyVietnamSwapsTheArrows() {
        for (LevelTheme theme : LevelTheme.values()) {
            assertTrue(theme.hasInvertedControls() == (theme == LevelTheme.VIETNAM), theme.name());
        }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\SriLankaTrapsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Dune;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.FakeChili;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.InvisibleBlock;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Monkey;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class SriLankaTrapsTest {
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
    private final CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();

    SriLankaTrapsTest() {
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
    }

    private static Trap first(List<Trap> traps, Class<?> type) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (type.isInstance(trap)) {
                return trap;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("piÃ¨ge absent : " + type.getSimpleName());
    }

    /** Fait tourner le jeu : le joueur avance (ou pas), saute (ou pas), touche les piÃ¨ges. */
    private void play(Level level, List<Trap> traps, Player player,
                      boolean right, boolean jump, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += 1f / 60f) {
            player.update(1f / 60f, false, right, jump);
            collisions.move(player, level, 1f / 60f);
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                trap.update(1f / 60f);
            }
            collisions.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void theFakeChiliLooksLikeTheGoalButKills() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "########",
                "#P....v#",
                "########"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        assertTrue(first(traps, FakeChili.class).isVisible());

        play(level, traps, player, true, false, 3f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
        assertFalse(received.contains(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED));
    }

    @Test
    void theInvisibleBlockAppearsWhenTheHeadHitsIt() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#####",
                "#...#",
                "#...#",
                "#.u.#",
                "#.P.#",
                "#####"));
        Player player = new Player(2 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Trap block = first(traps, InvisibleBlock.class);
        assertFalse(block.isVisible());
        assertFalse(level.isWall(2, 2));

        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 0.5f);
        assertTrue(block.isVisible());
        assertTrue(level.isWall(2, 2));
        assertTrue(player.getY() < 2 * 32f);
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void theDuneCollapsesUnderTheFeetAndTakesItsNeighbours() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#######",
                "#.....#",
                "#.www.#",
                "#.eee.#",
                "#######"));
        Player player = new Player(2 * 32f, 3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        assertTrue(first(traps, Dune.class) != null);
        assertTrue(level.isWall(2, 2) && level.isWall(3, 2) && level.isWall(4, 2));

        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1f);
        assertFalse(level.isWall(2, 2));
        assertFalse(level.isWall(3, 2));
        assertFalse(level.isWall(4, 2));
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void theMonkeyThrowsAFruitAtSomeoneStandingBelow() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "########",
                "#.z....#",
                "#......#",
                "#......#",
                "#.....P#",
                "########"));
        Player player = new Player(6 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Monkey monkey = (Monkey) first(traps, Monkey.class);
        float handY = monkey.getY();

        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 0.1f);
        assertTrue(monkey.getY() < handY);

        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 2f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void theMonkeyIgnoresSomeoneFarAway() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "###########",
                "#z........#",
                "#.........#",
                "#........P#",
                "###########"));
        Player player = new Player(9 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Monkey monkey = (Monkey) first(traps, Monkey.class);
        float handY = monkey.getY();
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 2f);
        assertTrue(monkey.getY() == handY);
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\TahitiCoconutFloorsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling.CocoFall;

class TahitiCoconutFloorsTest {
    private Level tahiti() throws Exception {
        Path map = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(map)) map = Path.of("..").resolve(map);
        return LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(map));
    }

    @Test void secondFloorCoconutWaitsForPlayerOnSecondFloorAndStopsOnPlatform() throws Exception {
        Level level = tahiti();
        Player player = new Player(18 * Level.TILE, Level.TILE, 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        CocoFall coco = (CocoFall) level.createTraps(player).stream()
                .filter(t -> t instanceof CocoFall && t.getY() == 10 * Level.TILE).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        coco.update(1f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, coco.getState());
        player.teleport(18 * Level.TILE, 7 * Level.TILE);
        coco.update(1f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.TRIGGERED, coco.getState());
        assertEquals(7 * Level.TILE, coco.getY(), 0.001f);
        coco.update(10f);
        assertEquals(7 * Level.TILE, coco.getY(), 0.001f);
    }

    @Test void thirdFloorCoconutWaitsForPlayerOnThirdFloor() throws Exception {
        Level level = tahiti();
        Player player = new Player(8 * Level.TILE, 7 * Level.TILE, 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        CocoFall coco = (CocoFall) level.createTraps(player).stream()
                .filter(t -> t instanceof CocoFall && t.getY() == 14 * Level.TILE).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        coco.update(1f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, coco.getState());
        player.teleport(8 * Level.TILE, 12 * Level.TILE);
        coco.update(1f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.TRIGGERED, coco.getState());
        assertEquals(12 * Level.TILE, coco.getY(), 0.001f);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\TahitiTrapsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;

class TahitiTrapsTest {
    private Player player() { return new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED); }

    @Test void coconutFallsWhenPlayerApproachesAndKillsOnTouch() {
        Player player = player();
        Trap trap = TrapFactory.create('c', 32f, 128f, player);
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        GameSession session = new GameSession();
        bus.subscribe(session);
        trap.update(0.25f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.TRIGGERED, trap.getState());
        assertTrue(trap.getY() < 128f);
        trap.update(0.5f);
        trap.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertEquals(1, session.getDeathCount());
        assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, trap.getState());
        trap.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertEquals(1, session.getDeathCount());
    }

    @Test void starfishLaunchesHorizontallyAtPlayerAndKillsOnContact() {
        Player player = new Player(128f, 32f, 32f, 32f, 200f);
        Trap starfish = TrapFactory.create('s', 32f, 32f, player);
        starfish.update(0.25f);
        assertTrue(starfish.getX() > 32f);
        GameSession session = new GameSession();
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        bus.subscribe(session);
        starfish.update(0.5f);
        starfish.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertEquals(1, session.getDeathCount());
    }

    @Test void quicksandSlowsPlayerOnlyWhileTouching() {
        Player player = player();
        Trap sand = TrapFactory.create('q', 32f, 32f, player);
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        sand.update(0.1f);
        sand.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        player.update(0f, false, true, false);
        assertTrue(player.getVx() > 0 && player.getVx() < GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        player.update(0f, false, true, false);
        assertEquals(GameConfig.RUN_SPEED, player.getVx());
    }

    @Test void doorReturnsPlayerToMapStart() {
        Player player = player();
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("#####\n#P.d#\n#####");
        player.teleport(3 * Level.TILE, Level.TILE);
        Trap door = TrapFactory.create('d', 3 * Level.TILE, Level.TILE, player);
        door.setStart(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY());
        door.onPlayerContact(player, new EventBus());
        assertEquals(Level.TILE, player.getX());
        assertEquals(Level.TILE, player.getY());
        assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, door.getState());
    }

    @Test void sharkEmergesFromCloudWhenPlayerApproachesAndKillsOnContact() {
        Player player = player();
        Trap shark = TrapFactory.create('h', 32f, 128f, player);
        shark.update(0.25f);
        assertTrue(shark.getY() < 128f);
        GameSession session = new GameSession();
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        bus.subscribe(session);
        shark.update(0.5f);
        shark.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertEquals(1, session.getDeathCount());
    }

    @Test void fakeFloorTrapDoesNotHarmPlayer() {
        Player player = player();
        Trap fake = TrapFactory.create('f', 32f, 32f, player);
        GameSession session = new GameSession();
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        bus.subscribe(session);
        fake.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertEquals(0, session.getDeathCount());
        assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, fake.getState());
    }

    @Test void factoryAndLevelLoaderPreserveSixTrapLettersAndSpawnThem() {
        for (char letter : "csqdhf".toCharArray()) assertTrue(TrapFactory.isTrap(letter));
        assertFalse(TrapFactory.isTrap('.'));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> TrapFactory.create('?', 0, 0, player()));
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("#########\n#Pcsqdhf#\n#########");
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player());
        assertEquals(6, traps.size());
        assertEquals(2 * Level.TILE, traps.get(0).getX());
    }

    @Test void busNotifiesEverySubscriber() {
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
        bus.publish(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
        assertEquals(List.of(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED, GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED), received);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\TrapSpriteMetadataTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.TrapType;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;

class TrapSpriteMetadataTest {
    @Test
    void factoryCarriesEachSpriteNameWithoutDependingOnLibgdx() {
        Player player = new Player(32, 32, 32, 32, 100);
        for (TrapType type : TrapType.values()) {
            Trap trap = TrapFactory.create(type.getLetter(), 64, 64, player);
            assertEquals(type.getSpriteName(), trap.getSpriteName(), type.name());
            Path sprite = Path.of("assets/sprites", type.getSpriteName() + ".png");
            assertTrue(Files.isRegularFile(sprite) || Files.isRegularFile(Path.of("..").resolve(sprite)),
                    type.name());
        }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\TunisiaTrapsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Cactus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.DoorSlam;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Jar;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class TunisiaTrapsTest {
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();

    TunisiaTrapsTest() {
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
    }

    private static Player player(float col, float row) {
        return new Player(col * 32f, row * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
    }

    private static Trap first(List<Trap> traps, Class<?> type) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (type.isInstance(trap)) {
                return trap;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("piÃ¨ge absent : " + type.getSimpleName());
    }

    private void play(List<Trap> traps, Player player, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += 1f / 60f) {
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                trap.update(1f / 60f);
                trap.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void twoJarsSideBySideFallTogether() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "##########",
                "######jj##",
                "#........#",
                "#........#",
                "#P.......#",
                "##########"));
        Player beside = new Player(5 * 32f - 10f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(beside);
        play(traps, beside, 1f);
        assertTrue(level.isWall(6, 1));
        assertTrue(level.isWall(7, 1));
        assertFalse(level.isWall(6, 4));
        assertFalse(level.isWall(7, 4));
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void theShaftBlockWaitsUntilThePlayerHasLanded() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "######",
                "#P.###",
                "#oo###",
                "#....#",
                "#....#",
                "######"));
        Player falling = player(1, 3);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(falling);
        play(traps, falling, 0.5f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, first(traps, DoorSlam.class).getState());
    }

    @Test
    void theShaftBlockCrushesAPlayerWhoStaysBelow() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "######",
                "#P.###",
                "#oo###",
                "#....#",
                "#....#",
                "######"));
        Player player = player(1, 1);
        CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();
        player.update(1f / 60f, false, false, false);
        collisions.move(player, level, 1f / 60f);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        for (int i = 0; i < 120 && received.isEmpty(); i++) {
            player.update(1f / 60f, false, false, false);
            collisions.move(player, level, 1f / 60f);
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                trap.update(1f / 60f);
                trap.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
            }
        }
        assertEquals(List.of(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED), received);
    }

    @Test
    void theCactusIsHiddenUntilThePlayerComesNear() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("##########\n#P.....k.#\n##########");
        Player far = player(1, 1);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(far);
        Trap cactus = first(traps, Cactus.class);
        play(traps, far, 0.2f);
        assertFalse(cactus.isVisible());

        Player near = player(6, 1);
        List<Trap> nearTraps = level.createTraps(near);
        play(nearTraps, near, 0.1f);
        assertTrue(first(nearTraps, Cactus.class).isVisible());
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());

        Player onIt = player(7, 1);
        List<Trap> onTraps = level.createTraps(onIt);
        play(onTraps, onIt, 0.1f);
        assertEquals(List.of(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED), received);
    }

    @Test
    void jumpingHighOverTheCactusIsSafe() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("##########\n#........#\n#........#\n#P.....k.#\n##########");
        Player above = player(7, 3);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(above);
        play(traps, above, 0.2f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, first(traps, Cactus.class).getState());
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void jarsAreFallingBlocks() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("#####\n##j##\n#P..#\n#####");
        assertTrue(first(level.createTraps(player(1, 1)), Jar.class)
                instanceof com.teikinui.game.model.traps.FallingBlock);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\TurkeyTrapsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Dervish;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.SimitVendor;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class TurkeyTrapsTest {
    private static final float DT = 1f / 60f;
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
    private final CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();

    TurkeyTrapsTest() {
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
    }

    private static Trap first(List<Trap> traps, Class<?> type) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (type.isInstance(trap)) {
                return trap;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("piÃ¨ge absent : " + type.getSimpleName());
    }

    private void play(Level level, List<Trap> traps, Player player, boolean right, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += DT) {
            player.update(DT, false, right, false);
            collisions.move(player, level, DT);
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                trap.update(DT);
            }
            collisions.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void theDervishGoesBackAndForth() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "################",
                "#P......D......#",
                "################"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Trap dervish = first(traps, Dervish.class);
        float start = dervish.getX();
        float min = start;
        float max = start;
        for (float t = 0; t < 6f; t += DT) {
            dervish.update(DT);
            min = Math.min(min, dervish.getX());
            max = Math.max(max, dervish.getX());
        }
        assertTrue(max > start + 2 * 32f);
        assertTrue(min < start - 2 * 32f);
    }

    @Test
    void touchingTheDervishKills() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "##########",
                "#P...D...#",
                "##########"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        play(level, traps, player, true, 3f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void loukoumHalvesTheSpeedButDoesNotKill() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "########",
                "#PLLLLL#",
                "########"));
        Player player = new Player(2 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        play(level, traps, player, true, 0.2f);
        assertTrue(player.getVx() < GameConfig.RUN_SPEED * 0.6f);
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void theSimitVendorThrowsAtThePlayerUntilHeIsHit() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "############",
                "#P........S#",
                "############"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        SimitVendor vendor = (SimitVendor) first(traps, SimitVendor.class);
        float trayX = vendor.getX();

        play(level, traps, player, false, 0.7f);
        assertTrue(vendor.getX() < trayX);
        assertFalse(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));

        play(level, traps, player, false, 2f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\model\VietnamTrapsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.CrumbleBlock;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.FallingBlock;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.GrowingSpike;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.PopSpike;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class VietnamTrapsTest {
    private final EventBus bus = new EventBus();
    private final List<GameEvent> received = new ArrayList<>();
    private final CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();

    VietnamTrapsTest() {
        bus.subscribe(received::add);
    }

    private static Trap first(List<Trap> traps, Class<?> type) {
        for (Trap trap : traps) {
            if (type.isInstance(trap)) {
                return trap;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("piÃ¨ge absent : " + type.getSimpleName());
    }

    /** Fait tourner le jeu : le joueur avance (ou pas), tombe, touche les piÃ¨ges. */
    private void play(Level level, List<Trap> traps, Player player, boolean left, boolean right, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += 1f / 60f) {
            player.update(1f / 60f, left, right, false);
            collisions.move(player, level, 1f / 60f);
            for (Trap trap : traps) {
                trap.update(1f / 60f);
            }
            collisions.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void theSpikeFloorKillsWhoeverFallsOnIt() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#####",
                "#P..#",
                "#...#",
                "#eee#",
                "#####"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void theCrumbleBlockLooksLikeAWallThenDropsIntoTheSpikes() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#######",
                "#.....#",
                "#..t..#",
                "#.....#",
                "#eeeee#",
                "#######"));
        Player player = new Player(3 * 32f, 4 * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Trap block = first(traps, CrumbleBlock.class);
        assertTrue(level.isWall(3, 3));
        assertFalse(block.isVisible());

        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 0.2f);
        assertTrue(player.isOnGround());
        assertTrue(level.isWall(3, 3));

        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1f);
        assertFalse(level.isWall(3, 3));
        assertFalse(level.isWall(3, 1));
        assertTrue(block instanceof FallingBlock);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void leavingTheCrumbleBlockQuicklyIsSafe() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#######",
                "#.....#",
                "#..t###",
                "#....##",
                "#eeee##",
                "#######"));
        Player player = new Player(3 * 32f, 4 * 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 0.05f);
        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 0.3f);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1f);
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());
        assertFalse(level.isWall(3, 3));
    }

    @Test
    void thePopSpikeIsHiddenUntilThePlayerComesClose() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "##########",
                "#........#",
                "#P.....i.#",
                "##########"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        Trap spike = first(traps, PopSpike.class);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 0.2f);
        assertFalse(spike.isVisible());

        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 1f);
        assertTrue(spike.isVisible());
        assertTrue(received.isEmpty());

        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 1f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void aCeilingPillarPoundsDownToTheFloorThenGoesBackUp() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "##########",
                "#....y...#",
                "#........#",
                "#........#",
                "#........#",
                "#P.......#",
                "##########"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        GrowingSpike pillar = (GrowingSpike) first(traps, GrowingSpike.class);
        assertTrue(pillar.isFromCeiling());

        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 0.6f);
        float lowest = 0f;
        boolean wentBackUp = false;
        boolean cameDownAgain = false;
        for (int frame = 0; frame < 4 * 60; frame++) {
            play(level, traps, player, false, false, 1f / 60f);
            lowest = Math.max(lowest, pillar.getHeight());
            if (lowest >= 5 * 32f && pillar.getHeight() == 0f) {
                wentBackUp = true;
            }
            if (wentBackUp && pillar.getHeight() > 0f) {
                cameDownAgain = true;
            }
        }
        assertEquals(5 * 32f, lowest, 0.01f);
        assertTrue(wentBackUp);
        assertTrue(cameDownAgain);
    }

    @Test
    void aCeilingPillarCrushesSomeoneStandingUnderIt() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "#######",
                "#..y..#",
                "#.....#",
                "#..P..#",
                "#######"));
        Player player = new Player(3 * 32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        play(level, traps, player, false, false, 0.5f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void aFloorPillarImpalesSomeoneWhoKeepsRunning() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "##########",
                "#........#",
                "#........#",
                "#........#",
                "#P....y..#",
                "##########"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        GrowingSpike pillar = (GrowingSpike) first(traps, GrowingSpike.class);
        assertFalse(pillar.isFromCeiling());
        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 2f);
        assertTrue(received.contains(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED));
    }

    @Test
    void aPillarIgnoresThePlayerOnTheFloorBelow() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(String.join("\n",
                "########",
                "#......#",
                "#......#",
                "#...y..#",
                "########",
                "#......#",
                "#P.....#",
                "########"));
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        play(level, traps, player, false, true, 2f);
        assertFalse(first(traps, GrowingSpike.class).isVisible());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\DeathRestartTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelManager;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameSession;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class DeathRestartTest {
    @Test
    void deathRestartsSameLevelWithoutEndingGameAndPreservesDeathCount() {
        GameSession session = new GameSession();
        LevelManager levels = new LevelManager();
        session.setCurrentLevel(levels.getCurrent().ordinal());
        session.onEvent(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
        levels.onEvent(GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED);
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.shouldRestartAfterDeath(true, false));
        assertFalse(levels.isFinished());
        assertEquals(levels.getCurrent().ordinal(), session.getCurrentLevel());
        assertEquals(1, session.getDeathCount());
    }

    @Test
    void goalHasPriorityOverDeath() {
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.shouldRestartAfterDeath(true, true));
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.shouldRestartAfterDeath(false, false));
    }

    @Test
    void deathAnimationCompletesBeforeRespawnUnlessNoSpriteExists() {
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.shouldRespawnAfterDeath(true, false, 0.4f, 0.2f));
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.shouldRespawnAfterDeath(true, false, 0.4f, 0.4f));
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.shouldRespawnAfterDeath(true, false, 0f, 0f));
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.shouldRespawnAfterDeath(true, true, 0.4f, 1f));
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.shouldRespawnAfterDeath(false, false, 0f, 1f));
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;

class HudFlagTest {
    @Test
    void flagSpriteNameFollowsTheCountryKey() {
        assertEquals("flag_srilanka", Hud.flagSprite(LevelTheme.SRI_LANKA));
        assertEquals("flag_france", Hud.flagSprite(LevelTheme.FRANCE));
    }

    @Test
    void everyCountryHasASmallFlagThatFitsInTheHudBar() throws Exception {
        for (LevelTheme theme : LevelTheme.values()) {
            Path path = Path.of("assets/sprites/" + Hud.flagSprite(theme) + ".png");
            if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "sprites", Hud.flagSprite(theme) + ".png");
            assertTrue(Files.exists(path), path.toString());
            BufferedImage image = ImageIO.read(path.toFile());
            assertEquals(24, image.getWidth(), theme.name());
            assertEquals(16, image.getHeight(), theme.name());
        }
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;

class LevelSpriteIntegrationTest {
    @Test
    void fakeChiliUsesExactlyTheGoalSpriteWhenAvailable() {
        assertEquals("goal_srilanka", PlayScreen.fakeGoalSprite(LevelTheme.SRI_LANKA, true));
        assertNull(PlayScreen.fakeGoalSprite(LevelTheme.SRI_LANKA, false));
        assertEquals("goal_tahiti", PlayScreen.fakeGoalSprite(LevelTheme.TAHITI, true));
        assertEquals("goal_chine", PlayScreen.fakeGoalSprite(LevelTheme.CHINE, true),
                "En Chine, le faux objectif est le bol de nouilles (la vraie sortie reste en pics)");
        assertEquals("goal_turquie", PlayScreen.fakeGoalSprite(LevelTheme.TURQUIE, true));
    }

    @Test
    void fakeBowlInChinaTouchesLikeTheBowlDrawing() {
        assertEquals(LevelTheme.CHINE.getGoalContactBounds(),
                PlayScreen.fakeGoalContactBounds(LevelTheme.CHINE, true));
        assertEquals(PlayScreen.goalContactBounds(LevelTheme.TURQUIE, true),
                PlayScreen.fakeGoalContactBounds(LevelTheme.TURQUIE, true));
    }

    @Test
    void hiddenSlabCopiesTheRealBackgroundInsteadOfFlatSkyWhenPresent() {
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.maskHiddenSlabWithBackground(LevelTheme.LAPONIE, true));
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.maskHiddenSlabWithBackground(LevelTheme.LAPONIE, false));
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.maskHiddenSlabWithBackground(LevelTheme.TAHITI, true));
    }
}
