========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\MenuScreenLayoutTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class MenuScreenLayoutTest {
    @Test
    void taglineLeavesTheRequiredGapAboveTheActionButton() {
        float taglineBottom = MenuScreen.taglineTopFromTop() + MenuScreen.TAGLINE_BLOCK_HEIGHT;
        float latestAllowedBottom = MenuScreen.MENU_BUTTON_TOP_FROM_TOP - MenuScreen.TAGLINE_BUTTON_GAP;

        assertTrue(taglineBottom <= latestAllowedBottom);
        assertEquals(214f, MenuScreen.taglineTopFromTop());
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;

class MessageScreenBoardTest {
    @Test
    void timeoutTicketShowsCurrentCountryAndCompletedCountries() {
        var ticket = MessageScreen.Board.forResult(LevelTheme.CHINE, 5, 0f, false);
        assertEquals("TT 02", ticket.flight());
        assertEquals("CHINE", ticket.destination());
        assertEquals("1 SUR 8", ticket.completed());
        assertEquals("00:00", ticket.clock());
        assertEquals("B2", ticket.gate());
        assertEquals("T'es mort 5 fois.", ticket.deathsLabel());
        assertFalse(ticket.victory());
    }

    @Test
    void endScreenKeysRestartOrReturnToMenuOnlyOnce() {
        assertEquals(MessageScreen.Action.RESTART, MessageScreen.actionFor(false, true, false, false));
        assertEquals(MessageScreen.Action.MENU, MessageScreen.actionFor(false, false, true, false));
        assertEquals(MessageScreen.Action.MENU, MessageScreen.actionFor(false, false, false, true));
        assertEquals(MessageScreen.Action.RESTART, MessageScreen.actionFor(false, true, true, true));
        assertEquals(MessageScreen.Action.NONE, MessageScreen.actionFor(false, false, false, false));
        assertEquals(MessageScreen.Action.NONE, MessageScreen.actionFor(true, true, true, true));
    }

    @Test
    void victoryTicketCompletesAllCountriesAndKeepsRemainingTime() {
        var ticket = MessageScreen.Board.forResult(LevelTheme.LAPONIE, 2, 61f, true);
        assertEquals("8 SUR 8", ticket.completed());
        assertEquals("01:01", ticket.clock());
        assertEquals("H8", ticket.gate());
        assertTrue(ticket.victory());
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -> MessageScreen.Board.forResult(null, 0, 0f, false));
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\PlayerDrawBoundsTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Hitbox;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;

class PlayerDrawBoundsTest {
    @Test
    void spriteIsCenteredAndMirroredAroundTheCollisionBox() {
        Player player = new Player(96, 64, 32, 32, 100);
        var bounds = PlayScreen.playerDrawBounds(player);
        assertEquals(80f, bounds.x());
        assertEquals(52f, bounds.y());
        assertEquals(64f, bounds.size());
        assertEquals(112f, bounds.x() + bounds.size() / 2f);
        assertEquals(144f, bounds.x() + bounds.size());
    }

    @Test
    void existingFramesAreSquareAndOpaqueFeetStayNearHitboxFloor() throws Exception {
        Player player = new Player(96, 64, 32, 32, 100);
        var bounds = PlayScreen.playerDrawBounds(player);
        for (String action : new String[] {"idle", "walk", "jump"}) {
            int count = 0;
            for (int i = 0; ; i++) {
                Path path = Path.of("assets/sprites/player_" + action + "_" + i + ".png");
                if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..").resolve(path);
                if (!Files.exists(path)) break;
                BufferedImage image = ImageIO.read(path.toFile());
                assertEquals(image.getWidth(), image.getHeight(), path.toString());
                int bottom = -1;
                for (int y = 0; y < image.getHeight(); y++) {
                    for (int x = 0; x < image.getWidth(); x++) {
                        if (((image.getRGB(x, y) >>> 24) & 255) > 127) bottom = Math.max(bottom, y);
                    }
                }
                assertTrue(bottom >= 0, path.toString());
                float feetY = bounds.y() + (image.getHeight() - 1 - bottom) * bounds.size() / image.getHeight();
                assertTrue(Math.abs(feetY - player.getY()) <= 12f, path + " foot=" + feetY);
                count++;
            }
            assertTrue(count > 0, "Missing " + action + " frames");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void playerContactBoundsMatchTheOpaqueEnvelopeOfAllAnimationFrames() throws Exception {
        Player player = new Player(96f, 64f, 32f, 32f, 100f);
        PlayScreen.PlayerDrawBounds drawBounds = PlayScreen.playerDrawBounds(player);
        int sourceWidth = -1;
        int sourceHeight = -1;
        int left = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        int top = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        int right = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        int bottom = Integer.MIN_VALUE;

        for (String action : new String[] {"idle", "walk", "jump"}) {
            for (int index = 0; ; index++) {
                Path path = Path.of("assets/sprites/player_" + action + "_" + index + ".png");
                if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..").resolve(path);
                if (!Files.exists(path)) break;
                BufferedImage image = ImageIO.read(path.toFile());
                sourceWidth = image.getWidth();
                sourceHeight = image.getHeight();
                for (int y = 0; y < image.getHeight(); y++) {
                    for (int x = 0; x < image.getWidth(); x++) {
                        if (((image.getRGB(x, y) >>> 24) & 255) == 0) continue;
                        left = Math.min(left, x);
                        top = Math.min(top, y);
                        right = Math.max(right, x + 1);
                        bottom = Math.max(bottom, y + 1);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Hitbox contact = player.getHitbox();
        float expectedX = drawBounds.x() + drawBounds.size() * left / sourceWidth;
        float expectedY = drawBounds.y() + drawBounds.size() * (sourceHeight - bottom) / sourceHeight;
        float expectedWidth = drawBounds.size() * (right - left) / sourceWidth;
        float expectedHeight = drawBounds.size() * (bottom - top) / sourceHeight;
        assertEquals(expectedX, contact.getX(), 0.001f);
        assertEquals(expectedY, contact.getY(), 0.001f);
        assertEquals(expectedWidth, contact.getWidth(), 0.001f);
        assertEquals(expectedHeight, contact.getHeight(), 0.001f);
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\PlayerSpritesTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertSame;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.Animation;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.TextureRegion;

class PlayerSpritesTest {
    @Test
    void countryAnimationTakesPrecedenceOverTheBaseAnimation() {
        Animation<TextureRegion> country = animation();
        Animation<TextureRegion> base = animation();

        assertSame(country, PlayerSprites.preferCountryAnimation(country, base));
    }

    @Test
    void baseAnimationIsUsedWhenCountryAnimationIsMissing() {
        Animation<TextureRegion> base = animation();

        assertSame(base, PlayerSprites.preferCountryAnimation(null, base));
        assertNull(PlayerSprites.preferCountryAnimation(null, null));
    }

    @Test
    void animationStateFollowsDeathAirMovementAndIdlePriority() {
        assertEquals(PlayerSprites.State.DIE, PlayerSprites.stateFor(150f, false, true));
        assertEquals(PlayerSprites.State.JUMP, PlayerSprites.stateFor(0f, false, false));
        assertEquals(PlayerSprites.State.WALK, PlayerSprites.stateFor(-150f, true, false));
        assertEquals(PlayerSprites.State.IDLE, PlayerSprites.stateFor(0f, true, false));
    }

    private static Animation<TextureRegion> animation() {
        return new Animation<>(0.1f, new TextureRegion());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\SkyCountdownTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.util.List;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.SkyFall;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;

class SkyCountdownTest {
    private static void wait(SkyFall sky, float seconds) {
        for (float t = 0; t < seconds; t += 1f / 60f) {
            sky.update(1f / 60f);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void thePanelShowsOnlyBeforeTheCurtainFalls() {
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString("##########\n#.......a#\n#P.......#\n##########");
        Player player = new Player(32f, 32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = level.createTraps(player);
        SkyFall sky = PlayScreen.findSky(traps);
        wait(sky, 1f);
        assertTrue(SkyCountdown.shows(sky));
        wait(sky, 3f);
        assertFalse(SkyCountdown.shows(sky));
        assertFalse(SkyCountdown.shows(null));
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.TrapType;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;

class SpriteCollisionAlignmentTest {
    @Test
    void playerDoesNotHitCoconutBeforeTheirVisiblePixelsTouch() {
        Player player = new Player(4f, 32f, 32f, 32f, 100f);
        Trap coconut = TrapFactory.create(TrapType.COCONUT.getLetter(), 32f, 32f, player);

        assertFalse(player.collidesWith(coconut),
                "The player and coconut PNGs are still separated at this position.");

        player.teleport(7f, 32f);
        assertTrue(player.collidesWith(coconut),
                "The contact hitboxes should overlap once the visible sprites touch.");
    }

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TahitiActualDeathRespawnTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Proxy;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Files;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Input;
import com.badlogic.gdx.files.FileHandle;
import com.teikinui.game.TripTrapGame;
import com.teikinui.game.app.InputHandler;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelManager;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.CollisionSystem;
import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameSession;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameTimer;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Goal;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling.CocoFall;
import sun.misc.Unsafe;

/** Uses the real PlayScreen.update -> contacts -> death -> loadLevel pipeline, without an OpenGL window. */
class TahitiActualDeathRespawnTest {
    private static final Unsafe UNSAFE = unsafe();

    @Test
    void centralMapCoconutDeathReloadsFixedTrapsAndKeepsReturnWaveDormantUntilGoalMoves() throws Exception {
        Files originalFiles = Gdx.files;
        Input originalInput = Gdx.input;
        try {
            Gdx.files = (Files) Proxy.newProxyInstance(Files.class.getClassLoader(), new Class<?>[] {Files.class},
                    (proxy, method, args) -> {
                        if (method.getName().equals("internal")) {
                            String name = (String) args[0];
                            Path path = Path.of(name);
                            if (!path.toFile().exists()) path = Path.of("..").resolve(path);
                            return new FileHandle(path.toFile());
                        }
                        return null;
                    });
            Gdx.input = (Input) Proxy.newProxyInstance(Input.class.getClassLoader(), new Class<?>[] {Input.class},
                    (proxy, method, args) -> method.getReturnType() == boolean.class ? false : null);

            Assets assets = (Assets) UNSAFE.allocateInstance(Assets.class);
            TripTrapGame game = (TripTrapGame) UNSAFE.allocateInstance(TripTrapGame.class);
            set(game, "assets", assets);
            PlayScreen screen = (PlayScreen) UNSAFE.allocateInstance(PlayScreen.class);
            set(screen, "game", game);
            set(screen, "theme", LevelTheme.TAHITI); // no GL/font/audio initialization in this test harness
            set(screen, "attempt", -1); // loadLevel() starts at attempt zero despite pre-set theme
            set(screen, "style", ThemeStyle.of(LevelTheme.TAHITI));
            set(screen, "themeKey", "tahiti");
            set(screen, "playerSprites", UNSAFE.allocateInstance(PlayerSprites.class));
            set(screen, "taunt", UNSAFE.allocateInstance(TauntBubble.class));
            set(screen, "skyCountdown", UNSAFE.allocateInstance(SkyCountdown.class));
            set(screen, "levels", new LevelManager(LevelTheme.TAHITI));
            set(screen, "session", new GameSession());
            set(screen, "timer", new GameTimer());
            set(screen, "keyboard", new InputHandler());
            set(screen, "collisionSystem", new CollisionSystem());
            EventBus bus = new EventBus();
            set(screen, "bus", bus);
            bus.subscribe((GameEvent event) -> {
                if (event == GameEvent.PLAYER_DIED) {
                    try { set(screen, "died", true); } catch (Exception e) { throw new RuntimeException(e); }
                }
            });
            bus.subscribe((GameSession) field(screen, "session"));

            invokeLoad(screen);
            Level firstLevel = (Level) field(screen, "level");
            Player firstPlayer = (Player) field(screen, "player");
            Goal firstGoal = (Goal) field(screen, "goal");
            List<Trap> before = traps(screen);
            assertEquals(firstLevel.createTraps(firstPlayer).size(), before.size(),
                    "Tahiti ne doit plus gÃ©nÃ©rer trois piÃ¨ges alÃ©atoires");
            List<float[]> startPositions = before.stream().map(t -> new float[] {t.getX(), t.getY()}).toList();
            CocoFall central = before.stream().filter(CocoFall.class::isInstance)
                    .map(CocoFall.class::cast).filter(t -> t.getX() == 18 * Level.TILE && t.getY() == 10 * Level.TILE)
                    .findFirst().orElseThrow();
            firstPlayer.teleport(central.getX(), 7 * Level.TILE); // fixed floor below the central coconut
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && field(screen, "level") == firstLevel; i++) screen.update(1f / 30f);
            assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, central.getState(), "Central map coconut must kill via contacts");
            // No die PNG in this headless harness: update() itself calls loadLevel in the death frame.
            assertNotSame(firstLevel, field(screen, "level"));
            assertNotSame(firstPlayer, field(screen, "player"));
            assertNotSame(firstGoal, field(screen, "goal"));
            assertTrue(((Level) field(screen, "level")).getAttempt() >= 1);
            List<Trap> after = traps(screen);
            assertEquals(before.size(), after.size());
            for (int i = 0; i < before.size(); i++) {
                assertNotSame(before.get(i), after.get(i));
                assertEquals(startPositions.get(i)[0], after.get(i).getX(), "fixed/extra trap X " + i);
                assertEquals(startPositions.get(i)[1], after.get(i).getY(), "fixed/extra trap Y " + i);
            }
            assertTrue(after.stream().anyMatch(t -> t instanceof CocoFall &&
                    t.getX() == 18 * Level.TILE && t.getY() == 10 * Level.TILE && t.getState() == Trap.State.WAITING));
            List<Trap> wave = (List<Trap>) field(screen, "tahitiReturnWave");
            assertEquals(3, wave.size());
            assertTrue(after.stream().noneMatch(wave::contains));
            Level reloaded = (Level) field(screen, "level");
            screen.update(1f / 30f); // still at P: wave must not move or render before G -> P
            assertSame(reloaded, field(screen, "level"), "No second respawn may hide the premature wave");
            assertTrue(traps(screen).stream().noneMatch(wave::contains));
            assertTrue(wave.stream().allMatch(t -> t.getState() == Trap.State.WAITING));
            ((Player) field(screen, "player")).teleport(((Goal) field(screen, "goal")).getX()
                    - 2 * Level.TILE, ((Goal) field(screen, "goal")).getY());
            screen.update(1f / 30f);
            assertTrue(((Goal) field(screen, "goal")).hasMovedToStart());
            assertTrue(traps(screen).containsAll(wave));
            screen.update(1f / 30f);
            assertEquals(3, traps(screen).stream().filter(wave::contains).count());
        } finally {
            Gdx.files = originalFiles;
            Gdx.input = originalInput;
        }
    }

    private static List<Trap> traps(PlayScreen screen) throws Exception { return (List<Trap>) field(screen, "traps"); }
    private static void invokeLoad(PlayScreen screen) throws Exception {
        var method = PlayScreen.class.getDeclaredMethod("loadLevel");
        method.setAccessible(true);
        method.invoke(screen);
    }
    private static Object field(Object target, String name) throws Exception {
        Field f = findField(target.getClass(), name);
        f.setAccessible(true);
        return f.get(target);
    }
    private static void set(Object target, String name, Object value) throws Exception {
        Field f = findField(target.getClass(), name);
        f.setAccessible(true);
        f.set(target, value);
    }
    private static Field findField(Class<?> type, String name) throws Exception {
        for (Class<?> current = type; current != null; current = current.getSuperclass()) {
            try { return current.getDeclaredField(name); } catch (NoSuchFieldException ignored) { }
        }
        throw new NoSuchFieldException(name);
    }
    private static Unsafe unsafe() {
        try {
            Field f = Unsafe.class.getDeclaredField("theUnsafe");
            f.setAccessible(true);
            return (Unsafe) f.get(null);
        } catch (Exception e) { throw new ExceptionInInitializerError(e); }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TahitiAmbushTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Goal;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.projectile.Starfish;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TahitiAmbushTest {
    @Test void goalMovesWithoutAddingSpikesAndFixedStarfishRemains() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(path));
        Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        Goal goal = level.createGoal();
        List<Trap> traps = new ArrayList<>(level.createTraps(player));
        int fixedCount = traps.size();
        long starfishCount = traps.stream().filter(Starfish.class::isInstance).count();
        float originalGoalX = goal.getX();
        player.teleport(originalGoalX - 2 * GameConfig.TILE, goal.getY());
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal,
                level.getStartX(), level.getStartY()));
        assertEquals(level.getStartX(), goal.getX());
        assertEquals(fixedCount, traps.size(), "La transition ne crÃ©e aucun nouveau piÃ¨ge");
        assertEquals(starfishCount, traps.stream().filter(Starfish.class::isInstance).count());
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal,
                level.getStartX(), level.getStartY()));
        assertEquals(fixedCount, traps.size());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TahitiGoalPathTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.CollisionSystem;
import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Goal;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.projectile.Starfish;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TahitiGoalPathTest {
    @Test
    void walkingNearGoalMakesItMoveBeforeGuardingStarfishCanKillPlayer() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(path));
        Goal goal = level.createGoal();
        // RangÃ©e du G, aprÃ¨s les cocos des colonnes 19 et 22 de la nouvelle carte.
        // Ce test vÃ©rifie la sÃ©quence objectif/Ã©toile, pas un passage sans saut sous les cocos.
        Player player = new Player(24 * Level.TILE, goal.getY(), GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE,
                GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = new ArrayList<>(level.createTraps(player));
        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        List<GameEvent> events = new ArrayList<>();
        bus.subscribe(events::add);
        CollisionSystem collisions = new CollisionSystem();
        boolean moved = false;
        for (int frame = 0; frame < 80 && events.isEmpty() && !moved; frame++) {
            float step = 1f / 30f;
            player.update(step, false, true, false);
            collisions.move(player, level, step);
            moved = PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal,
                    level.getStartX(), level.getStartY());
            PlayScreen.updateTraps(LevelTheme.TAHITI, level, goal, traps, step);
            collisions.checkContacts(player, traps, bus);
            collisions.checkFall(player, bus);
            collisions.checkContacts(player, List.of(goal), bus);
        }
        assertTrue(moved, "G doit rejoindre P avant le premier Ã©vÃ©nement : " + events
                + ", joueur x=" + player.getX() + ", G x=" + goal.getX());
        assertEquals(level.getStartX(), goal.getX());
    }

    @Test
    void guardingStarfishWaitsUntilGoalMoves() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(path));
        Goal goal = level.createGoal();
        Player player = new Player(21 * Level.TILE, goal.getY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> traps = new ArrayList<>(level.createTraps(player));
        Starfish guard = traps.stream().filter(Starfish.class::isInstance).map(Starfish.class::cast)
                .filter(t -> t.getY() == goal.getY()).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        PlayScreen.updateTraps(LevelTheme.TAHITI, level, goal, traps, 1f / 30f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, guard.getState());
        player.teleport(goal.getX() - 2 * Level.TILE, goal.getY());
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal,
                level.getStartX(), level.getStartY()));
        PlayScreen.updateTraps(LevelTheme.TAHITI, level, goal, traps, 1f / 30f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.TRIGGERED, guard.getState());
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TahitiGoalTransitionTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameEvent;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Goal;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TahitiGoalTransitionTest {
    @Test
    void goalMovesToSpawnOnceAtOneTileAndPlayerStaysPut() {
        Goal goal = new Goal(320f, 32f);
        Player player = new Player(320f - GameConfig.TILE - GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE - 1f,
                32f, 32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, 32f, 32f));
        assertEquals(320f, goal.getX());

        player.teleport(320f - GameConfig.TILE - GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE, 32f);
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, 32f, 32f));
        assertEquals(32f, goal.getX());
        assertEquals(32f, goal.getY());
        assertEquals(320f - GameConfig.TILE - GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE, player.getX());
        assertEquals(32f, player.getY());
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, 32f, 32f));

        EventBus bus = new EventBus();
        List<GameEvent> events = new ArrayList<>();
        bus.subscribe(events::add);
        player.teleport(32f, 32f);
        goal.onPlayerContact(player, bus);
        assertEquals(List.of(GameEvent.GOAL_REACHED), events);
    }

    @Test
    void goalRemainsInOtherCountries() {
        Goal goal = new Goal(320f, 32f);
        Player player = new Player(320f - GameConfig.TILE - GameConfig.PLAYER_SIZE, 32f,
                32f, 32f, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        for (LevelTheme theme : LevelTheme.values()) {
            if (theme != LevelTheme.TAHITI) {
                assertFalse(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(theme, player, goal, 32f, 32f));
                assertEquals(320f, goal.getX());
            }
        }
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TahitiRespawnTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Goal;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TahitiRespawnTest {
    @Test
    void consumedTrapResetsWhenLevelIsLoadedAgain() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        String map = Files.readString(path);
        Level first = LevelLoader.fromString(map);
        Player player = new Player(first.getStartX(), first.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> fixedTraps = first.createTraps(player);
        List<Trap> traps = new java.util.ArrayList<>(fixedTraps);
        assertEquals(first.createTraps(player).size(), traps.size());
        Trap fixed = traps.stream().filter(t -> t.getX() == 18 * Level.TILE && t.getY() == 10 * Level.TILE)
                .findFirst().orElseThrow();
        float originalY = fixed.getY();
        // Un piÃ¨ge consommÃ© reste consommÃ© pendant cette tentative.
        Trap consumed = TrapFactory.create('f', player.getX(), player.getY(), player);
        traps.add(consumed);
        consumed.onPlayerContact(player, new EventBus());
        assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, consumed.getState());
        consumed.update(1f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, consumed.getState());
        fixed.update(0.1f);

        int trapsBeforeGoalMoves = traps.size();
        Goal goal = first.createGoal();
        player.teleport(goal.getX() - 2 * Level.TILE, goal.getY());
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.moveTahitiGoalNearPlayer(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal,
                first.getStartX(), first.getStartY()));
        assertEquals(trapsBeforeGoalMoves, traps.size());

        // Comme loadLevel(), recharge la carte et crÃ©e un nouveau joueur et de nouveaux piÃ¨ges.
        Level next = LevelLoader.fromString(map);
        Player respawned = new Player(next.getStartX(), next.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> freshFixedTraps = next.createTraps(respawned);
        List<Trap> fresh = freshFixedTraps;
        assertEquals(fixedTraps.size(), fresh.size());
        Trap fixedAgain = fresh.stream().filter(t -> t.getX() == 18 * Level.TILE && t.getY() == 10 * Level.TILE)
                .findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertNotSame(fixed, fixedAgain);
        assertEquals(originalY, fixedAgain.getY());
        assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, fixedAgain.getState());
        assertNotSame(consumed, fresh.stream().filter(t -> t.getClass() == consumed.getClass()).findFirst().orElseThrow());
        assertTrue(fresh.stream().noneMatch(t -> t.getState() == Trap.State.DONE));
    }
}


========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TahitiReturnPhaseTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelLoader;
import com.teikinui.game.level.LevelTheme;
import com.teikinui.game.level.TahitiTrapPlacement;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Goal;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Level;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.falling.CocoFall;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.projectile.Starfish;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class TahitiReturnPhaseTest {
    @Test void returnWaveAppearsOnlyWhenGoalMovesAndReappearsAfterRespawn() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        String map = Files.readString(path);
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(map);
        Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        var plan = TahitiTrapPlacement.plan(level, level.createTraps(player), new Random(42));
        List<Trap> traps = TahitiTrapPlacement.instantiate(level, player, level.createTraps(player), plan);
        int before = traps.size();
        List<Trap> prepared = TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(level, player);
        Goal goal = level.createGoal();
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, level, traps, prepared));
        assertEquals(before, traps.size());
        player.teleport(goal.getX() - 2 * Level.TILE, goal.getY());
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, level, traps, prepared));
        assertEquals(before + 3, traps.size());
        assertEquals(2, traps.subList(before, traps.size()).stream().filter(CocoFall.class::isInstance).count());
        assertEquals(1, traps.subList(before, traps.size()).stream().filter(Starfish.class::isInstance).count());
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, level, traps, prepared));
        assertEquals(before + 3, traps.size());

        Level retry = LevelLoader.fromString(map);
        Player respawn = new Player(retry.getStartX(), retry.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> fresh = TahitiTrapPlacement.instantiate(retry, respawn, retry.createTraps(respawn), plan);
        assertEquals(before, fresh.size());
        Goal resetGoal = retry.createGoal();
        List<Trap> freshPrepared = TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(retry, respawn);
        respawn.teleport(resetGoal.getX() - 2 * Level.TILE, resetGoal.getY());
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, respawn, resetGoal, retry, fresh, freshPrepared));
        assertEquals(before + 3, fresh.size());
        for (int i = 0; i < fresh.size(); i++) {
            assertEquals(traps.get(i).getClass(), fresh.get(i).getClass());
            assertEquals(traps.get(i).getX(), fresh.get(i).getX());
            assertEquals(traps.get(i).getY(), fresh.get(i).getY());
            assertNotSame(traps.get(i), fresh.get(i));
        }
    }

    @Test void consumedMiddleCoconutIsRearmedAtSamePositionOnRespawn() throws Exception {
        Path path = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "tahiti.txt");
        String map = Files.readString(path);
        Level first = LevelLoader.fromString(map);
        Player player = new Player(first.getStartX(), first.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> active = first.createTraps(player);
        List<Trap> wave = TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(first, player);
        Goal goal = first.createGoal();
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, first, active, wave));
        assertTrue(active.stream().noneMatch(wave::contains));
        PlayScreen.updateTraps(LevelTheme.TAHITI, first, goal, active, 0.25f);
        assertTrue(wave.stream().allMatch(t -> t.getState() == Trap.State.WAITING));
        player.teleport(goal.getX() - 2 * Level.TILE, goal.getY());
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, player, goal, first, active, wave));
        Trap middle = wave.get(1);
        float middleStartY = middle.getY();
        player.teleport(middle.getX(), middle.getY() - Level.TILE);
        middle.update(0.25f);
        middle.onPlayerContact(player, new com.teikinui.game.model.EventBus());
        assertEquals(Trap.State.DONE, middle.getState());

        // MÃªmes Ã©tapes que loadLevel() aprÃ¨s la mort : carte et objets neufs, vague en rÃ©serve.
        Level retry = LevelLoader.fromString(map);
        retry.setAttempt(1);
        Player respawn = new Player(retry.getStartX(), retry.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);
        List<Trap> fresh = retry.createTraps(respawn);
        List<Trap> freshWave = TahitiTrapPlacement.returnWave(retry, respawn);
        assertEquals(3, freshWave.size());
        assertTrue(fresh.stream().noneMatch(freshWave::contains));
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            assertNotSame(wave.get(i), freshWave.get(i));
            assertEquals(wave.get(i).getClass(), freshWave.get(i).getClass());
            assertEquals(i == 1 ? 12 * Level.TILE : wave.get(i).getX(), freshWave.get(i).getX());
            assertEquals(i == 1 ? middleStartY : wave.get(i).getY(), freshWave.get(i).getY());
            assertEquals(Trap.State.WAITING, freshWave.get(i).getState());
        }
        Goal reset = retry.createGoal();
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, respawn, reset, retry, fresh, freshWave));
        assertTrue(fresh.stream().noneMatch(freshWave::contains));
        assertTrue(freshWave.stream().allMatch(t -> t.getState() == Trap.State.WAITING));
        respawn.teleport(reset.getX() - 2 * Level.TILE, reset.getY());
        assertTrue(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, respawn, reset, retry, fresh, freshWave));
        assertTrue(fresh.containsAll(freshWave));
        assertEquals(3, fresh.stream().filter(freshWave::contains).count());
        assertFalse(PlayScreen.activateTahitiReturnWave(LevelTheme.TAHITI, respawn, reset, retry, fresh, freshWave));
        assertEquals(3, fresh.stream().filter(freshWave::contains).count());
        respawn.teleport(freshWave.get(1).getX(), freshWave.get(1).getY() - Level.TILE);
        PlayScreen.updateTraps(LevelTheme.TAHITI, retry, reset, fresh, 0f);
        assertEquals(Trap.State.TRIGGERED, freshWave.get(1).getState());
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class TahitiSandDrawingTest {
    @Test
    void fixedAndExtraSandBothDrawInsideTheirGroundTile() throws Exception {
        Path map = Path.of("assets/tahiti.txt");
        if (!Files.exists(map)) map = Path.of("..").resolve(map);
        Level level = LevelLoader.fromString(Files.readString(map));
        Player player = new Player(level.getStartX(), level.getStartY(), 32, 32, GameConfig.RUN_SPEED);

        Trap extra = TrapFactory.create('q', 15 * Level.TILE, Level.TILE, player);
        assertEquals(0f, PlayScreen.trapDrawY(level, extra));
        assertEquals(Level.TILE, extra.getY(), "procedural contact stays at foot level");

========== C:\Users\julie\2DGame\julien2\core\src\test\java\com\teikinui\game\view\TrapSpriteDrawingTest.java ==========

package com.teikinui.game.view;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Player;
import com.teikinui.game.model.Hitbox;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.Trap;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.TrapType;
import com.teikinui.game.model.traps.factory.TrapFactory;

class TrapSpriteDrawingTest {
    private final Player player = new Player(32, 32, 32, 32, 100);

    private Trap create(TrapType type) {
        return TrapFactory.create(type.getLetter(), 64, 64, player);
    }

    @Test
    void supportsAllAvailableTrapSpritesAndPureShapeExceptions() {
        for (TrapType type : TrapType.values()) {
            Trap trap = create(type);
            boolean special = type == TrapType.FAKE_CHILI || type == TrapType.HIDDEN_SLAB
                    || type == TrapType.SKY_FALL || type == TrapType.TILT_SLAB
                    || type == TrapType.INVISIBLE_BLOCK;
            assertEquals(!special, PlayScreen.usesTrapSprite(trap, true), type.name());
            assertFalse(PlayScreen.usesTrapSprite(trap, false), type.name());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void spritesKeepBackdropsButDoNotDrawFallbackOverThem() {
        for (TrapType type : TrapType.values()) {
            Trap trap = create(type);
            boolean backdrop = type == TrapType.MONKEY || type == TrapType.SIMIT_VENDOR
                    || type == TrapType.CROISSANT;
            if (PlayScreen.usesTrapSprite(trap, true)) {
                assertEquals(backdrop, PlayScreen.drawsTrapShape(trap, true), type.name());
            } else {
                assertTrue(PlayScreen.drawsTrapShape(trap, true), type.name());
            }
            assertTrue(PlayScreen.drawsTrapShape(trap, false), type.name());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void floorImitatorsCopyTheOpaqueFloorAndKeepFullContactBounds() throws Exception {
        for (TrapType type : new TrapType[] {TrapType.FAKE_SLAB, TrapType.CRUMBLE_BLOCK,
                TrapType.FAKE_FLOOR}) {
            Trap trap = create(type);
            assertTrue(PlayScreen.copiesFloor(trap));
            assertEquals(trap.getWidth(), trap.getHitbox().getWidth(), 0.001f);
            assertEquals(trap.getHeight(), trap.getHitbox().getHeight(), 0.001f);
        }
        for (String country : new String[] {"tahiti", "chine", "tunisie", "vietnam",
                "srilanka", "turquie", "france", "laponie"}) {
            Path path = Path.of("assets/sprites/floor_" + country + ".png");
            if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..").resolve(path);
            BufferedImage image = ImageIO.read(path.toFile());
            assertEquals(32, image.getWidth());
            assertEquals(32, image.getHeight());
            for (int y = 0; y < 32; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 32; x++)
                assertEquals(255, image.getRGB(x, y) >>> 24, country + " (" + x + "," + y + ")");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void trapContactBoundsMatchTheOpaquePixelsOfTheirPngs() throws Exception {
        for (TrapType type : TrapType.values()) {
            Path path = Path.of("assets/sprites", type.getSpriteName() + ".png");
            if (!Files.exists(path)) path = Path.of("..", "assets", "sprites", type.getSpriteName() + ".png");
            if (!Files.exists(path)) continue;

            BufferedImage image = ImageIO.read(path.toFile());
            int left = image.getWidth();
            int top = image.getHeight();
            int right = -1;
            int bottom = -1;
            for (int y = 0; y < image.getHeight(); y++) {
                for (int x = 0; x < image.getWidth(); x++) {
                    if (((image.getRGB(x, y) >>> 24) & 255) == 0) continue;
                    left = Math.min(left, x);
                    top = Math.min(top, y);
                    right = Math.max(right, x + 1);
                    bottom = Math.max(bottom, y + 1);
                }
            }

            Trap trap = create(type);
            Hitbox hitbox = trap.getHitbox();
            if (right < 0) {
                assertEquals(trap.getX(), hitbox.getX(), type.name());
                assertEquals(trap.getY(), hitbox.getY(), type.name());
                assertEquals(trap.getWidth(), hitbox.getWidth(), type.name());
                assertEquals(trap.getHeight(), hitbox.getHeight(), type.name());
                continue;
            }

            float expectedX = trap.getX() + trap.getWidth() * left / image.getWidth();
            float expectedY = trap.getY() + trap.getHeight() * (image.getHeight() - bottom) / image.getHeight();
            float expectedWidth = trap.getWidth() * (right - left) / image.getWidth();
            float expectedHeight = trap.getHeight() * (bottom - top) / image.getHeight();
            assertEquals(expectedX, hitbox.getX(), 0.001f, type.name());
            assertEquals(expectedY, hitbox.getY(), 0.001f, type.name());
            assertEquals(expectedWidth, hitbox.getWidth(), 0.001f, type.name());
            assertEquals(expectedHeight, hitbox.getHeight(), 0.001f, type.name());
        }
    }
}

package com.teikinui.game.lwjgl3;

import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3Application;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration;
import com.teikinui.game.TripTrapGame;
import com.teikinui.game.model.GameConfig;

/** Lanceur de la version ordinateur (Windows, Linux, macOS). */
public class Lwjgl3Launcher {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration config = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
        config.setTitle("Trip Trap");
        config.setWindowedMode(GameConfig.VIEW_WIDTH, GameConfig.VIEW_HEIGHT);
        config.useVsync(true);
        config.setForegroundFPS(60);
        new Lwjgl3Application(new TripTrapGame(), config);
    }
}
